### [1] [Architecture-dependent Effects of Trade-offs on Evolutionary Navigability and Epistasis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02961)
*Nandita Chaturvedi*

Main category: q-bio.PE

TL;DR: 本文研究可调多层基因型-表型图谱上进化过程的动态特性，发现景观的粗糙度与可导航性之间存在复杂的架构依赖关系，权衡适应度在特定架构下能提高可导航性，且高适应度种群的微进化可产生类似短时交替选择的等位基因频率协方差信号。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 基因型-表型图谱的结构在决定种群如何在适应度景观中移动方面起着核心作用，但目前对其如何与环境约束及适应度权衡相互作用所知甚少。作者试图弥合这一认知缺口。

Method: 构建可调的多层基因型-表型图谱框架，其中二进制基因型依次通过前馈层转换后再由表型层面的适应度函数评估。通过显式建模表型到适应度的变换，比较了涌现权衡适应度与无权衡对照组；通过调节基因型长度和层数考察不同架构复杂度下的图谱特性。

Result: 发现：粗糙度与可导航性是架构依赖的复杂关系；宽浅图谱即使局部最大值少也可导航性低，而宽深图谱在平均粗糙度处可导航性仍高；权衡在宽浅图谱中提高可导航性但在其他架构中作用减弱；权衡对上位性的影响依赖进化历史，而背景上位性程度由图谱架构决定；高适应度种群的等位基因频率变化可产生类似短时交替选择的时间协方差。

Conclusion: 图谱架构是决定进化景观可搜索性的关键因素，权衡效应受架构调节且依赖进化历史；内部图谱结构本身即可产生看似环境驱动的选择信号，为解释微进化观测数据提供了新的机制视角。

Abstract: The structure of the genotype-phenotype map plays a central role in determining how populations move through fitness landscapes, yet less is known about how this structure interacts with environmental constraints and fitness trade-offs. Here, we study evolution on a tunable multilayer genotype-phenotype map in which binary genotypes are transformed through successive feed-forward layers before being evaluated by phenotype-level fitness functions. We explicitly model the phenotype to fitness transformation in order to compare an emergent trade-off fitness with a no-trade-off control. We explore different map architecture as models of more and less complex genotype-phenotype relationships by tuning the genotype length and number of layers. We find that ruggedness and navigability have a complex, architecture-dependent relationship. Landscapes with few local maxima can still have low navigability for wide and shallow maps, and navigability is high even at mean values of ruggedness for wide and deep maps. Trade-offs increase navigability in wide and shallow maps, while their effect is muted in other architectures. The effect of trade-offs on epistasis depends on evolutionary history. While trade-offs change which mutational neighborhoods are sampled by evolution, the background degree of epistatic interactions is set by map architecture. Finally, we use our framework to investigate micro-evolution of high-fitness populations and show that temporal covariance in allele-frequency changes can arise from the internal structure of a rugged genotype-phenotype map, producing signatures that resemble short-term alternating selection.

</details>

### [2] [Valley polarization dependence of candidate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in monolayer graphene](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02976)
*Saswata Sahu,Amarendra Pr. Indra,Moumita Indra*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文研究了单层石墨烯中偶分母分数量子霍尔（EDFQH）态，聚焦于实验观测到的ν=1/2和ν=1/4填充因子。基于自旋和谷自由度带来的近似SU(4)对称性，作者利用Chern-Simons复合费米子框架构建了不同自旋、谷及混合极化构型的变分波函数，并结合球形几何下的库仑相互作用进行数值能量比较，识别出能量最优的多组分关联量子霍尔液态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 偶分母分数量子霍尔态在石墨烯中的实验观测尚缺乏完善的理论解释。由于石墨烯独特的自旋与谷自由度赋予系统近似SU(4)对称性，可能稳定多种多组分分数量子霍尔态，因此需要系统研究不同极化构型的竞争关系，以理解实验观测到的ν=1/2和ν=1/4态的物理本质。

Method: 采用Chern-Simons复合费米子框架处理SU(4)对称系统，构建对应于自旋极化、谷极化及混合极化等不同构型的变分试探波函数；然后在球形几何中利用库仑相互作用对这些多组分态的基态能量进行数值评估，通过跨极化扇区的能量对比确定能量最优构型。

Result: 变分能量比较表明，在ν=1/2和ν=1/4处能量最优的候选态对应多组分关联量子霍尔液体。在所考虑的限制性试探态族内，这些态不同于简单的复合费米子费米海描述。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了谷极化和SU(4)对称性在稳定石墨烯中偶分母分数量子霍尔态中的关键作用，为理解这些奇异量子态的形成机制提供了新的理论见解。

Abstract: We investigate even-denominator fractional quantum Hall (EDFQH) states in monolayer graphene, focusing on the experimentally observed filling fractions $ν= 1/2$ and $ν= 1/4$. Owing to the approximate SU(4) symmetry arising from spin and valley degrees of freedom, graphene hosts a rich variety of multi-component fractional quantum Hall states with different polarization configurations. Using the Chern-Simons composite fermion framework for SU(4) systems, we construct candidate variational wave functions corresponding to distinct spin, valley, and mixed polarization states. The ground-state energies of these states are evaluated numerically using the Coulomb interaction in spherical geometry. By comparing energies across different polarization sectors, we identify the energetically favored configurations at $ν= 1/2$ and $ν= 1/4$. Our variational energy comparison suggests that the energetically favored candidate states correspond to multi-component correlated quantum Hall liquids. Within the restricted family of trial states considered here, these states differ from a simple composite-fermion Fermi-sea description. These findings provide insight into the role of valley polarization and SU(4) symmetry in stabilizing even-denominator fractional quantum Hall states in graphene.

</details>


### [3] [Orbital current rectifier and linear magnon Edelstein effect in $p$-wave antialtermagnets](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03075)
*Kristian Mæland,Björn Trauzettel*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文揭示了p波磁性材料通过磁振子的轨道磁矩产生线性热Edelstein效应实现高效磁化，并可通过纯偶次非线性响应产生完全非互易的轨道电流，从而建立了磁子学与轨道电子学的联系。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索p波磁性材料中磁振子（自旋波量子）的输运性质，特别是其轨道磁矩如何带来新颖的输运现象，并连接磁子学与轨道电子学两个领域。

Method: 利用对称性分析和解析论证建立一般性结论，并在具有最少子格数的共面基态（以CeNiAsO为灵感）上进行显式演示，结论在有无自旋-轨道耦合的情况下均成立。

Result: 1) p波磁体通过磁振子轨道磁矩产生线性热Edelstein效应，高效实现磁化；2) 可通过纯偶次非线性响应产生完全非互易的轨道电流；3) 发现奇宇称p波磁体中共面基态的磁振子具有零自旋磁矩，而携带共线面外轨道磁矩，实现轨道版本的反交变磁性。

Conclusion: p波磁性材料因其磁振子独特的轨道磁矩性质，有望成为轨道电流整流器件的有力候选者，并为磁子学与轨道电子学的交叉研究提供新平台。

Abstract: We show that $p$-wave magnets efficiently generate magnetization via a linear thermal Edelstein effect arising from the orbital magnetic moments of their magnons. Furthermore, they can generate perfectly nonreciprocal orbital currents through a purely even-order nonlinear response. This makes them promising candidates for orbital-current rectification. Because these transport phenomena originate from the orbital magnetic moment of magnons, they connect magnonics and orbitronics. More generally, we find that odd-parity-wave magnets with coplanar ground states host magnons with zero spin magnetic moment. Instead the magnons carry a collinear, out-of-plane orbital magnetic moment, realizing an orbital version of antialtermagnetism. We establish these general results using symmetry arguments and demonstrate them explicitly for a coplanar ground state with the minimal number of sublattices, inspired by the ground state of CeNiAsO. Our conclusions hold both in absence and presence of spin-orbit coupling.

</details>


### [4] [Antiferromagnetism-altered plasmon dynamics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03138)
*Yifan Su,Suheng Xu,Rocco A. Vitalone,Ziyu Liu,Emil Viñas Boström,Na Wu,Chun-Ying Huang,Zhuquan Zhang,Jaehoon Jung,Daniel G. Chica,Vinícius da Silveira Lan Avelar,Yiping Wang,Takashi Taniguchi,Kenji Watanabe,James C. Hone,Xiaoyang Zhu,Cory R. Dean,Xavier Roy,Angel Rubio,D. N. Basov*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本研究首次在二维材料（石墨烯/NiPS$_3$界面）上构建了等离激元-磁振子杂化平台，利用散射型扫描近场光学显微镜（s-SNOM）结合太赫兹辐射，在NiPS$_3$的反铁磁转变过程中观测到等离激元-极化激元色散的明显重整化，为二维材料中磁振子-等离激元相互耦合提供了首个实验证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 三维体系中等离激元与磁振子因能量尺度严重失配而耦合被抑制，而二维等离激元具有无能隙色散，可在宽光谱范围内与磁振子重叠。尽管理论预测众多，磁振子-等离激元相互作用的实验观测长期缺失，作者旨在通过二维材料平台首次实现该相互作用的直接观测。

Method: 构建基于石墨烯/NiPS$_3$界面的杂化等离激元-磁振子平台；使用散射型扫描近场光学显微镜（s-SNOM）结合太赫兹辐射成像传播的等离激元波包；追踪波包动力学随NiPS$_3$反铁磁转变的变化；辅以互补的拉曼散射和纳米太赫兹光谱测量，分析谱权重再分布和介电屏蔽变化。

Result: 在NiPS$_3$反铁磁序出现的同时，观测到等离激元-极化激元色散出现明显的重整化。拉曼散射和纳米太赫兹光谱揭示了可能与多磁振子连续态相关的谱权重再分布和介电屏蔽变化，该机制可解释上述色散重整化现象。

Conclusion: 该研究为等离激元与反铁磁序之间的耦合提供了坚实的实验证据，标志着二维材料中杂化磁振子-等离激元相互作用的潜在平台成为现实，为相干自旋-等离激元器件和可调谐太赫兹自旋电子组件开辟了新途径。

Abstract: The interaction between plasmons and magnons is a long-sought phenomenon with implications for fundamental physics and spintronics applications. In three-dimensional systems, this coupling is suppressed by the large mismatch in energy scales, but two-dimensional (2D) plasmons with gapless dispersion can overlap with magnons over a broad spectral range. Despite numerous theoretical predictions, experimental observation of magnon-plasmon interaction has remained elusive. In this work, we study a first-of-its-kind hybrid plasmon-magnon platform based on 2D materials. By deploying scattering-type scanning near-field optical microscopy (s-SNOM) with terahertz radiation, we image propagating plasmon wavepackets at a graphene/NiPS$_3$ interface and track their dynamics across the antiferromagnetic transition of NiPS$_3$. We observe a clear renormalization of the plasmon-polariton dispersion concurrent with the onset of antiferromagnetic order. With complementary Raman scattering and nano-terahertz spectroscopy, we unveil spectral weight redistribution and dielectric screening changes, potentially associated with the multi-magnon continuum, as the underlying mechanism. These results provide solid evidence of coupling between plasmon and antiferromagnetic order, marking a cornerstone for a potential platform for hybrid magnon-plasmon interactions in 2D materials, opening avenues for coherent spin-plasmon devices and tunable terahertz spintronic components.

</details>

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索非磁性材料中产生交替磁性的新途径，扩展交替磁性的材料平台和实现机制，不同于传统依赖磁性元素或特定晶体对称性的方案。

Method: 通过摩尔干涉机制，利用晶格失配摩尔方晶格的结构弛豫产生自发扭曲，结合电子相互作用分析关联绝缘态与摩尔带的交替磁分裂（d波和g波）。

Result: 单电子掺杂每个摩尔畴时打开关联绝缘能隙；摩尔带可展现d波或g波交替磁分裂，且d波分裂可被有限转角增强，g波分裂易受应变影响并转变为d波。

Conclusion: 该工作为在无磁性材料体系中设计和调控交替磁性序提供了全新的摩尔工程途径，并揭示了转角与应变对交替磁对称性的可调性。

Abstract: We propose a mechanism for nonmagnetic materials to develop altermagnetic order by moiré interference with nonmagnetic substrate, which is driven by structural relaxation and spontaneous twirls in moiré domain walls of lattice-mismatched moiré square lattices. When doped with one electron per moiré domain, a correlated insulating gap is opened by electron interaction. Depending on the location of the moiré potential minima, the moiré bands can show d-wave or g-wave altermagnetic splitting. The former can be enhanced by a finite twist angle; the latter is sensitive to strains that drive a transition to d-wave.

</details>


### [6] [Resolving the Magnetic Ground-State Controversy in RuO2 through A Flat Magnetic Energy Landscape](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03274)
*Tianxiao Liang,Fanhan Kong,Jijun Zhao,Xue Jiang*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文开发了一个广义的环境依赖自旋-晶格框架，统一了局域Heisenberg交换、巡游Stoner磁性（通过Landau自旋涨落）以及自旋-轨道耦合介导的自旋-晶格相互作用，用于解释RuO2磁基态的争议。该框架通过机器学习从高通量第一性原理数据库参数化，并由大规模蒙特卡洛模拟求解，揭示了RuO2中极其平坦的磁能量景观——非磁态与多种交替磁(altermagnetic)构型近乎简并。研究发现材料扰动（如本征缺陷）主要通过修饰局域Heisenberg交换来选择性确定不同的磁基态，巡游Stoner极化起次要贡献，而自旋-轨道耦合仅控制Néel矢量的取向与稳定性，不决定长程磁序的产生。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 金红石RuO2是交替磁性的重要候选材料，但其磁基态存在高度争议——实验报道既有非磁态也有交替磁序。需要统一的理论框架来解释这些矛盾实验结果，并理解材料扰动如何选择竞争性的磁基态。

Method: 开发了一种广义的环境依赖自旋-晶格(spin-lattice)框架，将三种磁相互作用统一：(1)局域化Heisenberg交换、(2)通过Landau自旋涨落描述的巡游Stoner磁性、(3)自旋-轨道耦合介导的自旋-晶格相互作用。参数由高通量第一性原理数据库经机器学习获得，解算依靠大规模蒙特卡洛模拟。通过引入本征缺陷作为材料扰动的代表来研究磁基态选择机制。

Result: 揭示了RuO2中磁能量景观异常平坦，非磁态与多个交替磁构型近乎简并。材料扰动主要通过修饰局域Heisenberg交换选择不同磁基态，而扰动引发的巡游Stoner极化仅提供必要的次级贡献。自旋-轨道耦合控制Néel矢量取向与稳定性，但不决定长程磁序的出现。

Conclusion: 该框架为RuO2中矛盾的实验观测提供了统一解释，建立了一个通用的微观框架来理解在近平坦磁能量景观系统中材料扰动如何选择竞争磁基态。

Abstract: Rutile RuO2 is a prominent candidate for altermagnetism, yet its magnetic ground state remains highly controversial, with experiments reporting either a nonmagnetic state or altermagnetic order. Here, we develop a generalized environment-dependent spin-lattice framework that unifies localized Heisenberg exchange, itinerant Stoner magnetism via Landau spin fluctuations, and spin-orbit-coupling-mediated spin-lattice interactions. Parameterized from a high-throughput first-principles database using machine-learning and solved by large-scale Monte Carlo simulations, the framework reveals an exceptionally flat magnetic energy landscape in RuO2, where the nonmagnetic state lies nearly degenerate with multiple altermagnetic configurations. We find that material perturbations, exemplified by intrinsic defects, select distinct magnetic ground states primarily by modifying the localized Heisenberg exchange, with perturbation-induced itinerant Stoner polarization provides an essential secondary contribution. Spin-orbit coupling controls the orientation and stability of the Néel vector, but does not determine the emergence of long-range magnetic order. These results provide a unified explanation for the conflicting experimental observations and establish a general microscopic framework for understanding how material perturbations select competing magnetic ground states in systems with nearly flat magnetic energy landscapes.

</details>


### [7] [Symmetry-breaking line defects embedded to a 3D $O(N)$ critical bulk](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03289)
*Shuai Yang,Liang-dong Hu,Yan Chen,W. Zhu*

Main category: cond-mat.str-el

TL;DR: 本文通过模糊球面技术研究嵌在3D O(N) Wilson-Fisher临界体中的一维pinng-field线缺陷，发现N≳3时存在稳定的缺陷共形不动点并支持对称性自发破缺，而N<3时由于畴壁增生导致缺陷固定点不稳定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一维经典系统由于短程相互作用无法自发破缺离散对称性，但理论上预期嵌入临界体的线缺陷可能实现稳定的离散对称性自发破缺。作者旨在通过计算检验这一预期是否成立，并确定缺陷稳定性的与体N参数的依赖关系。

Method: 采用模糊球面（fuzzy sphere）数值技术，研究保持在O(N) Wilson-Fisher临界点附近的3D体中的钉扎场线缺陷；通过计算g-函数展示缺陷共形不动点的重整化群稳定性（无非相关算符、有效自由度少于体固定点）；并通过计算不同N下的缺陷畴壁算符标度维数来判断其相关/无关性。

Result: 提供了缺陷共形不动点存在的有力证据；发现畴壁算符在N≲3时是相关的、N≳3时变为无关的。因此，对于N<3的Wilson-Fisher普适类，一维缺陷耦合临界体无法维持稳定的对称性自发破缺不动点；而对于N≳3的情形，对称性破缺的缺陷固定点则是可能的。

Conclusion: 一维线缺陷能否在临界体中稳定地自发破缺离散对称性取决于O(N)对称群的N值：N≲3时域壁增生阻止了破缺相，N≳3时则可存在稳定的对称性破缺缺陷不动点，这一临界维数/阀值行为为理解缺陷诱导的对称性破缺与维数依赖提供了重要见解。

Abstract: While spontaneous breaking of a discrete symmetry in one-dimensional classical systems with short-range interactions is absent, it is expected that a line defect embedded in a bulk criticality exhibits a stable discrete symmetry spontaneous breaking. Here, we investigate the behavior of a pinning-field line defect immersed in a 3D bulk that remains tuned to the $O(N)$ Wilson-Fisher critical point. Employing the fuzzy sphere technique, we provide convincing evidence of the existence of stable defect conformal fixed points, and we demonstrate their renormalization group stability by showing no relevant operator and less effective degrees of freedom than that at bulk fixed point via $g$-function. Moreover, we investigate the defect domain wall operator for various $N$, and we identify that it becomes irrelevance for $N\gtrsim 3$ but it is relevant for $N<3$.These evidence indicate that a one-dimensional defect coupled to a critical bulk cannot support a stable symmetry spontaneously broken defect fixed point due to domain wall proliferation for $N<3$ Wilson-Fisher universality, while in the case of $N \gtrsim 3$ a symmetry broken defect is possible.

Abstract: Quantum many-body systems exhibit rich and complex behaviour. Magic has recently emerged as a powerful new diagnostic for probing such systems, complementing other key features such as many-body entanglement. Here, starting from an investigation of the onset of magic within subsystems of a larger many-body quantum system, we show that intricate structures emerge which shed important light on the underlying many-body physics. Focusing on the Kitaev honeycomb model, we first identify the temperature below which local subsystems acquire magic. Remarkably, we find that the optimal magic witnesses active at the onset of magic reveal compact operator spaces that continue to capture the local thermal states throughout their subsequent evolution. For the six-site hexagonal marginal, this connection can be made stronger: the same operator space emerges independently from the symmetries of the local marginal and forms the symmetry-resolved plaquette algebra. When these symmetries are realized exactly, the local state lies entirely within this algebra and the reduced robustness of magic is equal to the full robustness of magic. The same reduced description can also be applied to other local quantum resources, which we illustrate using genuine multipartite entanglement. Furthermore, the operator spaces possess a rich algebraic structure in the form of finite-dimensional Euclidean Jordan algebras, with a natural interpretation in terms of bond and bond-cycle operators. Our general methodology therefore show how the onset of local magic can reveal a compact, physically meaningful operator structure underlying the finite-temperature Kitaev spin liquid, and opens up a new avenue towards reduced descriptions of quantum resources in many-body systems.

Abstract: Phase transitions are typically driven by symmetry-breaking structural distortions that lift electronic degeneracies, yet in some cases, these transitions may be driven by a hidden order without conventional structural signatures. Symmetry-based Landau analysis supports a hidden zone centered magnetic octupole order parameter, whose improper coupling accounts for the weak ferromagnetism and pronounced symmetry-breaking strain. Density functional calculations show that spin-orbit coupling suppresses the competing Jahn-Teller instability, and thereby favors higher rank magnetic multipolar degrees of freedom. These findings establish KFeF$_3$ as a model system in which the competing energy scales between spin-orbit coupling and orbital degeneracy result in the emergence of hidden, multipolar order. We show that the control of such magnetic, multipolar order could, in principle, provide a route to induce altermagnetism.

Abstract: Quantum criticality in one-dimensional quantum systems is characterized by emergent conformal field theories (CFTs), whose central charge counts independent gapless degrees of freedom. Real- and momentum-space entanglement spectra provide complementary microscopic signatures, revealing both the conformal content and momentum-space organization of critical modes.   <summary>Details</summary>

Method: 通过建立几何自同构群阶数的反向不等式（即上界）来证明恰好等于4(q+1)。具体而言，先利用已有的下界结论，再通过分析函数域F_i的几何自同构群的结构限制，证明其阶数不可能超过4(q+1)，从而两个方向的不等式同时成立，得出精确等式。

Conclusion: 该特定类别的F_{q^2}-极值函数域的几何自同构群阶数完全确定，猜想得到证实。这一结果进一步加深了对极值函数域自同构群结构的理解，并为相关领域（如极值曲线的分类）提供了新的闭式结论。

Abstract: Let $q=p^h>7$ be odd, put $m=(q+1)/2$, and suppose that $i=(m-2)/2$ satisfies $\gcd(i,m)=\gcd(i+2,m)=1$. We prove this equality by establishing the reverse inequality.

For each of these primes $\ell$, we find a polynomial of degree $\ell+1$ whose splitting field has Galois group $\operatorname{PSL}_2(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$. These fields satisfy Böge's criterion and the associated central embedding problem has a proper solution with Galois group $\operatorname{SL}_2(\mathbb{F}_\ell)$. One can choose the resulting fields totally real via a quadratic twist. The degree $14$ polynomial is found through a genus one Hurwitz family of degree 14 covers. The degree 20 polynomial is found using Yang's modular equations for the Shimura curve $X_6^*(1)$.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>

Result: 获得了不含N^ε因子的估计|Σ_{n≤N}μ²(n)e(αn)| ≪ (N/q+q)(log 2N)^5，并在Hardy-Littlewood分解的minor arcs上在范围Q≤N^{1/2}内推出了相应的估计，其关于q和Q的依赖性与此前Schlage-Puchta及Tolev的结果一致，但去除了额外因子N^ε。

Abstract: Let $μ$ be the Möbius function and $e(t)=e^{2πit}$. We prove that if $N\ge2$, $α\in\mathbb{R}$, $(a,q)=1$, and $|α-a/q|\le q^{-2}$, then \[\bigg|\sum_{n\le N}μ^2(n)e(αn)\bigg|\ll\left(\frac Nq+q\right)(\log 2N)^5, \] with an absolute implied constant, and we deduce the corresponding estimate on the minor arcs of the Hardy--Littlewood dissection throughout the range $Q\le N^{1/2}$. The estimates of Schlage-Puchta [SP] and of Tolev [T] have the same dependence on $q$ and $Q$ but carry a factor $N^{\varepsilon}$. The proof uses Heath-Brown's square sieve with sieving primes confined to an interval $(P,2P]$, where $P$ may be as small as a multiple of $\log N$; a finite Fejér majorant in place of a truncated Fourier series; and, after completion of the character sums, a count of representations that exploits the restriction on the primes in place of the divisor function.

</details>

Abstract: Let $p$ be a prime. We prove nontrivial bounds on short sums of Dirichlet characters mod $p$ evaluated at a class of polynomials, not necessarily homogeneous, in $n$ variables and of degree $k$. For large $n$, we further achieve nontrivial bounds for sums over boxes with side-lengths as short as $p^{1/(k-1)+\varepsilon}$, which breaks past the Burgess barrier of $p^{1/4+\varepsilon}$ as soon as $k\geq 6$. In the proof, we develop a new variation of the Burgess amplification method that reduces the problem to bounding additive character sums. This is the first case in which a Burgess-type method succeeds in the inhomogeneous setting.

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自守L-函数的次凸性问题是解析数论和自守形式领域的核心问题之一。对于GL₂上对称平方L-函数（对应GL₃自守表示），在重数（level）方面的次凸界此前尚未解决。作者试图在特定局部表示类S的假设下，首次证明水平方向的次凸界，从而填补这一空白。

Result: 证明了当局部表示π_p属于合适的表示类S时，L(1/2, Sym²π) ≪ q(Sym²π)^{1/4-1/168+o(1)}，其中隐含常数对π_∞的谱参数呈多项式依赖。这是GL₃(Q)自守表示L-函数在水平方向次凸界的第一个结果。

Conclusion: 该结果通过delta符号法与代数几何工具（Katz超几何和、Deligne-Weil猜想）的结合，首次在水平方向上建立了GL₃自守表示L-函数的次凸界，推进了次凸性问题的研究进展。

Abstract: In this paper, we address the subconvexity problem in level aspect for symmetric square $L$-functions for cuspidal automorphic representation of $\mathrm{GL}_2(\mathbb{Q})$ with a prescribed local ramification at prime $p$. To be more precise, let $π$ be a tempered cuspidal automorphic representation of conductor $q(π)=p^2$ with a non-quadratic central character of conductor $p$. We prove that if the corresponding local representation $π_p$ belongs to a suitable class of representations $\mathcal S$, then
  \[
  L\left(\frac{1}{2},\,\mathrm{Sym}^2π\right)\ll_{\varepsilon, π_\infty} q(\mathrm{Sym}^2π)^{\frac{1}{4}-\frac{1}{168}+o(1)},
  \]
  where implied constant depends polynomially on the spectral parameters of $π_\infty$. This is the first instance of level-aspect subconvex bound for $L$-functions of a $\mathrm{GL}_3(\mathbb Q)$ automorphic representation. Our approach is based on the delta-symbol method. Apart from some standard analytic number theoretic tools, Katz's theory of hypergeometric sums, and Deligne's proof of Weil-conjectures play an important role in the proof.

</details>

### [41] [Uncertainty quantification of fatigue initiation life for powder bed fusion metal additive manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03163)
*Yulin Guo,Veera Sundararaghavan,Boris Kramer*

Main category: stat.AP

TL;DR: 本文提出一个框架，将微结构和缺陷不确定性直接传播到特定零件的疲劳起始寿命分布中，为激光粉末床熔融增材制造关键部件提供解析的概率密度函数，并应用于316L不锈钢部件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 关键部件认证需要带量化不确定性的疲劳寿命预测，而传统确定性预测过于保守，无法为增材制造部件的合格认定和部署提供充分支持。

Method: 通过EBSD和micro-CT获取微结构与缺陷特征，输入三个基于物理的仿真，得到影响疲劳的三个量：弹性应变能释放率、裂纹路径表面能和疲劳指示参数。考虑这些量之间的高度相关性（源于共享底层微结构），推导出疲劳起始寿命的闭式概率密度函数。

Result: 应用于EOS M290激光粉末床熔融机制备的316L不锈钢部件，发现晶粒尺寸和孔隙分布都影响疲劳起始寿命分布。固定总孔隙体积分数时，更大晶粒导致疲劳起始寿命略有降低；多而小的孔隙比等体积的少而大的孔隙对疲劳寿命更有利。

Conclusion: 该框架提供了解析的疲劳寿命分布而非保守的确定性预测，实现了更明智的合格认证与部署决策，可有效支持增材制造关键部件的可靠性评估。

Abstract: Predicting fatigue life with quantified uncertainties is essential for the qualification of critical components produced by laser-based powder bed fusion additive manufacturing. We present a framework that propagates microstructure and defect uncertainties directly to a fatigue initiation life distribution for a specific part. In particular, microstructure and defect characterizations are obtained from electron backscatter diffraction and micro-computed tomography scan data, which in turn inform three physics-based simulations yielding the fatigue-affecting quantities: the elastic energy release rate, the surface energy along the crack path, and the fatigue indicator parameter. Accounting for the uncertainties in these quantities and the high correlations among them due to the shared underlying microstructure, we derive a closed-form probability density function for the fatigue initiation life. This provides an analytical distribution instead of conservative deterministic predictions and enables more informed decision making for the qualification and deployment of additively manufactured components. Applying the framework to 316L stainless steel parts produced by an EOS M290 laser powder bed fusion machine, we find that both grain sizes and void distributions influence the fatigue initiation life distribution. Specifically, for a fixed total void volume fraction, larger grain sizes cause a marginal reduction in fatigue initiation life, and a population of many small voids is more favorable for fatigue life than fewer, larger voids of equivalent total volume.

</details>

Method: 使用1991至2021年覆盖县级单位的面板数据集，并采用适用于面板数据的因果推断方法，研究自然灾害损害对不同县级非营利组织结果变量的影响。

Result: 与先前研究发现灾害损害与非营利组织收入或资产之间存在小而正向的关联不同，本研究未发现因果效应的证据。

Conclusion: 在因果框架下，自然灾害损害对县级非营利组织产出没有因果影响，挑战了先前基于相关性研究的结论。

Abstract: When natural disasters strike, individuals, communities, and even entire countries can suffer. Researchers have studied the impacts of disasters on various factors of interest, from mental health, to poverty, to economic activity. However, the impact of disasters on the nonprofit sector is understudied despite the nonprofit sector's perhaps surprising role in local or national economies as well as its role in disaster response and recovery. Thus, we study the effect of natural disaster damage on different county-level nonprofit outcomes using a panel dataset spanning 1991 to 2021 and causal inference methods tailored to panel data. Contrary to prior work, which found small but positive associations between disaster damage and nonprofit revenue or assets, we find no evidence of a causal effect.

</details>

### [43] [Seeing Less Is Not Seeing Safely: Privacy Leakage from Task-Scoped Robot Perception Exports](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03055)
*Yuqiao Xu,Erman Ayday*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出任务功能感知蒸馏(TFPD)——一种任务限定的表示导出框架，将丰富的感知数据保留在本地，并根据任务效用与多重残余推理风险对下游导出进行剖析。在AI2-THOR场景与ProcTHOR复现实验中发现：任务等价并不等同于隐私等价，字段删除或更强抽象并不产生通用隐私排序，强调需要对完整公开表示进行任务特定、多风险评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 家用机器人依赖丰富感知在私宅中运作，即使原始传感器数据保留在本地，导出到下游规划器、云服务、日志或学习管道的结构化表示仍可通过语义、几何、空间结构和任务目标泄露家庭信息。现有隐私保护缺乏对残留推理风险的系统评估，需要一种任务限定且风险感知的表示导出框架。

Method: 提出TFPD框架，依据任务效用、直接暴露以及多种残留推理风险对下游导出进行画像。使用120个AI2-THOR场景，采用场景不相交的训练/验证/测试划分、冻结攻击者选择与表示感知的留出攻击，评估导航、碰撞检查和目标对象执行三项任务。通过ProcTHOR进行复现验证。

Result: 三种导航导出实现了相同的成功率(1.000)与平均路径比(0.898)，但表示级别可链接性范围从0.532到0.970。将显式目标标签替换为目标区域，使目标类别macro-F1从1.000降至0.077且保持成功率0.995；几何粗化使对象类别macro-F1从0.704降至0.556但产生可测的碰撞效用代价。ProcTHOR复现保留任务等价/隐私不等价的发现，但改变了归一化与拓扑导出的相对排序。

Conclusion: 任务等价性并不蕴含隐私等价性，字段删除或更强的抽象都不会产生通用的隐私排序。结论提出了任务特定、多风险评估完整公开表示的必要性，而非依赖单一抽象层次或字段移除的简化策略。

Abstract: Domestic robots rely on rich perception to operate in private homes, but privacy risk persists even when raw sensor data remain local. Structured representations exported to downstream planners, cloud services, logs, or learning pipelines can still reveal household information through semantics, geometry, spatial structure, and task targets. We introduce Task-Functional Perception Distillation (TFPD), a task-scoped representation-export framework that keeps rich perception local and profiles downstream exports according to task utility, direct exposure, and multiple residual inference risks. Using 120 AI2-THOR scenes with scene-disjoint train/validation/test splits, frozen attacker selection, and representation-aware held-out attacks, we evaluate navigation, collision checking, and object-goal execution. Three navigation exports achieve identical success (1.000) and mean path ratio (0.898), yet representation-level linkability ranges from 0.532 to 0.970. Replacing an explicit target label with a target region reduces target-category macro-F1 from 1.000 to 0.077 while preserving success at 0.995, while geometric coarsening reduces object-category macro-F1 from 0.704 to 0.556 at a measurable collision-utility cost. A ProcTHOR replication preserves the navigation task-equivalence/privacy-inequivalence finding while changing the relative ordering of normalized and topological exports. These results show that neither field removal nor stronger abstraction induces a universal privacy ordering and motivate task-specific, multi-risk evaluation of the complete public representation.

</details>


### [44] [GPU-Accelerated Astrodynamics World Models for Spacecraft Rendezvous and Proximity Operations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03067)

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文首次将世界模型（World Models）范式应用于航天器交会对接与近距操作任务。作者构建了一个基于JAX的、支持GPU并行仿真的国际空间站（ISS）对接环境，并提出"Out-of-this-World-Model"——一个基于Transformer、使用流匹配（flow matching）进行单步预测的世界模型，可对相对运动学和相机图像进行潜在状态编码与预测。在胶囊自主对接ISS（含禁飞区约束）的任务中，该方法在样本效率、任务性能（对接成功率53% vs 29%）、分布外泛化（40% vs 17%）以及异常物体检测（98%分类精度）上均优于DreamerV3风格的基线及强化学习基线，并在保持更少参数量和超参数的同时实现了这些提升。代码与模型均已开源。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 世界模型在机器人和游戏环境中已展现强大能力，但在太空领域尚未得到应用。航天器交会对接与近距操作（RPO）涉及复杂且不确定的动力学、存在禁飞区等安全约束，且传统强化学习方法样本效率低、泛化能力有限。作者希望通过引入世界模型范式，实现对太空任务中随机动力学与逐时间步不确定性的建模，从而提升任务性能与安全性。

Method: 本文方法包含三个核心组成部分：（1）构建开源的JAX-based ISS对接仿真环境，支持GPU并行模拟航天器轨道与姿态动力学，以高效生成世界模型训练所需的大规模状态-动作转移数据；（2）提出Out-of-this-World-Model，一种基于Transformer的世界模型架构，将相对运动学状态与体装相机图像编码为潜在表示，并在推力与扭矩指令下利用单步流匹配（flow matching）预测潜在状态的演化，从而输出未来观测的分布并刻画随机动力学与逐时间步不确定性；（3）将该模型嵌入到胶囊飞行器自主对接ISS的任务框架中，在禁飞区约束下进行规划与决策，并与DreamerV3风格的后验修正基线及强化学习基线进行对比评估。

Result: 实验结果表明：在ISS对接任务中，世界模型方法在对接成功率上显著优于强化学习基线（53% vs 29%，覆盖多个对接端口）；在分布外泛化方面，模型在未见过的端口的成功率超过基线的两倍（40% vs 17%）；在接近过程中对异常物体的分类检测精度达到98%；同时相比DreamerV3风格的基线具有更少的可训练参数量和超参数，展现出更好的样本效率。

Conclusion: 本文证明了世界模型范式可以有效迁移到航天器交会对接与近距操作领域，不仅大幅提升了任务性能、样本效率和分布外泛化能力，还具备良好的不确定性量化与异常检测能力。作者开源了仿真环境和模型架构，为该领域后续研究提供了基础平台，有望推动数据驱动的自主航天操作走向实际应用。

Abstract: World models are an emerging paradigm in representation learning in which an agent jointly learns state-action dynamics and observation models from offline trajectory data, enabling multi-step planning and trajectory prediction with uncertainty estimates. They have shown strong results in robotics and game environments, but, to the best of our knowledge, have not previously been applied to the space domain. This paper introduces a world model-based approach to cooperative and non-cooperative spacecraft rendezvous and proximity operations. First, we introduce an open-source, JAX-based International Space Station (ISS) docking environment supporting parallel GPU simulation of spacecraft orbit and attitude dynamics, generating the thousands of state-action transitions that world model training requires. Second, we introduce Out-of-this-World-Model, a transformer-based world model that encodes relative kinematic states and body-fixed camera imagery into a latent state and predicts its evolution under commanded thrusts and torques using one-step flow matching. It produces a distribution over future observations, capturing stochastic dynamics and per-timestep uncertainty, and outperforms DreamerV3-style posterior-correction baselines with fewer trainable parameters and hyperparameters. Third, we apply the approach to a capsule autonomously docking with the ISS under keep-out-zone constraints, demonstrating improved sample efficiency and task performance over reinforcement learning baselines (53% versus 29% docking success across ports), better out-of-distribution generalization (on held-out ports the world model more than doubles baseline success, 40% versus 17%), and detection of anomalous objects encountered during approach with 98% classification accuracy. We open-source the simulation environment and model architecture to enable further study of this paradigm.

</details>

*Yue Yang,Diego Romeres,Chiori Hori,Gedas Bertasius,Daniel Szafir,Siddarth Jain*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Evidence-Gated Regularization (EGR)，一种模态无关的训练目标，用于解决VLA策略中的"模态纠缠"（modality entanglement）问题——即模型在异构且同质的机器人演示上训练时学习到虚假的跨传感器相关性，导致对遮挡和干扰物的鲁棒性差。EGR通过逐帧逐传感器推导任务相关性信号，对低证据传感器施加不变性约束，对高证据传感器施加单传感器充分性约束，零推理开销。在BEHAVIOR-1K基准和两个真实机器人平台（双臂Kinova+三RGB相机，以及单臂MELFA ASSISTA+视觉/GelSight触觉）上验证，显著提升了各场景下的成功率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLA策略融合多模态感知输入，但机器人演示数据规模有限且模态同质，导致模型容易学习跨传感器的虚假相关性（即模态纠缠），而非与任务真正相关的信号。这在实际场景中表现为对无信息传感器损坏的过度敏感，以及在仅剩单一信息传感器时任务性能严重退化。现有方法缺乏对这种多模态纠缠问题的系统性研究与针对性解决方案。

Method: 提出EGR (Evidence-Gated Regularization)，一种模态无关的训练目标。核心包括：(1) 推导逐帧、逐传感器的任务相关性证据（evidence）信号；(2) 基于证据信号门控两个状态条件一致性目标——对低证据传感器施加不变性约束，对高证据传感器施加单传感器充分性约束；(3) 零推理时间开销，仅在训练时生效。还构建了基于BEHAVIOR-1K的基准，包含快速推理诊断套件和47个基于rollout的技能任务，用于专门评估模态纠缠问题。

Result: 在BEHAVIOR-1K仿真基准上，EGR将全模态成功率从12.5%提升至16.4%（+31%），无信息传感器损坏下从9.4%提升至16.5%（+75%），单传感器回退下从2.8%提升至6.1%（+120%）。在真实机器人上，物理物体干扰物场景下EGR将双臂平台（Kinova+三RGB）成功率从30%提升至85%（+183%），触觉平台（MELFA ASSISTA+视觉/GelSight）从55%提升至70%（+27%）。

Conclusion: EGR有效缓解了VLA策略中的模态纠缠问题，通过利用任务相关性证据进行有选择的表征学习正则化，显著增强了模型在传感器损坏、单模态可用和物理干扰物下的鲁棒性，且不引入任何推理开销，具备跨真实平台、跨具身形态的普适性。

Abstract: Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policies fuse multimodal sensory inputs, but training on limited and homogeneous robot demonstrations encourages spurious inter-sensor correlations rather than task-relevant signal, a failure we term modality entanglement. Under real-world occlusions and distractors, this manifests as nuisance sensitivity to corruption of uninformative sensors and single-modality insufficiency when only one informative sensor remains intact. We propose Evidence-Gated Regularization (EGR), a modality-agnostic training objective that introduces zero inference-time overhead. EGR derives a per-frame and per-sensor task-relevance signal to gate two state-conditional consistency objectives: invariance on low-evidence sensors, and single-sensor sufficiency on high-evidence ones. We introduce a benchmark based on BEHAVIOR-1K, comprising a fast inference-only diagnostic suite and 47 rollout-based skills targeting modality entanglement. We validate EGR on this benchmark and on two real-robot setups with fundamentally different embodiments: a bi-manual setup with two Kinova arms and three RGB cameras, and a single-arm MELFA ASSISTA setup combining vision and GelSight tactile sensors. EGR improves simulation success rates (SR) from 12.5% to 16.4% under full modalities (+31%), from 9.4% to 16.5% under uninformative-sensor corruption (+75%), and from 2.8% to 6.1% under single-sensor fallback (+120%). Under physical-object distractors, EGR boosts SR from 30% to 85% on the bi-manual setup (+183%) and from 55% to 70% on the tactile setup (+27%).

</details>

Conclusion: 该框架实现了多段软体机械臂动态、可扩展且精确的实时形状控制。

Abstract: Multi-segment soft robotic arms can continuously reconfigure their body shapes for safe interaction, but tip control alone is insufficient for constrained-space tasks. Therefore, shape control is a more important task for multi-segment soft arms than tip control, but remains challenging due to the high dimensionality and nonlinear dynamics of continuum deformation. In existing work, shape control accuracy is defined by the error in the global frame (global shape error). For multi-segment soft arms, using only global shape error as the control objective is insufficient, as segment coupling, gravity-induced loading, and inertial effects become more significant. This difficulty increases with the number of segments. In this paper, we present a Koopman-based model predictive control framework that combines global and local observables, enabling real-time shape control on multi-segment soft robotic arms. The framework is evaluated through numerical and physical experiments. Numerical experiments demonstrate the scalability of the proposed controller by achieving shape control on robots with up to 10 independently actuated segments. The physical experiments demonstrate that the controller is capable of (1) real-time shape control of 3- and 5-segment robotic arms with tip speeds up to 0.6 m/s, (2) robust tracking without retraining, including distal payloads up to 400~g and recovery from a 7~N lateral disturbance, and (3) the potential for future inspection applications through a confined-space demonstration. These results demonstrate that the proposed framework enables dynamic, scalable, and accurate real-time shape control on multi-segment soft robotic arms.

</details>


### [47] [Following a Unique Path: A Fast Certifier Applied to Outlier-Robust Pose Registration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03222)
*Connor Holmes,Abhishek Goudar,Timothy D. Barfoot*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出Central-Path Certifier (CP-Cert)，一种针对具有退化性半定规划松弛的机器人优化问题定制的可认证方法。该方法从候选解出发，在可行空间附近寻找中心路径以获得有效证书，并利用间接线性代数、问题稀疏性和并行性提升效率，应用于矩阵加权位姿配准和点云数据关联，运行速度比最先进的直接求解器快三个数量级。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的可认证方法通常采用"局部求解然后认证"（local-solve-then-certify）范式，即用局部求解器获取候选解，再用高效线性代数技术认证其最优性。然而，对于机器人学中的许多问题，松弛中存在一种退化性（degeneracy），导致这种范式失效，只能诉诸于代价高昂的凸松弛优化。因此需要一种专门针对这类退化性问题的高效可认证方法。

Method: CP-Cert方法以候选解为起点，在可行空间中寻找邻近区域——即中心路径（central path）——在该区域可以轻易获得有效的最优性证书。方法通过利用间接线性代数技术、问题稀疏性以及并行计算来保持高效性。此外，论文还提出了一种新的矩阵加权位姿注册与点云数据关联的联合SDP松弛，并将其应用于离群鲁棒的位姿估计流水线中。

Result: 在模拟实验中，论文探索了所提出新松弛的性质，证明CP-Cert具有快速和可扩展的特点，运行速度比最先进的直接求解器快最多三个数量级。论文还将这些贡献整合为一个可认证、鲁棒于离群值的位姿估计流水线，并在真实世界数据上进行了验证。

Conclusion: CP-Cert为退化性SDP松弛下难以用传统"局部求解-认证"范式解决的问题提供了一条高效的认证路径，通过中心路径搜索避免了昂贵的直接松弛优化。其结合新提出的矩阵加权位姿注册与数据关联SDP松弛，构成了一个即快速又具可认证保障、且在真实数据中有效的位姿估计完整方案。

Abstract: Certifiable methods have arisen as a means to guarantee global optimality of solutions to non-convex problems using convex semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations. The most performant of these methods use a local solver to obtain the candidate solution, and then certify its optimality using efficient linear algebra techniques. However, for many problems of interest in robotics, this local-solve-then-certify approach is impeded by a form of degeneracy in the relaxation, leaving a costly optimization of the relaxation as the only recourse. In this paper, we introduce our Central-Path Certifier (CP-Cert), a certifiable method explicitly tailored to certify candidate optima to problems that exhibit this form of degeneracy. Using a candidate as a starting point, our approach seeks a nearby region of the feasible space -- known as the central path -- where a valid certificate can be readily obtained. The approach is kept efficient by exploiting indirect linear algebra techniques, problem sparsity, and parallelism. We apply CP-Cert to both matrix-weighted pose registration and pointcloud data association, whose novel SDP relaxation is of independent interest. On simulated examples, we explore the properties of this novel relaxation and show that CP-Cert is fast and scalable, achieving runtimes that are up to three orders of magnitude faster than state-of-the-art direct solvers. Finally, we combine these contributions into a certifiable, outlier-robust pose-estimation pipeline, which we apply to real-world data.

</details>


### [48] [Long-Horizon Consistent and Interaction-Aware World Models for Multi-Style End-to-End Driving](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03225)
*Yuxuan Han,Kunyuan Wu,Liyunong Yang,Zilu Wang,Cansen Jiang,Yi Xiao,Liang Hu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: StyleDrive是一个基于世界模型的端到端自动驾驶强化学习框架，通过时间一致性正则化、显式状态解耦和多风格策略优化三大创新，在Bench2Drive基准上实现了88.44的驾驶分数和66.82%的成功率，并在真实自动导引车上验证了sim-to-real迁移能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有世界模型在端到端自动驾驶中存在三个关键局限性：长时域想象rollouts中的时间不一致性、对车辆-环境交互建模不足，以及对多样化驾驶风格的适应性有限，这些问题制约了基于想象rollouts的强化学习效率。

Method: 提出了三个核心技术组件：(1) 时间一致性正则化——通过门控交叉注意力整合历史潜在状态，稳定长时域想象rollouts并减轻误差累积；(2) 显式状态解耦模块——将自车相关与自车无关的交互状态分离，提升复杂交通场景下决策的可解释性和效率；(3) 使用Group Relative Policy Optimization实现多风格驾驶行为，以轨迹级相对优势替代逐步骤奖励优化，降低奖励方差并支持无需重新训练的多样驾驶风格。

Result: 在Bench2Drive闭环驾驶基准上，驾驶分数达到88.44（较此前最优的世界模型方法提升17.08），成功率达到66.82%（提升16.58%）；并在真实自动导引车平台上验证了动态驾驶场景中良好的sim-to-real迁移能力。

Conclusion: StyleDrive通过统一学习范式中联合强化长时域一致性、解耦交互状态并支持多风格策略优化，有效克服了现有世界模型方法的局限性，在仿真与真实场景中均展现出显著优势。

Abstract: End-to-end autonomous driving has increasingly adopted world model-based reinforcement learning frameworks to improve learning efficiency through \textit{imagined rollouts}. However, existing world models suffer from three key limitations: temporal inconsistency in long-horizon imagined rollouts, inadequate modeling of ego-environment interactions, and limited adaptability to diverse driving styles. To address these challenges, we propose \textit{StyleDrive}, a world-model-based learning framework that jointly enforces long-horizon consistency, explicitly disentangles interactive traffic states, and supports multi-style policy optimization within a unified learning paradigm. First, we introduce a temporal consistency regularization that integrates historical latent states through gated cross-attention, stabilizing long-horizon imagined rollouts and mitigating error accumulation. Second, we design an explicit state disentanglement module that separates ego-relevant from ego-irrelevant interactive states, enabling more interpretable and efficient decision-making in complex traffic scenarios. Third, we enable multi-style driving behaviors through Group Relative Policy Optimization, which replaces per-step reward optimization with trajectory-wise relative advantages, reducing reward variance and supporting diverse driving styles without retraining. We evaluate StyleDrive on the Bench2Drive closed-loop driving benchmark, achieving a driving score of 88.44 (+17.08 over the previous best world model-based method) and a success rate of 66.82 (+16.58). Furthermore, we deploy StyleDrive on a real automated guided vehicle platform and demonstrate promising sim-to-real transfer capability in dynamic driving scenarios.

</details>

Abstract: This paper presents an experimental design for constructing a multimodal dataset to analyze user engagement in human-robot interaction (HRI). Prior studies have mainly relied on observable behavioral cues, with limited frameworks integrating physiological signals. We therefore propose a structured data-collection protocol to build a multimodal dataset that includes wearable physiological signals, behavioral data, and self-report measures under different levels of task complexity defined in this experiment.

### [50] [R2S-Eval: Robot Evaluation with Real-to-Sim Calibration via Vision-Language Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03276)
*Yidi Wang,Feixiang Ruan,Ruoqu Chen,Jie Yin,Yang Yu,Mengdi Xu,Kaifeng Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: R2S-Eval提出了一种结合真实到仿真（real-to-sim）标定与视觉语言模型（VLM）偏好评估的机器人操作策略评估流水线，通过仿真生成rollout视频并由VLM评估执行质量，避免反复实机试验，产出稳定且质量感知的策略排名。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统真实世界机器人评估成本高、不稳定且信息量不足：需要反复硬件试验、人工重置场景和持续监控，重复评估可能产生不同排名，且主要依赖二元成功率指标，无法反映执行质量。人类评估机器人则通过观察和比较完整行为而非仅依赖成败结果。

Method: R2S-Eval包含两个核心组件：(1) real-to-sim标定组件，在与真实世界评估设置对齐的仿真环境中高效生成rollout视频，减少重复硬件试验需求；(2) VLM评估器，对rollout视频的执行质量进行评估并生成成对偏好，再聚合成策略排名。此外引入一个评估协议，用于验证该流水线能得到经证实的策略结论，同时缓解传统真实世界评估的关键挑战。

Result: 在仿真和真实世界的实验中，R2S-Eval产生了可靠且稳定的策略结论，与人类偏好达成一致，显著减少了重复硬件操作的工作量，并能揭示二元成功标签无法捕捉的行为质量差异。

Conclusion: R2S-Eval将机器人评估从人工成功计数推进到自动化、统计稳定且质量感知的行为评估范式，为通用VLA模型的真实部署评价提供了更高效可靠的替代方案。

Abstract: Evaluating robot manipulation policies is becoming increasingly important as generalist models, particularly vision-language-action (VLA) models, are deployed on physical robots. However, conventional real-world evaluation remains labor-intensive, unstable, and insufficiently informative. It requires repeated hardware trials, manual scene resets, and continuous operator monitoring, may produce different policy rankings across repeated evaluations, and primarily relies on success-rate metrics that provide limited information about execution quality. In contrast, humans assess robot performance by observing and comparing complete behaviors rather than relying solely on binary success outcomes. To this end, we propose R2S-Eval, an evaluation pipeline that combines real-to-sim calibration with vision-language model (VLM) preference evaluation. The real-to-sim component efficiently generates rollout videos in a simulator calibrated to the real-world evaluation setting, thereby reducing the need for repeated hardware trials. The VLM evaluator assesses the execution quality of rollout videos and produces pairwise preferences, which are subsequently aggregated into policy rankings. We further introduce a protocol to assess whether the proposed evaluation pipeline yields validated policy conclusions while mitigating the key challenges of conventional real-world evaluation. Experiments in both simulation and real-world settings demonstrate that R2S-Eval produces reliable and stable policy conclusions, achieves agreement with human preferences, substantially reduces repeated hardware-operation effort, and reveals behavior-quality differences that are not captured by binary success labels. In general, R2S-Eval advances robot evaluation from manual success counting toward automated, statistically stable, and quality-aware evaluation of robot behavior. Project page: https://r2s-eval.github.io.

</details>


### [51] [ARTiS: An Adaptive Robotic Gripper for Enhanced Tool Manipulation in Disassembly Applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03362)
*Roman Mykhailyshyn,Domae Yukiyasu,Harada Kensuke*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出ARtiS（自适应机器人工具夹持器），一种用于拆装系统的软硬结合机械夹持器，结合软体手掌与指尖的适应性、拟人手的灵活性以及刚性机构的鲁棒性。通过手掌主动堵塞（jamming）和指尖鳍条（fin-ray）自适应实现多种场景下工具的牢固夹持，并利用七自由度设计实现高手指灵巧度。作者使用多种常规拆装工具进行了全面评估，验证其顺应性、耐用性与功能通用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 机器人与人类在使用工具时都需要在抓取与持握之间保持稳定，尤其在拆装流程中，快速自适应操作决定效率与一致性。然而现有机器人抓取技术在作业中安全操控工具方面存在显著局限性，需要在适应性、灵巧性和鲁棒性之间取得平衡。

Method: 设计了一种名为ARTiS的新型夹持器，采用"软体手掌+指尖+刚性机构"的混合架构。手掌通过主动堵塞机制实现可变刚度以稳固夹持，指尖利用鳍条效应实现自适应变形贴合工具表面；手指配备七自由度设计，使指尖能够定向到任意表面，兼顾自动化与协作任务需求。

Result: 使用一系列常规拆装工具对夹持器的顺应性（compliance）、耐用性（durability）和功能通用性（functional versatility）进行了全面评估，验证了所提设计在实际工具操控场景中的有效性。

Conclusion: ARTiS通过融合软体、拟人与刚性机制的创新设计，有效解决了拆装系统中工具安全夹持与灵巧操控的难题，为自适应机器人工具抓取提供了可行的解决方案，后续公布了硬件说明与视频资料供复现与扩展。

Abstract: Grasping and holding tools while using them presents a considerable challenge not only for robots but also for humans. Such a challenge is particularly noticeable in processes involving assembly and disassembly, where efficiency and consistency depend on performing rapidly adaptive tasks. Nonetheless, contemporary robotic grasping technologies that can securely manipulate tools during operation frequently have significant constraints. In this paper, introduce ARTiS (Adaptive Robotic Tool Gripper in Disassembly Systems), a novel gripper that combines the adaptability of soft grippers, the dexterity of anthropomorphic hands, and the robustness of rigid mechanisms with a soft palm and fingertips. This unique combination makes it possible to hold tools securely in a variety of situations through using active jamming in the palm and fin-ray adaptation in fingertips. Furthermore, high finger dexterity is achieved through the seven degrees of freedom design, which enables the fingertips to orient to any surface, both for automated solutions and collaborative tasks. A comprehensive evaluation was conducted using a range of conventional disassembly tools to assess the gripper's compliance, durability, and functional versatility. More information, hardware instructions, and videos at https://romanmykhailyshyn.github.io/artis/

</details>


### [52] [Programming and execution of skill-based human-robot-crane collaborative tasks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03392)
*Taneli Lohi,Markku Suomalainen,Roope Mellanen,Tapio Heikkilä*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文展示了一个基于CAD模型的参数化机器人技能编程与执行系统，采用改进的行为树（仅事件通信）作为动态参数化执行控制结构，并通过人-机器人-起重机协作插入重物的技能实例验证了系统的可行性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 生产环境的剧烈变化给机器人制造和室内物流带来挑战，需要具备敏捷性、灵活性和鲁棒性的新能力，以应对参数多变的任务集合。

Method: 提出基于CAD模型的易用编程与执行系统，支持参数化技能和技能监控器；采用改进的行为树作为动态参数化执行控制结构，仅使用基于事件的通信；通过人指令操作悬挂式起重机和机械臂协作插入重型物体的技能作为测试案例。

Conclusion: 该参数化技能系统及其事件驱动行为树控制结构能够为多变生产任务提供灵活、鲁棒的执行机制，并通过人机协作案例得到验证。

Abstract: Highly varying production sets increasing challenges for robotic manufacturing and indoor logistics. New capabilities for agility, flexibility, and robustness are needed. Robot skills, integrating motions, tool operations, and sensor perceptions consistently provide an execution mechanism for a versatile set of tasks with varying parameters. In this paper, easy-to-use CAD-model based programming and execution system for parametrized skills and skill monitors is showcased. The execution control structure is dynamic and parametrized, based on a modified Behavior Tree, where only event based communication is used. A human-robot-crane collaborative skill is shown as a test example, where a human instructs an overhead crane and a manipulator in inserting a heavy object supported by the crane, and guided by the manipulator, into the goal.

</details>


### [53] [Air-Ground Collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation via Shared Bird's-Eye Maps](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03483)
*Shuning Zhang,Liang Li,Yunheng Wang,Tao Wang,Yihang Kang,Renjing Xu*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出AGC-VLN，这是首个用于空地协同视觉-语言导航（Air-Ground Collaborative VLN）的免训练基线方法。核心思想是将导航任务分解为基于VLM的语义推理和确定性几何执行两部分，在二者之间暴露协作接口：无人机在全局鸟瞰视角上渲染无人车位姿及VLM锚定的目标标记（CAR/GOAL），形成共享鸟瞰地图，从而为无人车提供全局空间上下文并规划沿路路径，同时无人机运行3D-SPF空间搜索算法定向飞向目标。在CARLA-Air Town10HD的100个闭环episode中达到77.0%的联合成功率，较最弱单体agent（无人机50.0%）实现+27.0%协作增益，并超出最强已发布单agent基线（Travel UAV，53.0%）24个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 空地协同VLN（UAV提供全局鸟瞰视角，UGV提供局部第一人称视角）尚未被充分探索：现有免训练方法仅解决单agent任务、缺乏协作机制；近期CARLA-Air评估发现五种SOTA VLA模型在协同场景下均无稳定合作行为；朴素语义通信或双向耦合甚至会导致性能退化。因此亟需建立有效的空地协同训练免基线。

Method: AGC-VLN将免训练方法分解为VLM语义推理与确定性几何执行两部分，暴露协作接口：无人机以其全局视角渲染UGV报告位姿和VLM锚定目标为带距离标签的CAR/GOAL标记，生成共享鸟瞰地图；UGV从该地图获取第一视角无法提供的全局空间上下文，用冻结VLM规划沿路路径并在闭环控制下执行；同时无人机运行3D-SPF（SPF的空间搜索升级版）在下视图中定位目标并飞向其。

Result: 在CARLA-Air Town10HD场景的100个闭环episode上，AGC-VLN达到77.0%联合成功率；相对较弱单体agent（UAV的50.0%）实现+27.0%协作增益；超越最强已发布单agent基线（Travel UAV，53.0%）24个百分点。

Conclusion: AGC-VLN表明无人机全局视角与无人车沿路执行的互补性是实现空地协同VLN有效协作的关键，证明无需训练即可通过合理设计协作接口获得显著的协同增益。

Abstract: Air-ground collaborative Vision-and-Language Navigation (VLN) pairs an unmanned aerial vehicle (UAV) with a global bird's-eye view and an unmanned ground vehicle (UGV) with a local first-person view, yet the setting remains largely unexplored: existing training-free methods solve single-agent tasks but offer no collaboration mechanism, and a recent CARLA-Air evaluation found no stable cooperative behavior across five state-of-the-art VLA models; naive semantic communication or bidirectional coupling even degrades performance. We establish AGC-VLN (Air-Ground Collaborative VLN), the first training-free baseline for air-ground collaborative VLN. The key insight is that training-free methods decompose navigation into VLM-based semantic reasoning and deterministic geometric execution, exposing a collaboration interface: the UAV's global view, over which it renders the UGV's reported pose and the VLM-anchored target as CAR/GOAL markers with distance labels, yielding a shared bird's-eye map. From this map, the UGV acquires global spatial context its first-person view cannot provide, plans a road-following path with a frozen VLM, and executes it under closed-loop control; in parallel, the UAV runs 3D-SPF, a spatial-search upgrade of SPF that localizes the target in the downward view and flies toward it. On 100 closed-loop episodes in CARLA-Air's Town10HD scene, AGC-VLN reaches a 77.0% joint success rate, a collaboration gain of +27.0% over the weaker individual agent (the UAV, 50.0%), and exceeds the strongest published single-agent baseline (Travel UAV, 53.0%) by 24.0 points, stemming from the complementarity of the UAV's global view and the UGV's road-following execution. Project page: https://github.com/ZSN2024/AGC-VLN.

</details>


### [54] [BRIDGE: An Open-Source Humanoid Platform via Morphology-Control Co-Design for Physical AI](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03497)
*Jianren Wang,Letian Qian,Zikai Wang,Weiwei Wu,Junjie Zong,Abhinav Gupta,Deepak Pathak*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种数据驱动的形态-控制协同设计框架，通过联合优化人形机器人的形态参数与全身控制策略，使其更贴近人类运动数据。引入新颖指标量化形态保真度，并实现为88cm高的开源人形平台Bridge，在基础运动、鲁棒平衡和动态机动方面均达到SOTA性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统人形机器人开发采用硬件设计与全身控制相分离的解耦范式，导致系统次优——牺牲了人类运动的流畅性与灵活性。为了弥合这一鸿沟，需要一种统一的数据驱动框架，使人形机器人能够有效利用人类行为数据实现通用具身智能。

Method: 提出数据驱动的形态-控制协同设计框架，联合优化人形机器人形态（morphology）与整体控制策略。引入新指标来量化形态保真度，该指标同时考虑(a)对人类动作的逆运动学重定向保真度和(b)动态跟踪性能，从而指导形态与控制的联合优化。

Result: 与基线人形机器人（Bumi、K1、Toddlerbot）相比，所提框架在所有指标上均达到SOTA表现。所设计的Bridge平台（88cm高）能够以更高保真度捕获人类运动数据，在基础运动、鲁棒平衡和高度动态机动任务中均展现卓越性能。

Conclusion: Bridge作为开源人形平台及其控制策略展示了数据驱动形态-控制协同设计范式的有效性，验证了该框架在提升人类运动数据保真度与整体动态性能方面的潜力。

Abstract: Developing humanoid robots capable of leveraging human behavioral data is essential for general-purpose embodiment, yet conventional development remains bottlenecked by a decoupled paradigm that isolates hardware design from whole-body control. This approach leads to suboptimal systems that compromise human-like fluidity and agility. To bridge this gap, we introduce a data-driven morphology-control co-design framework that optimizes humanoid morphology for human-like movement. To quantify morphological fidelity, we also introduce a novel metric that jointly considers kinematic retargeting fidelity to human motion and dynamic tracking performance. Our framework achieves state-of-the-art (SOTA) performance across all metrics compared to baseline humanoids (Bumi, K1, and Toddlerbot). Finally, we realize this design in Bridge, an open-source, 88cm-tall humanoid platform released alongside its control policy. We demonstrate that Bridge captures human motion data with superior fidelity, exhibiting exceptional performance across foundational locomotion, robust balance, and highly dynamic maneuvers. Videos and open-source materials: https://sites.google.com/view/bridgerobot.

</details>


### [55] [TRaIL-Odom: Tightly Coupled Continuous Time Radar-IMU-LiDAR Odometry with Adaptive Doppler Weighting](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03561)
*Chiyun Noh,Turcan Tuna,William Talbot,Marco Hutter,Laurent Kneip,Ayoung Kim*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出TRaIL-Odom框架，通过两种退化感知的多普勒重加权模块（逐点重加权与扫描级增益调度），在紧耦合的雷达-IMU-LiDAR里程计中根据LiDAR几何退化方向自适应分配雷达Doppler信息权重，显著提升了几何退化场景下的定位精度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有雷达-LiDAR融合方法使用固定的残差权重，但雷达Doppler与LiDAR几何的信息量随扫描和方向变化，导致均匀的雷达权重在不同平移方向上错误分配Doppler信息，无法适应几何退化场景。

Method: 提出两种退化感知的多普勒重加权模块：(1) 逐点雷达重加权——从LiDAR几何中识别弱平移方向，根据各雷达Doppler约束与弱子空间的对齐程度进行重加权；(2) 扫描级雷达增益调度——利用LiDAR几何各向异性调整雷达整体贡献，在LiDAR可观测性差时增强雷达，在LiDAR约束可靠时抑制雷达。

Result: 在13个评估序列中达到最先进的整体性能，在几何退化场景中优势明显；在3个退化序列的消融实验中，两种自适应加权模块组合使RMSE ATE和RTE相对于固定权重基线分别降低86.0%和78.5%。

Conclusion: 退化感知的自适应雷达多普勒权重分配能有效克服固定权重在方向退化和场景退化中的局限性，大幅提升雷达-IMU-LiDAR紧耦合里程计在退化环境下的鲁棒性和精度。代码与数据集已公开。

Abstract: Existing radar-LiDAR fusion methods rely on fixed residual weights, even though the informativeness of radar Doppler and LiDAR geometry is scan- and direction-dependent, leading to uniform radar weighting that misallocates Doppler information across translational directions. To address this limitation, we propose two degeneracy-aware Doppler reweighting modules within a tightly coupled Radar-IMU-LiDAR odometry framework: per-point radar reweighting and scan-wise radar gain scheduling. Since geometric degeneracy is directional, we first identify weak translational directions from the LiDAR geometry and reweight individual radar Doppler constraints based on their alignment with the weak subspace. We further adjust the overall radar contribution using LiDAR geometric anisotropy such that radar is emphasized when LiDAR observability is poor and suppressed when LiDAR constraints are already reliable. Across 13 evaluated sequences, TRaIL-Odom achieves state-of-the-art overall performance, with clear advantages in geometrically degenerate scenes. In ablation experiments on three degenerate sequences, combining the two adaptive weighting modules reduces RMSE ATE and RTE by 86.0% and 78.5% relative to the fixed-weight baseline. We make our code and an accompanying dataset publicly available at https://github.com/ChiyunNoh/TRaIL-Odom.

</details>


### [56] [Toward Physically Grounded JEPA World Models for Goal-Conditioned Robotic Planning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03565)
*Muyuan Liu,Yue Huang,Zheng Liang,Xiang Gao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种端到端的JEPA世界模型，在潜在状态预测的基础上引入逆动力学（IDM）和状态对齐（SA）两个辅助目标，以增强机器人规划所需的表征信息。在四个基准任务中，该模型在TwoRoom（100%）、PushT（98%）和OGBench-Cube（87%）上取得最高成功率，在Reacher上与LeWorldModel表现相当。消融实验表明状态对齐在各任务上均超越仅含IDM的模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的动作条件JEPA世界模型虽然能通过潜在预测实现视觉目标规划而不重建未来像素，但单独的潜在预测并不能显式地鼓励所学习的表征保留与机器人控制相关的信息，导致表征可能缺乏对控制决策有用的关键信息。

Method: 提出的模型在潜在预测之外引入两个辅助学习目标：(1) 逆动力学（Inverse Dynamics Model, IDM），用于防止潜在塌缩并使潜在转移能够反映产生它们的动作信息；(2) 状态对齐（State Alignment, SA），将连续的表征与其对应的物理构型和运动信息进行对齐，使表征具有物理语义基底的支撑。整体以端到端方式联合优化潜在预测、IDM与SA损失。

Result: 在TwoRoom、PushT和OGBench-Cube三个基准上分别达到100%、98%和87%的最高成功率，在Reacher上性能与LeWorldModel相当。消融研究表明，加入状态对齐在所有四个任务上都一致优于仅使用IDM的模型。虽然LeWorldModel在OGBench-Cube上平均直化度更高，但迁移子空间分析显示其转移能量集中在维度显著更低的子空间中；本文提出的状态对齐模型具有更高的有效转移维度。

Conclusion: 状态对齐是逆动力学在机器人规划中的有效补充。通过将表征锚定到物理构型和运动，状态对齐提高了潜在转移的有效维度、防止表征丢失控制相关信息，从而在规划任务中取得更优性能，可作为机器人规划JEPA世界模型的通用改进策略。

Abstract: Action-conditioned JEPA world models enable planning toward visually specified goals without reconstructing future pixels, yet latent prediction alone does not explicitly encourage the learned representations to retain information relevant to robotic control. We introduce an end-to-end JEPA world model that augments latent prediction with inverse dynamics (IDM) and state alignment (SA). While inverse dynamics discourages latent collapse and makes latent transitions informative of the actions that produced them, state alignment grounds consecutive representations in their associated physical configuration and motion. Across four benchmark tasks, our model attains the highest success rates on TwoRoom (100%), PushT (98%), and OGBench-Cube (87%), while performing comparably to LeWorldModel on Reacher. Our ablation further shows that adding state alignment consistently improves planning success over IDM alone across all four tasks. Although LeWorldModel, our primary baseline, attains higher average straightening on OGBench-Cube, transition-subspace analysis shows that its transition energy is concentrated in a substantially lower-dimensional subspace. Our state-aligned model exhibits a higher effective transition dimension than LeWorldModel and improves planning over IDM alone, supporting state alignment as an effective complement to inverse dynamics for robotic planning.

</details>


### [57] [Scaling Bimanual Household Manipulation from 1,500 hours of Demonstrations to On-Policy Corrections](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03591)
*Jiafeng Xu,Qi Li,Yan Shen,Yiyu Ren,Travis Davies,Shaowen He,Ze Wang,Yifan Yang,Ran Cheng,Hao Dong*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文发布了1,500小时的双臂操作示范数据集，并基于该语料训练了XR-2视觉-语言-动作（VLA）模型，系统性实验表明其在多种数据规模下表现出稳定的性能缩放趋势，且训练效率和数据利用率高。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 鲁棒双臂操作的高质量大规模人类示范数据极为稀缺，限制了通用型（generalist）策略的学习，因此需要收集大规模多样化示范数据并验证其训练潜力。

Method: 采用专用高通量数据管道采集1,500小时多样化日常家务双臂操作示范；设计了精心编排的多阶段训练范式；系统实验验证两条关键缩放轴线——专家示范数据量变化以及基于DAgger实时人工干预修正数据的后训练。

Result: XR-2在双臂操作中表现出强性能，在所有探测的数据规模范围内任务成功率随数据量增加持续改善，呈现清晰一致的缩放趋势；同时保留良好的训练效率和高数据利用率。

Conclusion: 实验结果验证了XR-2的学习能力以及发布数据集的良好可扩展性（scaling properties），并开源数据集以支持双臂机器人操作学习的可复现研究。

Abstract: Learning generalist policies for robust bimanual manipulation is bottlenecked by the scarcity of high quality large scale human demonstration data. In this work, we release 1,500 hours of diverse bimanual manipulation demonstrations covering everyday household tasks, and use this comprehensive corpus to train XR-2, a powerful vision-language-action (VLA) model. Enabled by a purpose built high throughput data pipeline and a carefully designed multi stage training paradigm, XR-2 attains strong manipulation performance in our systematic experiments while retaining favorable training efficiency and high data utilization. We further study two critical scaling axes: varying the amount of expert demonstration data, and post training on DAgger correction data from real time human interventions. In both settings, task success rate improves steadily over the data ranges we probe, exhibiting a clear consistent scaling trend at our current data scale. These results validate both the learning capacity of XR-2 and the promising scaling properties of the released dataset, which we open source to support reproducible research on bimanual robot manipulation learning.

</details>


### [58] [FailBench: How Reliable are VLMs at Judging Robot Task Success?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03611)
*Zaruhi Navasardyan,Tatul Danielyan,Hrant Davtyan*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出FailBench，一个包含2,197次机器人操作尝试的跨领域失败检测基准（14个公开来源，12个真实世界+2个仿真）。评估13种VLM检测器发现最佳模型仅达0.77平均平衡准确率，失败检测微调模型反而劣于通用VLM。模型在物体运动可见的任务上接近饱和，但在接触密集型装配任务上退化为接近随机水平（<0.60）。错误分析揭示系统性的"歧义证据下偏向预测成功"偏差，且增加推理努力无法消除。输入级空间局部化裁剪可将最佳检测器提升2.4个百分点。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有机器人操作结果评估基准缺乏跨领域泛化的充分证据，需要构建一个涵盖多来源、多类型的失败检测基准以客观衡量VLM的泛化能力。

Method: 构建FailBench基准：整合14个公开数据源（12个真实世界+2个仿真）的2,197次操作尝试，其中75%的失败为自然发生（非人为构造），6个真实世界数据源来自非失败检测目的的数据集。系统性评估13种VLM检测器，并进行错误分析及输入级干预实验（空间定位并裁剪结果相关区域）。

Result: 最佳模型仅达0.77平均平衡准确率；失败检测微调模型一致性地劣于通用VLM及其预训练基线。模型在依赖可见物体运动的任务上接近饱和，但在接触密集型装配任务上降至<0.60的接近随机水平。模型存在系统性"成功偏向"偏差，增加推理努力无法缓解。空间局部化输入干预无额外训练即可将最佳检测器提升2.4个百分点。

Conclusion: FailBench揭示了当前VLM在机器人失败检测上的跨领域泛化局限：任务困难度由所需视觉证据类型决定（运动可见vs.接触密集），微调策略可能适得其反，输入级空间干预是无需重训练的可行改进方向。

Abstract: Vision-Language Models (VLMs) are increasingly used to evaluate robot manipulation outcomes, but existing benchmarks offer limited evidence of cross-domain generalization. We introduce FailBench, a benchmark for robot failure detection comprising 2,197 manipulation attempts across 14 public sources (12 real-world, 2 simulated). In FailBench, 75% of failures occur naturally, and six real-world sources come from non-failure-detection datasets. Evaluating 13 VLM-based detectors, we find the best model achieves only 0.77 mean balanced accuracy. Notably, models fine-tuned for failure detection consistently underperform general-purpose VLMs and their own pretrained baselines. Performance depends heavily on required visual evidence: models approach saturation when outcomes depend on observable object motion, but degrade to near-chance (<0.60 balanced accuracy) on contact-intensive assembly tasks. Error analysis reveals a systematic bias toward predicting success under ambiguous evidence, which persists even with increased reasoning effort. Finally, we show that input-level intervention--spatially localizing and cropping outcome-relevant regions--improves the top detector by 2.4 percentage points without extra training.

</details>

*Mohamad S. Moudallal,Noel J. Maalouf*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文介绍了一款名为QLAUN Bot的四足机器人平台，它是一款研究导向、经济高效、几乎完全3D打印的扭矩控制四足机器人，具有12个自由度、模块化可更换腿部设计、3D打印的准直驱（QDD）驱动器以及宽运动范围关节，旨在赋能黎巴嫩及中东和北非地区大学和研究机构的机器人研究。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 旨在为黎巴嫩和中东及北非（MENA）地区的大学和研究机构提供一款既坚固又敏捷、同时成本可负担的四足机器人研究平台，降低进入腿式机器人研究领域的门槛，促进该地区的机器人研究能力建设。

Method: 提出一种新颖的无电子器件腿设计策略，采用模块化且易于拆卸和更换的腿部结构。每条腿配备三个自由度（共12 DoF），每个关节由完全3D打印的准直驱（QDD）执行器驱动——该执行器由无刷直流电机、低减速比齿轮箱及皮带传动系统组成，用于显著提升关节扭矩输出。髋关节和膝关节执行器解耦设计以提升模块性。机器人主体由聚乳酸（PLA）3D打印并用现成部件组装，脚部采用TPU-95A打印的柔性足底以缓冲冲击和应对地形不确定性。腿部关节具有宽运动范围，包括连续的髋关节屈曲-伸展自由度。

Result: QLAUN是一款重约15公斤、具有12自由度（每条腿3个）的四足机器人平台，几乎完全采用3D打印工艺制造，在保证坚固性、敏捷性和经济性的同时实现类似工业级平台的性能，为腿式机器人运动研究提供了既有成本效益又有研究价值的开放式平台。

Conclusion: QLAUN作为一个全新的四足机器人研究平台，其设计概念和原理旨在向机器人学术和研究社区推广，为低成本、可定制且功能强大的腿式机器人研究提供可行方案。

Abstract: QLAUN Bot (Quad-Legged Adaptive Unmanned Navigator Robot) is a torque-controlled quadruped robot that is research-oriented, cost-effective, and aimed at achieving simultaneous robustness and agility while being completely 3D-printed. It is a quadruped robot that is aimed at empowering robotics research at universities and research institutes in Lebanon and the MENA region. Using a novel electronics-free leg design strategy, we present a modular robot with interchangeable and easily replaceable legs. The 15 kg robot possesses 12 DoF (Degrees-of-Freedom) with three per leg, each paired with a completely 3D-printed Quasi-Direct Drive (QDD) actuator that consists of a brushless DC motor and a low-ratio gearbox transmission that is connected to a belt transmission system for significantly increasing the torque outputs at the joints. We present legs that have decoupled hip and knee actuators to improve the overall modularity of the robot. QLAUN is almost completely 3D-printed using polylactic acid (PLA) and assembled using off-the-shelf parts to create a robust, agile, and affordable robot for legged robot locomotion research. The legs possess joints with wide ranges of motion, including a continuous hip flexion-extension joint. A compliant foot, printed using TPU-95A is also implemented for alleviating hard impacts and handling terrain uncertainties. This extended abstract aims to introduce QLAUN, a novel platform for robotics research, emphasizing the design concepts and principles that underpin its development to the academic and research communities in the field of robotics.

</details>


### [60] [Local Path Planning and Obstacle Avoidance for an Omnicopter Platform](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03630)
*Mikolaj Helinski,Spilios Theodoulis,Mahmoud Hamandi,Abdullah Mohamed Ali,Anthony Tzes,Marija Popovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种面向六自由度全向多旋翼（omnicopter）的实时局部规划与避障模块，通过将动态窗口法（DWA）扩展到六自由度（6D-DWA），结合局部地图体素化、球体近似机身几何和自适应速度采样，实现了在0.2秒控制周期内的实时规划，并通过情境感知的"敏捷模式"在线调整评分权重以提升对未知障碍物的响应能力。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自主无人机在杂乱环境中运行时，全局规划器（如RRT*）无法直接以控制频率部署，亟需一种能够在受限空间内实时运行、且支持六自由度运动的局部规划与避障方法。

Method: (i) 将DWA扩展至六自由度（6D-DWA）；(ii) 通过局部地图体素化降低计算复杂度；(iii) 采用紧凑球体对飞行器几何进行近似；(iv) 在6D搜索空间中进行自适应速度采样；(v) 引入情境感知的"敏捷模式"，在线调整评分权重以权衡目标进度、安全裕度及避障机动中的航向/朝向约束。

Result: 规划器在0.2秒控制循环内稳定运行；对密集航点全局路径的跟踪平均横向误差小于0.1m，平均航向误差为13度；在静态环境中成功避障。对于未知障碍物，敏捷模式在偏心障碍场景中成功率为79.3%，居中障碍场景为41.4%。

Conclusion: 6D-DWA方法有效实现了全向多旋翼在杂乱环境中的实时规划与避障，自适应权重机制（敏捷模式）显著提升了对未知障碍物的响应能力，但在高度受限的几何约束下仍存在局限性，需要进一步改进。

Abstract: Autonomous unmanned aerial vehicles (UAVs) increasingly operate in cluttered environments where global planners such as RRT* are not directly deployable at control rates. This paper presents a real-time local planning and obstacle avoidance module for an omnidirectional multirotor (omnicopter) by extending the Dynamic Window Approach to six degrees of freedom (6D-DWA). Our method achieves real-time feasibility through (i) local-map voxelisation, (ii) a compact sphere-based approximation of the vehicle geometry, and (iii) adaptive velocity sampling in the 6D search space. To improve reactivity to unknown obstacles, we introduce a context-aware "Agile Mode" that adjusts scoring weights online to trade-off between goal progress, clearance, and heading/facing constraints during evasive manoeuvres. We evaluate our approach in simulation across computational stress tests, dense-waypoint path tracking, and static/unknown obstacle scenarios. Our planner runs consistently within a 0.2s control loop, tracks waypoint-dense global paths with < 0.1m average cross-track error and 13deg average heading error, and avoids collisions in static environments. For unknown obstacle avoidance, Agile Mode achieves 79.3% success for an off-centre obstacle and 41.4% for a centred obstacle, highlighting both the effectiveness of adaptive weighting and remaining limitations in highly constrained geometries.

</details>


### [61] [WISE: World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03681)
*Chenhao Zhang,Hanyu Zhao,Hang Cheng,Tengfei Pan,Long Zeng*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: WISE是一个统一的框架，通过智能调度世界模型想象来高效后训练VLA策略，在交互相关的状态下选择性触发想象、进行有界多视角回滚、利用进度与完成信号评估候选未来，并据此精炼动作。在π0和π0.5上均表现一致提升，同时将GPU计算时间减少约80%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 后训练VLA策略的传统方法存在明显瓶颈：监督微调依赖昂贵专家示范，强化学习需要昂贵且不稳定的真实世界探索。世界模型虽可通过想象未来评估候选行为，但有效的后训练不仅仅需要准确的预测——想象需要在有用处被调度、在可靠虚拟范围内有界、并转化为可信的策略监督信号。在机器人操作中，想象价值在不同执行阶段差异显著，而延长回滚会累积预测误差并产生不可靠的学习信号。

Method: WISE在策略精炼过程中协调世界模型想象的使用时机与方式：1) 在交互相关状态下选择性调用想象；2) 执行有界的多视角回滚；3) 利用进度与完成信号评估候选未来；4) 使用相对结果精炼来自真实交互情境的动作。该框架统一了何时（when）以及如何（how）使用想象进行策略改进。

Result: 在π0和π0.5两个模型上的大量实验表明，WISE在多种操作任务上实现了一致性能提升，同时将GPU计算时间相比完全想象方法减少了约80%。真实世界评估进一步显示，在各种分布偏移下取得了显著的鲁棒性和泛化能力提升。

Conclusion: WISE通过智能调度世界模型想象，实现了高效且可靠的VLA策略后训练，在显著降低计算开销的同时提升了策略性能、鲁棒性和泛化能力，验证了"准确预测之外，想象调度本身也是关键"的核心观点。

Abstract: Post-training VLA policies typically rely on supervised fine-tuning with costly expert demonstrations or reinforcement learning with expensive and potentially unstable real-world exploration. World models offer a promising alternative by evaluating candidate behaviors through imagined futures, yet effective post-training requires more than accurate prediction: imagination must be scheduled where it is useful, bounded within reliable horizons, and translated into trustworthy policy supervision. In robotic manipulation, the value of imagination varies substantially across execution stages, while extended rollouts can accumulate prediction errors and introduce unreliable learning signals. We introduce WISE (World-model-guided Imagination Scheduling for Efficient Post-training of Vision-Language-Action Models), a unified framework that coordinates when and how world-model imagination is used during policy refinement. WISE selectively invokes imagination at interaction-relevant states, performs bounded multi-view rollouts, evaluates candidate futures using progress and completion signals, and uses their relative outcomes to refine actions generated from real interaction contexts. Extensive experiments with both $π_0$ and $π_{0.5}$ demonstrate consistent improvements across diverse manipulation tasks while reducing GPU computation time by approximately 80% compared with full imagination. Real-world evaluations further show substantial gains in robustness and generalization under diverse real-world distribution shifts.

</details>


### [62] [Predictive Zonotope Reduction: Precise Runtime Monitoring under Uncertainty](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03699)
*Vladimir Krsmanovic,Florian Kohn,Bernd Finkbeiner,Milan Simovic*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出预测性Zonotope缩减（PZR）方法，将运行时监控中的zonotope缩减器选择建模为最优控制问题，使用波束搜索模型预测控制求解，并通过策略蒸馏得到小型神经策略以提高执行速度，在RLola框架和MuJoCo模拟的5自由度机械臂上验证了相比静态缩减策略显著降低误报率的效果。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在物理环境中运行的机器人基于不确定传感器测量做出控制决策，可能产生不安全或次优行为。运行时监控器用zonotope表示不确定性时，持续纳入新测量会导致其阶数无界增长，需周期性施加过近似缩减。现有方法在全程使用固定缩减方法，但最优选择依赖于当前状态，因此需要一个能动态选择缩减器的方法。

Method: 将缩减器选择形式化为最优控制问题，利用波束搜索模型预测控制（MPC）进行求解；进一步通过策略蒸馏将MPC策略提炼为小型神经网络策略，兼顾高执行速度与良好性能；实现在RLola运行时监控框架中。

Result: 在MuJoCo模拟的5自由度机械臂上（传感器不确定性按ISO 5725建模），在树莓派5上实验表明动态缩减相比静态缩减策略显著降低监控中的误报率，且神经策略推理速度远高于MPC。

Conclusion: PZR证明了不确定性感知运行时监控中缩减策略动态选择的有效性与可行性，在保持精度的同时满足了资源受限实时系统对执行速度的要求。

Abstract: Robots operating in physical environments make control decisions based on uncertain sensor measurements, which can lead to unsafe or suboptimal actions. Runtime monitors that check their behavior against safety specifications must represent this uncertainty soundly. Zonotopes are a widely used representation, but continuously incorporating new measurements grows their order unboundedly, so monitors must periodically apply an over-approximating reduction. The choice of the reduction method substantially affects the zonotope's precision, yet existing approaches typically utilize a fixed method throughout the run, even though the optimal choice depends on the current state. This paper presents a Predictive Zonotope Reduction (PZR) approach, which frames reducer selection as an optimal control problem and solves it using beam-search model predictive control. Policy distillation into a small neural policy further provides substantially higher execution speed than model predictive control while maintaining improved performance, enabling uncertainty-aware runtime monitoring on resource-constrained real-time systems. We implement our approach in the RLola runtime monitoring framework and evaluate it on a 5-degree-of-freedom robotic arm simulated in MuJoCo, with sensor uncertainty modeled according to ISO 5725. Experiments on a Raspberry Pi 5 show that dynamic reduction significantly lowers false-positive rates in monitoring compared with static reduction strategies.

</details>


### [63] [Toward an~Integrated Cognitive--Ergonomic Architecture for~Human--Machine Interaction: Combining Cognitive Models with~Human Factors Ergonomics](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03704)
*Antoine Lenat,Olivier Cheminat,Damien Chablat,Camilo Charron*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种综合认知架构与人为因素工效学(HFE)理论的集成方法，通过比较分析SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM等认知模型，设计了一种面向动态人机交互(HMI)环境的定制化架构，并基于工业机器人应用进行了实证验证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 认知架构（如SOAR、ACT-R等）与人为因素工效学在理论上具有很强的互补性，但两者在实践应用中常被割裂，缺乏统一的框架来桥接认知理论与系统设计，尤其在动态人机交互环境中难以有效建模人的能力。

Method: 对SOAR、ACT-R、LIDA和COCOM四种主流认知模型进行比较分析，基于其优势综合构建一种定制化的认知架构，并将其置于工效学框架中进行情境化，重点整合决策制定、技能习得与适应性行为等机制。

Result: 所提出的框架在工业机器人应用中进行了实证研究，验证了操作员专业知识、规范性知识与实时反馈回路在架构中的关键作用。该架构改善了人机交互系统的认知对齐，并提供了一种可扩展的设计方法论。

Conclusion: 该集成框架既推进了人类能力的理论理解，也为高风性环境中设计自适应、工效学优化的人机系统提供了实际可行的实施路径。

Abstract: This paper presents an integrated approach to modeling human competencies by combining the theoretical foundations of cognitive architectures with principles from Human Factors Ergonomics (HFE). Through a comparative analysis of established cognitive models-SOAR, ACT-R, LIDA, and COCOM-we synthesize a tailored architecture designed to address the complexities of human-machine interaction (HMI) in dynamic environments. By contextualizing this model within ergonomic frameworks, we elucidate the mechanisms underlying decision-making, skill acquisition, and adaptive behavior, bridging the gap between cognitive theory and applied system design. Our framework is empirically grounded in industrial robotics applications, where operator expertise, normative knowledge, and real-time feedback loops are critical. The proposed architecture not only enhances the cognitive alignment of HMI systems but also provides a scalable methodology for designing intelligent, human-centered interfaces in high-stakes environments. This work advances both the theoretical understanding of human competencies and the practical implementation of adaptive, ergonomically optimized systems.

</details>


### [64] [MINERVA: How Small Can a Manipulation Policy Be and Still Solve LIBERO?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03715)
*Kohei Sendai,Tatsuya Matsushima,Yusuke Iwasawa*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出MINERVA，一个仅0.54M参数的紧凑型视觉-动作策略，在LIBERO基准四个套件上实现95.1%的平均成功率，仅比使用7700倍参数的LeRobot π_0.5低2.4个点。研究发现LIBERO的任务特定容量下限约为1M参数，且该基准的指令调节主要依赖任务ID记忆而非语义理解。模型在笔记本电脑CPU上以5-9ms/块的速度重规划，比SmolVLA快113倍，比π_0.5快1400倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然数十亿参数的VLA模型在LIBERO操纵基准上占据主导地位，但该基准实际上所需要的最小模型容量尚不清楚。作者旨在通过系统性地缩小模型规模来测量LIBERO的任务特定容量下限，以揭示超大模型在该基准上是否实际上超出必要。

Method: 设计MINERVA策略家族，通过广泛扫描架构、训练和推理配置来确定性能饱和点与崩溃点。具体方法包括：对参数规模从0.25M以下到数M进行扫描；比较flow matching与L1回归两种训练范式（跨三个种子）；进行任务ID置换探针实验以测试指令条件化的语义理解程度；在LIBERO四标准套件、LIBERO-90和LIBERO-Plus上评估泛化与鲁棒性。

Result: 0.54M参数策略在LIBERO四个套件上2000次rollout中达到95.1%平均成功率；性能在约1M参数处饱和，在0.25M以下崩溃。仅action-chunk长度和视觉容量对性能影响一致超过训练种子波动带（±1点）。flow matching相对L1回归无可检测优势且回归GPU速度更快（最高3.8x）。任务ID置换探针显示改变任务ID映射即将成功率降至接近随机，表明LIBERO指令调节主要选择记忆任务。在89个LIBERO-90任务上达到94.6%，但LIBERO-Plus光强扰动下降至46-56%，对光度变换鲁棒性接近零。

Conclusion: LIBERO基准的实验容量下限远低于当前VLA模型使用的数亿乃至数十亿参数，目前的超大模型在该基准上存在显著过度参数化。这促使社区转向容量感知的设计与蒸馏方法，以开发部署高效的机器人策略。同时，LIBERO指令条件化可能从记忆中检索而非真正理解语言，这一发现对基准的有效性提出质疑。

Abstract: Vision-language-action (VLA) models with billions of parameters now dominate the LIBERO manipulation benchmark, but the model capacity actually required by the benchmark remains unclear. We introduce MINERVA (MINimal Efficient Robotic Vision-Action policy), a family of deliberately compact visuomotor policies designed to measure this task-specific capacity floor. A 0.54M-parameter policy achieves 95.1% average success over 2,000 rollouts on the four standard LIBERO suites, only 2.4 points below the reported LeRobot $π_{0.5}$ result despite using 7,700$\times$ fewer parameters. Performance saturates near 1M parameters and collapses below 0.25M. Across broad architectural, training, and inference sweeps, only action-chunk length and vision capacity consistently exceed a $\pm$1-point training-seed band. Flow matching provides no detectable advantage over direct L1 regression across three seeds, while regression is up to 3.8$\times$ faster on GPU. A task-ID permutation probe shows that standard LIBERO instruction conditioning primarily selects among memorized tasks: changing only the task-ID mapping reduces success to near chance. The same recipe achieves 94.6% success across 89 LIBERO-90 tasks, while LIBERO-Plus perturbations reduce performance to 46--56%, with near-zero robustness to photometric shifts. The 0.54M policy replans every control step in 5--9 ms per chunk on a laptop CPU, 113$\times$ faster than SmolVLA and 1,400$\times$ faster than $π_{0.5}$, without a GPU. These results establish a first empirical estimate of LIBERO's task-specific capacity floor and motivate capacity-aware design and distillation for deployment-efficient robot policies.

</details>


### [65] [RoughSense: Lightweight Terrain-Induced Rover Vibration Prediction Using Point Clouds and IMU Feedback](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03720)
*Gabriel Manuel Garcia,Stephanie Aravecchia,Miguel Angel Olivares-Mendez*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种轻量级的实时振动感知可通行性建图方法，利用LiDAR点云和IMU测量进行自主导航中的地形评估。通过RANSAC从局部点云块估计初始振动代理量，并利用IMU观测通过递归最小二乘进行在线修正，适应地质环境下的实际行驶响应。方法在月球模拟环境、户外场地和地下矿井中进行了评估。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 地下环境中自主导航面临通讯受限、计算资源有限和功率预算紧张的挑战，需要可靠的地形评估以确保安全运行。现有方法难以在资源受限条件下实现实时、准确的振动感知可通行性评估，因此亟需一种轻量级方案。

Method: 该方法包含三个关键环节：(1) 利用SLAM算法生成的局部点云块，通过RANSAC拟合估算地形几何相关的初始振动代理量；(2) 并行使用IMU提供漫游车行驶过程中的局部振动观测；(3) 采用递归最小二乘（Recursive Least Squares）将点云几何预测与IMU实测振动在线融合校正，实现自适应调整。

Result: 该方法在月球模拟环境（lunar analogue）、户外场地以及地下矿井三种不同环境中进行了实验验证，证明其具有跨场景的实际可行性与适应性。

Conclusion: 该工作表明，通过将几何信息与惯性感知在线融合，可以在资源受限的地下环境中实现轻量级、实时且自适应的振动感知可通行性建图，为空间自主导航中的地形评估提供了有效方案。

Abstract: Autonomous navigation in space requires reliable terrain assessment for safe operations, especially in underground environments with limited communication, computing resources, and power budget. This paper presents a lightweight method for real-time vibration-aware traversability mapping using a Light Detecting And Ranging (LiDAR) point cloud and Inertial Measurement Unit (IMU) measurements. An initial vibration proxy is estimated from terrain geometry by applying Random sample consensus (RANSAC) to local point-cloud patches produced by a Simultaneous Localisation And Mapping (SLAM) algorithm. In parallel, the IMU provides local observations of the vibration experienced by the rover during traversal. The point-cloud-based prediction is then corrected online using Recursive Least Squares, allowing the system to adapt the geometric estimate to the measured rover response. The approach is evaluated in a lunar analogue environment, an outdoor field, and an underground mine.

</details>
