TL;DR: 本文提出一种多葡萄藤（multi-vine）软体翻转机器人架构，通过将两条葡萄藤机器人耦合到外部集成的工作通道上，实现主动尖端转向与工具输送的独立控制，实验显示可在生长过程中实现近90度的急转弯。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 软体翻转机器人（葡萄藤机器人）在医疗导航检查中具有低摩擦生长的优势，但受限于环境交互（接触角、自由部署长度等），难以通过被动生长应对如乙状结肠等急剧弯曲的路径。同时现有工作通道集成方案限制工具尺寸、引入摩擦且仅限尖端访问环境，需要一种既能主动转向又能灵活输送工具的架构。

Method: 提出多葡萄藤架构：两条葡萄藤机器人通过软性安装尖端与外部集成的工作通道耦合。两条葡萄藤各自独立加压驱动，通过主动差动生长实现工作通道的主动尖端导向；工作通道不嵌入葡萄藤体内，而是外部集成，从而不限制工具尺寸并减少摩擦。

Result: 实验验证了该架构在生长过程中可实现接近90度的锐利转向，展示了其在医用及非医用场景的应用潜力。

Conclusion: 多葡萄藤架构有效解决了现有葡萄藤机器人主动转向与工具输送互斥的问题，为ERK协同项目EndoTheranostics中的显微手术工具输送提供了可行的技术方案，具有广泛的医疗和非医疗应用前景。

Abstract: Soft eversion robots, also known as vine robots, have attracted growing interest for navigation and inspection tasks, including minimally invasive medical applications [1]. A vine robot consists of a thin, flexible, inextensible tube folded inward that everts and grows forward when pressurized. This tip-growth enables navigation with minimal friction, making vine robots well suited for complex environments such as the human colon [2]. While their inherent softness allows passive conforma- tion to curved pathways in confined spaces, navigation performance strongly depends on environmental inter- actions, including contact angle and the length of un- constrained deployed material [3], [4]. Sharp directional changes, such as those in the sigmoid colon, often limit passive growth and necessitate active steering. Existing solutions include distributed artificial muscles [5] or dedicated tip-based steering mechanisms [6]. In addition, many applications require payload delivery, such as sensors and tools [7], [8]. Within the ERC Synergy project EndoTheranostics, this motivates the development of vine robots capable of delivering micro- surgical tools during growth. Prior work has integrated working channels within the vine body [8], [9], but these approaches constrain tool size, introduce friction, and limit access to the environment to the robot tip. In this work, we propose a multi-vine architecture in which two vine robots are coupled to an externally integrated working channel via soft mounting tips [10]. Independent vine actuation enables active tip steering while advancing the working channel without embed- ding it within the vine bodies Figure 1. Experiments demonstrate sharp steering of nearly 90 degrees during growth, highlighting the potential of this architecture for versatile medical and non-medical applications.

</details>


### [67] [Virtual Testing of Automated Driving Systems through Credible Simulations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03760)
*Riccardo Dona,Espedito Rusciano,Biagio Ciuffo*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于风险的框架，用于评估自动驾驶系统（ADS）安全评估中仿真工具链的可信度。该框架借鉴NASA STD-7009标准，扩展了传统V&V方法，将可信度要求与仿真输出的预期用途及决策的安全关键性显式关联，提供涵盖工具链管理、建模假设、验证、确认和敏感性分析的全生命周期评估方案，并按用途差异化设定可信度阈值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自动驾驶系统行为复杂且运行设计域规模庞大，使得仅依赖物理测试不可行，虚拟测试（VT）在审批阶段被广泛使用。然而，现有ADS领域的虚拟测试认证方法通常仅依赖"验证（validation-only）"实践，在应用于复杂、多工具仿真环境时扩展性差，无法有效支撑道路安全决策所需的仿真可信度评估，因此亟需一种更系统的可信度评估框架。

Method: 提出一个基于风险的仿真工具链可信度评估框架，具体包括：（1）从NASA STD-7009等安全关键领域借鉴经验；（2）将可信度要求与仿真输出的预期用途及拟支持决策的安全关键性显式关联；（3）提供全生命周期导向的评估方案，整合工具链管理、建模假设与限制、验证、确证（validation）和敏感性分析等维度；（4）定义按比例划分的可信度接受阈值，区分探索性安全分析、部分决策支持和物理测试替代等不同使用场景。

Result: 该框架在ADS领域进行了验证演示，表明其能够根据不同仿真用途提供差异化的可信度要求。框架不限于ADS，可直接适用于以虚拟测试为核心的交通安全评估和监管决策场景中，提供比validation-only方法更具可扩展性的可信度评估路径。

Conclusion: 基于风险的可信度评估框架是传统V&V方法的有效扩展，能够应对复杂多工具仿真环境下的认证挑战。通过将可信度需求与用途和决策安全关键性挂钩，并在全生命周期中对仿真工具链进行系统评估，可以为安全监管审批提供更可靠、更具可扩展性的仿真可信度判断依据。

Abstract: Simulation is increasingly used to support safety-related decision-making in road transport, particularly for the assessment and approval of automated driving systems (ADS). The complexity of ADS behavior and size of their operational design domains make exclusive reliance on physical testing impractical, leading to extensive use of virtual testing (VT) during the approval phase. This shift raises critical questions regarding the credibility of modelling and simulation (M&S) results used to support road safety decisions. Current VT accreditation approaches in the ADS domain typically rely on validation-only practices, which have been shown to scale poorly when applied to complex, multi-tool simulation environments. To address this limitation, this paper proposes a risk-based framework for assessing the credibility of simulation toolchains used in ADS safety evaluation, drawing inspiration from established practices in other safety-critical domains, notably NASA's STD-7009 for models and simulations. The framework extends traditional verification and validation (V&V) by explicitly linking credibility requirements to the intended use of simulation outputs and to the safety criticality of the decisions they support within the approval process. It provides a lifecycle-oriented assessment scheme integrating toolchain management, modelling assumptions and limitations, verification, validation, and sensitivity analysis. Credibility acceptance thresholds are defined proportionally, allowing differentiated requirements depending on whether simulation is used for exploratory safety analysis, partial decision support, or as a substitute for physical testing. While demonstrated for ADS, the proposed approach is directly applicable to road safety and simulation studies where VT plays a central role in safety assessment and regulatory decision-making.

</details>


### [68] [Robot Aware Computational Design of Object Specific Passive Grippers for Additive Manufacturing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03761)
*Abdullah Yahya Abdullah Omaisan,Ibrahim Sheikh Mohamed*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一个端到端计算管道，将选定物体网格、实测物体状态和六轴机器人转换为一个物体专用、非驱动的增材制造抓手。管道集成位姿配准、接触采样、不确定性扳手筛选、被动捕获机构选择、物体贴合表面合成、全姿态逆运动学、扫掠体积感知制造域、定向FDM有限元筛选和约束SIMP拓扑优化，并通过可追溯的设计标识符将所有输出与源网格、位姿、法兰、接触集和插入假设绑定。四个存档设计（兔、相机法兰、3DBenchy、多面半身像）通过数字筛选门槛，但未达到操作级发布。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有工作流将抓取选择、工具几何、运动和结构设计视为分离问题，缺乏将抓取设计与机器人运动学、制造约束及结构力学统一考虑的完整管道，导致设计不可追溯且难以验证。

Method: 提出的管道包含以下耦合模块：精确网格RGB-D/ICP位姿配准、确定性表面接触采样、不确定性感知扳手筛选、六种被动捕获机构选择、物体贴合表面合成、全姿态机器人逆运动学、考虑扫掠体积的制造域、定向熔融沉积有限元筛选以及约束三维SIMP拓扑优化。同时每个导出设计通过可追溯设计标识符与源网格、物体位姿、机器人法兰、接触集和插入假设绑定，并推导了配准、接触、公差、有限元和密度优化方程。

Result: 研究者推导了数值构造的三种性质：节点载荷保持、SIMP插值下单调柔度灵敏度、拓扑后处理后体素域包含性。四个存档设计（兔、相机法兰、3DBenchy、多面半身像）通过标称配合、不确定扳手、运行时扫掠和基线/拓扑后FEA门槛。在人为放大的±3mm、±5度姿态压力测试中，160个模拟试验保留29-134个。重建拓扑保留92.0-97.6%的FE域。但缺少未校准的仪器测试和标签校准材料属性，四个设计仅为数字筛选状态而非操作发布。

Conclusion: 该管道将抓取选择、几何设计、运动规划与结构优化统一为可追溯的端到端流程，结构域完整性和功能区域保护在拓扑优化中表现良好。但由于缺乏实物校准和仪器测试，设计仍停留在数字筛选验证阶段，需要进一步实验验证才能实现操作级发布。

Abstract: This paper presents an end-to-end computational pipeline that converts a selected object mesh, a measured object state, and a selected six-axis robot into an object-specific, unactuated, additively manufacturable gripper. The method couples exact-mesh RGB-D/ICP pose registration, deterministic surface-contact sampling, uncertainty-aware wrench screening, selection among six passive capture mechanisms, object-conformal surface synthesis, full-orientation robot inverse kinematics, a swept-volume-aware manufacturing domain, directional fused-deposition finite-element screening, and constrained three-dimensional SIMP topology optimization. Unlike workflows that treat grasp selection, tool geometry, motion, and structural design as separate problems, every exported design is bound to the source mesh, object pose, robot flange, contact set, and insertion hypothesis by a traceable design identifier. We derive the implemented registration, contact, fit-tolerance, finite-element, and density-optimization equations and prove three properties of the numerical construction: nodal load preservation, monotonic compliance sensitivity under SIMP interpolation, and voxel-domain containment after topology post-processing. Four archived object-specific attempts - a rabbit, camera flange, 3DBenchy, and faceted bust - meet the nominal fit, uncertain-wrench, runtime-sweep, and baseline/post-topology FEA gates. A deliberately enlarged +/-3 mm, +/-5 degree pose stress check differentiates the designs, retaining 29-134 of 160 simulated trials. Their reconstructed topologies retain 92.0-97.6% of the FE domain because functional regions are protected. Archived robot photographs show the corresponding printed assemblies qualitatively, while nominal material properties and absent coupon-calibrated, instrumented tests keep all four at digital-screening status rather than operational release.

</details>


### [69] [A comparative study on the accuracy & repeatability of mobile robotic platforms for the delivery of precision NDE measurement](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03794)
*SeyedMohammadAmin Nabi Pour,S. Gareth Pierce,Randika Vithanage,Ehsan Mohseni,David Carswell,Matthew Shields*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于激光跟踪仪的外部参考评估协议（地面真值约6微米），对五种商用移动机器人平台（KUKA KMP-1500、KUKA KMR、MiR250、Boston Dynamics Spot、Clearpath Husky）的静态定位精度和分段轨迹跟踪精度进行了统一评估。静态定位精度中位数从8.2 mm（KMP-1500）到63.5 mm（Spot）不等，动态横向误差从6.9 mm到112.1 mm。所有平台的基座精度均不满足航空航天NDE的0.2-1.0 mm公差要求，结果可作为确定各平台所需补充传感量级（最佳平台约一个数量级、最差平台近两个数量级）的设计输入。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 移动机器人平台为大型航空航天结构的无损检测（NDE）提供了比固定机械臂更灵活的替代方案，但各平台的基座定位精度及其对部署的指导意义尚未在统一的外部参考协议下进行评估，缺乏跨平台的可比基准。

Method: 采用激光跟踪仪构建评估流程（地面真值约6微米），在统一协议下测量五种商用移动平台的静态和分段轨迹定位精度。通过耦合多角点标定恢复激光到机器人的变换及反射器偏移，使用全位姿普通最小二乘估计，稳健估计仅作为粗差检查手段。

Result: 静态定位精度中位数范围8.2 mm（KMP-1500）至63.5 mm（Spot），仅依靠轮式里程计的Husky无法标定。动态路径跟踪以横向误差表征，对时间对齐不敏感，范围6.9 mm（KMP-1500）至112.1 mm（Spot）。精度和可标定性均与定位能力水平相关，从最新的LiDAR SLAM平台到无地图视觉里程计依次递减。所有平台基座精度均不满足航空航天NDE的0.2-1.0 mm公差。

Conclusion: 结果作为设计输入而非可行性声明，用于量化各平台所需的补充传感规模（最佳平台需约一个数量级的精度提升，最差平台需近两个数量级），为移动平台选型提供了可复现的决策依据。

Abstract: Mobile robotic platforms offer a flexible alternative to fixed manipulators for non-destructive evaluation (NDE) of large aerospace structures, but their base-positioning accuracy and how that accuracy should inform deployment have not been assessed under a common, externally referenced protocol. This work presents a laser tracker-based evaluation workflow (ground truth approximately 6 micrometers) that measures the static and segmented trajectory positioning accuracy of five commercial mobile platforms (KUKA KMP-1500, KUKA KMR, MiR250, Boston Dynamics Spot, Clearpath Husky) under a common protocol. A coupled multi-corner calibration recovers the laser-to-robot transformation and reflector offsets; ordinary least squares over all poses is used, with robust estimation retained only as a blunder check.
  Static positioning accuracy ranged from a median of 8.2 mm (KMP-1500) to 63.5 mm (Spot), with the wheel-odometry-only Husky uncalibratable. Dynamic path following was characterised by cross-track error; the component was insensitive to temporal alignment, which ranged from 6.9 mm (KMP-1500) to 112.1 mm (Spot). Both accuracy and calibratability tracked localisation capability, from the newest LiDAR SLAM platform to map-free visual odometry. No configuration meets the 0.2 to 1.0 mm aerospace NDE tolerance from the base alone; the results are framed as a design input that sizes the supplementary sensing each platform requires: roughly one order of magnitude for the best platform and nearly two for the worst, providing a reproducible basis for platform selection rather than a feasibility claim.

</details>


### [70] [FWBC-VLA: Force-Aware Whole-Body Compensation for Contact-Rich Loco-Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03889)
*Yutian Zhang,Siyuan Ma,Liwen Yang,Yang Li,Ce Hao,Haozhen Chi,Dong We,Qiaojun Yu,Dibo Hou*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出FWBC-VLA框架，实现轮腿机器人的力感知接触丰富型移动操作。通过在VLA动作解码中注入无传感器的残余力矩估计（HSR-Force），并结合整身补偿控制，实现任务级动作生成与物理交互控制的桥接，在白板擦拭和带闭门器开门等真实实验中验证了有效性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLA模型只能从视觉语言输入生成任务级动作，无法理解这些动作引发的物理交互；WBC策略虽能稳定机器人，但无法区分操作相关的接触力与外部扰动引起的力；而安装力/扭矩传感器又带来额外硬件成本和集成负担。因此需要一个无需额外传感器的力感知框架来桥接高层动作生成与低层物理控制。

Method: (1) 提出HSR-Force无传感器残余力矩估计器，推断接触强度及其时间变化；(2) 将接触估计编码为token注入VLA动作解码器，使策略感知接触起始、持续加载与释放；(3) 在包含5000+episodes的WL&Arm数据集上微调预训练VLA主干；(4) 将本体感觉状态、Jacobian导出的机体力估计和接触状态联合输入补偿生成器，产生修正动作；(5) 将操作动作与修正动作结合，送入WBC策略执行。

Result: 在白板擦拭和带闭门器开门两项真实接触丰富型移动操作任务上验证了FWBC-VLA的有效性，表明框架能够在接触丰富场景中实现有效的移动操作。

Conclusion: FWBC-VLA通过无传感器的力感知机制将任务级VLA动作生成与低层整身补偿控制桥接起来，为轮腿机器人接触丰富型移动操作提供了一种低成本、无需硬件改造的力感知解决方案。

Abstract: Contact-rich loco-manipulation requires a bridge between semantic action generation and physical interaction control. Existing Vision-language-action (VLA) models generate task-level actions from visual and linguistic observations, but cannot interpret the physical interactions induced by those actions. While the whole-body control (WBC) policy can stabilize the robot, it cannot distinguish task-relevant interaction forces from forces induced by external disturbances during manipulation. Although force/torque sensors provide direct measurements of physical interactions, retrofitting them entails additional hardware costs and substantial integration effort, particularly for platforms not designed with sensor integration in mind. To address this problem, we propose FWBC-VLA, a force-aware framework that bridges task-level VLA action generation and low-level whole-body compensation control for wheeled-legged robots. First, we introduce HSR-Force, a sensorless residual-torque estimator for inferring contact strength and its temporal variation. These contact estimates are then encoded as tokens and injected into the VLA action expert during action decoding, enabling the policy to perceive contact onset, sustained loading, and release. For loco-manipulation tasks, all parameters of the pretrained VLA backbone are fine-tuned on our WL\&Arm Dataset, which comprises more than 5,000 episodes. Moreover, the robot's proprioceptive state, the Jacobian-derived body-frame force estimate, and the estimated contact state are jointly fed into a compensation generator to produce corrective actions. The manipulation-centric actions are subsequently combined with the corrective actions and passed to the WBC policy for execution. Real-world experiments on whiteboard wiping and door opening with a door closer demonstrate the effectiveness of our FWBC-VLA in contact-rich loco-manipulation.

</details>


### [71] [A hybrid pipeline for dynamic ontology-based semantic mapping](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03891)
*Konstantinos Dimitropoulos,Ioannis Hatzilygeroudis*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种混合语义映射流水线系统，结合外部标定摄像机（通过单应性投影进行几何建图和定位）、目标检测、持续目标跟踪以及本体驱动的语义更新，构建动态语义世界模型，并使用线性回归模型校正真实世界坐标的估值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 语义映射对于机器人感知、交互和导航至关重要。传统的语义映射流程（SLAM、感知、语义融合与表示）缺乏对环境的上下文理解能力，而现有研究虽开始引入先验知识（如知识图谱或语义场景图），仍需一个能动态更新对象实例、空间属性和语义关系的统一框架来增强环境理解。

Method: 提出混合流水线系统：(1) 使用外部标定摄像机，通过单应性投影实现几何建图与定位；(2) 结合目标检测与持续目标跟踪；(3) 采用线性回归模型对真实世界坐标估值进行校正；(4) 基于本体（Ontology）驱动语义更新，持续更新对象实例、空间属性和语义关系。本体因其层级结构、语义表达能力和对动态世界建模的支持而被选为知识表示形式。

Result: 系统能够基于实时传感数据持续更新对象实例、空间属性与语义关系，构建动态语义世界模型，并借助线性回归对坐标估值进行修正。

Conclusion: 该混合流水线有效整合了几何与语义信息，利用本体作为知识表示形式支撑动态世界建模，为机器人在复杂环境中提供更丰富的上下文语义理解能力。

Abstract: Semantic mapping plays a crucial role in the ability of a robot to interact with objects, operate and navigate a complex environment. The most common pipeline for semantic mapping consists of geometric mapping and localization (SLAM), perception, semantic fusion and semantic representation. However, more recent works also integrate a form of prior knowledge in their application, most notably knowledge graphs or semantic scene graphs, to improve contextual understanding of the environment. In this paper, we present a hybrid pipeline for semantic mapping. Our system incorporates an external calibrated camera using homography projection for geometric mapping and localization, combined with object detection, persistent object tracking and ontology driven semantic updates to build a dynamic semantic world model. Linear regression models are also used for correction of the estimated values of real world coordinates. The system continuously updates object instances, spatial properties and semantic relations based on real time sensory data. Ontologies are selected as form of knowledge representation due to their hierarchical structure, semantic expressiveness and support for dynamic world modelling.

</details>


### [72] [Revisiting Topological Graphs for Macro Action based Closed-loop Reinforcement Learning of Vision Language Navigation in Continuous Environment](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03906)
*Shuhao Ye,Sitong Mao,Yuxiang Cui,Yufei Wei,Xuan Yu,Shichao Zhai,Wen Chen,Shunbo Zhou,Rong Xiong,Yue Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出将VLN-CE任务重构为分层MDP，通过拓扑图抽象将高层策略的决策空间压缩到宏动作（边界节点），配合无训练的底层控制器，并设计了动作感知价值头来支持基于图的PPO优化，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上达到SOTA。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: VLN-CE任务中现有的模仿学习管线（如行为克隆和DAgger）在闭环环境中面临分布偏移和专家动作歧义问题。虽然RL天然适合闭环决策，但直接在微观动作空间上应用RL因奖励稀疏而面临样本效率低下的瓶颈。

Method: 1) 将VLN-CE重构为分层MDP，显式解耦高层规划与低层控制；2) 将环境抽象为拓扑图，高层策略在边界节点的宏动作空间上运作，使用无训练的底层控制器作为状态转移；3) 提出动作感知价值头以在动态边界动作空间上有效评估状态价值；4) 基于该架构实现图上的PPO算法进行强化学习优化。

Result: 实验验证了所提出架构的有效性，在R2R-CE和RxR-CE基准上取得了最先进的性能。通过压缩决策时域，使闭环RL变得可行，显著提升了导航效果。

Conclusion: 通过分层MDP和拓扑图抽象，将VLN-CE从微观动作层面的稀疏奖励RL问题转化为宏动作空间上的可解RL问题，证明了该方案的有效性并达到当前最优水平。

Abstract: Vision-Language Navigation in Continuous Environments (VLN-CE) requires an agent to follow natural language instructions through unseen environments. Existing imitation learning (IL) pipelines struggle in this closed-loop setting: behavior cloning suffers from distribution shift, and DAgger's expert actions become ambiguous upon trajectory deviation. While Reinforcement Learning (RL) offers a natural paradigm to address this, directly applying RL to micro action spaces is sample-inefficient due to reward sparsity. To overcome this bottleneck, we reformulate VLN-CE as a Hierarchical Markov Decision Process (MDP), explicitly decoupling high-level planning from low-level control. By abstracting the environment into a topological graph, our high-level policy operates on a macro action space of frontier nodes, with a training-free low-level controller acting as its state transition, which significantly compresses the decision horizon and makes closed-loop RL tractable. To support RL optimization on the macro MDP, we propose an action-aware value head to effectively evaluate state values under the dynamic frontier action space, powering a graph-based PPO. Extensive experiments demonstrate the effectiveness of our architecture. Finally, our model achieves state-of-the-art performance on the R2R-CE and RxR-CE benchmarks.

</details>


### [73] [Toward Unified Robot Learning: Bridging Representation, Vision-Language-Action, and World Models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03927)
*Shaunak A. Mehta,Ananya Hazarika,Haochen Zhang,Fan Yang,Ryo Moriyama,Wenkai Li,Yash Patel,Kanata Suzuki*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文是一篇关于机器人学习的综述论文，提出了一个统一的视角，将现有方法沿着三条互补的轴进行组织：通过表示学习进行理解、通过VLA模型进行行动、通过世界模型进行推理。论文构建了一个结构化分类体系，分析各组件之间的交互，并指出机器人学习领域的关键挑战及未来方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的机器人学习范式（表示学习、VLA模型、世界模型）通常是孤立发展的，导致系统碎片化，难以在泛化、长时程时间推理与规划、非结构化环境部署等方面取得突破。作者希望提供一个统一的视角来整合这些范式，促进更集成化的系统设计。

Method: 这是一篇综述论文，作者沿着"理解（表示学习）、行动（VLA模型）、推理（世界模型）"三条互补轴对现有方法进行组织，构建了涵盖环境表示、策略学习和预测建模等关键设计选择的结构化分类体系，系统总结了各领域的最新进展，并分析这些组件之间的交互关系。

Result: 论文识别出机器人学习领域的多个关键挑战，包括不确定性量化、分布外泛化、跨本体迁移、长上下文理解和长时程规划。作者论证这些挑战不仅源于各组件自身的局限，更源于感知、行动与推理之间缺乏集成。

Conclusion: 作者主张未来的机器人学习应朝着统一、物理落地且具有概率性建模能力的方向发展，以构建能保持一致内部表征并在真实世界长时间交互中支持决策的鲁棒机器人系统。

Abstract: For robots to operate reliably in real-world environments, they need to perceive their surroundings, act, and reason about the consequences of those actions. Rapid progress in the domains of representation learning, VLA models, and world models has significantly enhanced the capabilities of robot learning systems, enabling robots to work in increasingly complex environments. However, these paradigms are typically developed in isolation, resulting in fragmented systems that struggle with generalization, long-horizon temporal reasoning and planning, and deployment in unstructured environments. In this survey, we present a unified perspective on robot learning by organizing the existing methods along three complementary axes: understanding through representation learning, acting through VLA models, and reasoning through world models. We introduce a structured taxonomy that captures key design choices in environment representation, policy learning, and predictive modeling, and summarize the recent progress in these domains. Beyond classifying the existing works, we analyze how these components interact, discuss common limitations, and highlight emerging trends towards more integrated systems. Through this lens, we identify the challenges in the domain of robot learning, including uncertainty quantification, out-of-distribution generalization, cross-embodiment transfer, long-context understanding, and long-horizon planning. We argue that these challenges arise not only from limitations within individual components but also from the lack of integration across perception, action, and reasoning. Building on this analysis, we outline future directions towards unified, physically grounded, and probabilistic robot learning to develop robust robotic systems that maintain consistent internal representations and support decision making over extended interactions in real-world environments.

</details>


### [74] [Automated Weld Seam Recognition and 3D Mapping for Robotic Post Processing Using Photogrammetry and Semantic Segmentation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03970)
*Augustin Raju,Abilash Madavath,Chandra Yuvesh Aubeeluck,Nicolas Pyschny,Felix Hackelöer,Florian Zwanzig*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于视觉的焊缝近似定位流水线，作为高精度测量前的预步骤，通过多视角图像采集、语义分割识别焊缝、摄影测量重建工件，并将识别出的焊缝投影到重建模型中，以减少扫描工作量并提高数据采集效率。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 大型工件的完整表面扫描使用高精度激光扫描仪或结构光传感器耗时且会产生大量不相关的数据，因此需要一种高效的预定位方法，仅对焊缝区域进行高精度测量，减少整体扫描工作量。

Method: 该方法包括四个步骤：1) 从多个视角捕获工件图像；2) 使用语义分割从图像中识别焊缝；3) 利用摄影测量技术重建工件三维模型；4) 将识别出的焊缝投影到重建模型中，实现焊缝的近似定位。

Result: 该方法作为高精度测量前的预定位阶段，能够有效减少整体扫描努力并提高数据采集效率，适用于机器人打磨、精加工和检测等后处理操作中的焊缝定位。

Conclusion: 所提出的实验性视觉流水线可用于大型工件焊缝的快速近似定位，作为高精度测量前的引导步骤，从而提升自动化机器人后处理操作的整体效率。

Abstract: Accurate identification of weld seam geometries is essential for automated robotic post processing operations such as grinding, finishing, and inspection. For large workpieces, complete surface scanning using high precision laser scanners or structured light sensors can be time consuming and often generates substantial amount of data that are not relevant. This paper presents an experimental vision based pipeline for the approximate localization of weld seams. This serves as a preliminary stage before high precision measurement. The proposed approach aims to reduce the overall scanning effort and data acquisition efficiency. The proposed method includes capturing images of the workpiece from multiple viewpoints, identifying weld seams from the images using semantic segmentation, reconstructing the workpiece using photogrammetry, and projection of identified weld seams into the reconstructed model.

</details>


### [75] [MulDP: Multimodal Diffusion Policy for Autonomous Quadruped Parkour Navigation across Complex Terrains](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03984)
*Kangmai Hu,Yueqi Zhang,Peng Zhai,Xiaoyi Wei,Jiabin Hu,Zhixiang Liu,Quancheng Qian,Lihua Zhang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出多模态扩散策略（MulDP），将视觉感知、机器人本体感受和目标信息相结合，生成时间上连贯且具有前瞻性的导航速度指令，以实现四足机器人在复杂地形上的自主跑酷导航；同时构建了首个四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND），仿真与真实实验验证了方法的鲁棒性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 四足机器人虽已在跑酷运动上展现出敏捷性，但仍依赖人工进行高层规划，自主跑酷导航尚未被充分探索。核心挑战包括细粒度速度调节、长时域预测行为和感知与具身执行之间的紧密耦合。

Method: 提出多模态扩散策略（MulDP），融合视觉感知、机器人本体感受（proprioception）与目标信息，利用扩散模型生成时间上连贯且有前瞻性的导航速度指令，将感知与具身控制紧密耦合。同时构建四足跑酷导航数据集（QPND），一个包含多样化导航行为和复杂地形的多模态数据集，用于训练MulDP。

Result: 大量仿真与真实世界实验表明，MulDP能够实现鲁棒的长时间跨度的自主导航，并能有效穿越复杂地形。

Conclusion: MulDP通过多模态扩散策略有效解决了四足机器人自主跑酷导航中感知-控制耦合、长时域预测和速度调节等关键挑战，为四足机器人完全自主导航提供了可行方案。

Abstract: Quadruped robots have demonstrated impressive agility in parkour locomotion across complex terrains. However, most systems still rely on human intervention for high-level planning, and autonomous parkour navigation remains underexplored. The key challenges include fine-grained velocity regulation, long-horizon anticipatory behaviors, and tight coupling between perception and embodied execution. To address these challenges, we propose a Multimodal Diffusion Policy (MulDP) that integrates visual perception with robot proprioception and goal information to generate temporally coherent and anticipatory navigation velocity commands, tightly coupling perception with embodied control to enable robust autonomous navigation. To support the training of MulDP, we construct the first Quadruped Parkour Navigation Dataset (QPND), a multimodal dataset that encompasses diverse navigation behaviors and complex terrains. Extensive simulation and real-world experiments demonstrate that MulDP enables robust long-horizon autonomous navigation and effective traversal across complex terrains.

</details>


### [76] [Adaptive Vision-Language Grasping via Composable Foundation Priors and Generalizable Grasp Synthesis](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04096)
*Sixu Yan,Shikang Wang,Binhua Huang,Xuanlai Tang,Guohua Fan,Fan Huang,Haoxuan Li,Yongkang Li,Yuhan Li,Bencheng Liao,Zeyu Zhang,Wenyu Liu,Hangxin Liu,Xinggang Wang*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 提出AdaRoboVLG，一种任务自适应的视觉-语言-抓取框架，将物理可行的抓取合成与任务依赖的理解解耦，支持跨机器人手的泛化抓取，无需重新训练底层抓取策略。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有VLG方法将基础模型与端到端抓取策略紧密耦合，难以实现跨手泛化和任务自适应，且任务理解与物理抓取合成的耦合制约了可扩展性。

Method: AdaRoboVLG学习一个高效且可泛化的基础策略，通过显式运动学映射和力闭合稳定性估计生成和评估物理可行的抓取候选；同时将任务相关理解解耦到专门的通用模型模块，这些模块提供可组合的先验信息并集成到抓取合成过程中，实现上下文自适应的抓取合成而无需重新训练基础抓取策略。

Result: 仿真和真实实验表明：(i)基础策略具有高效学习和强跨手泛化能力；(ii)框架有效整合空间、认知和时间先验，解决三类代表性抓取挑战且不损害抓取合成性能；(iii)这些先验可联合运作，在杂乱和动态环境中实现功能性抓取。

Conclusion: 将物理抓取合成与任务依赖理解解耦是一种可扩展的机器人抓取范式，使基础模型的未来进展可直接转化为改善的抓取能力，而无需重新设计或重训底层抓取策略。

Abstract: This paper proposes AdaRoboVLG, a task-adaptive Vision-Language-Grasp (VLG) framework that supports generalizable grasp synthesis across different robotic hands. Unlike existing VLG methods that tightly couple foundation models with end-to-end grasp policies, AdaRoboVLG learns an efficient generalizable base policy that generates and evaluates physically feasible grasp candidates through explicit kinematic mapping and force-closure-based stability estimation, while offloading task-dependent understanding to specialized foundation-model modules. These modules provide composable priors that are integrated into the grasp synthesis process, enabling contextually adaptive grasp synthesis without retraining the underlying grasp policy. Through extensive simulation and real-world experiments, we demonstrate that (i) the base policy exhibits efficient learning and strong cross-hand generalization, (ii) the framework effectively incorporates spatial, cognitive, and temporal priors to address three representative grasping challenges without compromising grasp synthesis performance compared to state-of-the-art methods, and (iii) these priors can operate jointly to enable functional grasping in cluttered and dynamic environments. These results indicate that decoupling physical grasp synthesis from task-dependent understanding provides a scalable paradigm for robotic grasping, allowing future advances in foundation models to be directly translated into improved grasp capabilities without redesigning or retraining the underlying grasp policy. Supplementary videos are available at https://adarobovlg.github.io/

</details>


### [77] [Corner Cases: Headland Coverage Path Planning for Autonomous Driving in Arable Farming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04103)
*Riikka Soitinaho,Timo Oksanen*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出一种新的农田地头（headland）覆盖路径规划方法，通过修改多边形角点允许倒车转弯，从而实现对田间角落的完整覆盖。与两种已有方法相比，在约90度及240度以上的田间角落处，覆盖效果（间隙、重叠、越界）显著改善，并通过多个真实多边形农田地图验证了方法的适用性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的地头覆盖路径规划方法多采用嵌套多边形和光滑转弯策略，但这些方法在面对农田角落时无法实现完全覆盖，必须引入倒车机动才能覆盖所有角落区域。因此需要一种能够处理田间角落（尤其是约90度及240度以上角度）的新方法，以提升覆盖质量并减少间隙、重叠和越界问题。

Method: 该方法对多边形角点进行修改（corner modification），使得农业机械在田间角落处能够执行包含倒车的转弯机动，从而实现完整覆盖。方法通过在角点处设计专门的修改几何结构，使路径规划不再受限于纯前向行驶的转向约束，而是允许倒车动作来应对难以用光滑曲线转弯覆盖的角落区域。

Result: 与另外两种已有方法进行对比实验，评估指标包括间隙（gap）、重叠（overlap）和跨越田间边界（crossing the field boundary）。结果显示，新方法在约90度角以及240度及以上的田间角落处，覆盖结果具有明显改善。此外，通过在多个真实多边形农田地图上的测试验证了方法的实际适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究提出的改进地头覆盖路径规划方法，通过允许倒车转弯有效解决了传统方法难以完全覆盖田间角落的问题，特别在约90度及240度以上的角区域表现优异，并在真实农田场景中具备良好的适用性和推广潜力。

Abstract: This paper presents a new method for headland coverage path planning for arable fields. Several earlier approaches suggest covering the headland with nested polygons and smooth turns, however, covering the field corners entirely requires manoeuvres with reversing. In the new method, the polygon corners are modified to allow a reversing turn. A comparison to two other methods considering gap, overlap, and crossing the field boundary shows an improvement in the coverage result especially in field corners of around 90 degrees, and 240 degrees and above. Applicability of the new method is shown with several examples of real polygonal field maps.

</details>


### [78] [GIFT: Guided Intermediate Feature Training via Action-Oriented Structural Supervision for Robotic Manipulation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04193)
*Yupeng Zheng,Xiang Li,Songen Gu,Yuhang Zheng,Shuai Tian,Weize Li,Linbo Wang,Chaoyue Li,Qichao Zhang,Haoran Li,Zhongpu Xia,Ya-Qin Zhang,Shuicheng Yan,Dongbin Zhao*

Main category: cs.RO

TL;DR: 本文提出GIFT框架，通过引导中间特征保持几何、可负担性和目标三种控制相关结构，弥合视觉预训练表征与机器人控制效用之间的"动作充分性差距"。该框架可灵活应用于VLA、直接动作世界模型和逆动力学世界模型，无需改变各自动作表达，在LIBERO-Plus和RoboCasa上均显著优于基线。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 视觉-语言预训练与世界模型预测虽然为机器人策略提供了丰富的语义和动态视觉特征，但其原生目标和视觉预测损失可能遗漏关键的物理与任务结构，同时保留与控制无关的视觉冗余。这种视觉丰富性与控制效用之间的不匹配（称为动作充分性差距）阻碍了下游操纵任务的策略学习。

Method: 提出GIFT（Guided Intermediate Feature Training）框架，一种架构灵活的中间特征学习方法。通过三种训练约束将控制相关结构注入中间特征：(1) 几何对齐，编码控制流动可行性的几何结构；(2) 可负担性预测，编码与指令相关的实体；(3) 目标区域重建，将指令定基于任务相关区域。GIFT被实例化在VLA策略、直接动作的世界动作模型（WAM）和逆动力学世界动作模型（WAM-IDM）中，同时保留各自的动作表达形式。

Result: 在零样本迁移至LIBERO-Plus上，GIFT-VLA、GIFT-WAM-Fast和GIFT-WAM-IDM分别达到79.6%、72.6%和87.8%，较StarVLA-OFT、Fast-WAM和Fast-WAM-IDM提升4.6、12.6和5.2个百分点。在RoboCasa上，三种GIFT变体分别达到61.4%、83.6%和82.3%，较对应基线提升12.6、9.0和8.4个百分点。

Conclusion: 实验结果证明，学习功能上有结构的中间特征是一种跨模型特定动作表达的可复用原则，尤其在铰接物体任务及面临未见视觉和空间扰动的高精度真实世界操纵中带来显著收益。这为桥接视觉预训练与机器人控制的"动作充分性差距"提供了有效的通用解决方案。

Abstract: Vision-language pre-training and predictive world modeling provide robot policies with rich semantic and dynamic visual features, but their native action and visual-prediction objectives may omit critical physical and task structure while retaining control-irrelevant visual redundancy. We call this mismatch between visual richness and control utility the action-sufficiency gap. We investigate whether this gap can be bridged by guiding intermediate features to preserve three control-relevant structure in robotic manipulation: geometry governing motion feasibility, affordance encoding instruction-relevant entities, and goals grounding instructions in task-relevant regions. To this end, we present GIFT (Guided Intermediate Feature Training), an architecture-flexible framework for learning intermediate features that translates these structures into training-time constraints through geometry alignment, affordance prediction, and goal-region reconstruction. We instantiate GIFT in a Vision-Language-Action (VLA) policy, a direct-action World-Action Model (WAM), and an inverse-dynamics WAM while retaining each model's action formulation. Under zero-shot transfer to LIBERO-Plus, GIFT-VLA, GIFT-WAM-Fast, and GIFT-WAM-IDM outperform StarVLA-OFT, Fast-WAM, and Fast-WAM-IDM by 4.6, 12.6, and 5.2 points, reaching 79.6%, 72.6%, and 87.8%, respectively. On RoboCasa, the three GIFT variants reach 61.4%, 83.6%, and 82.3%, outperforming their counterparts by 12.6, 9.0, and 8.4 points, respectively. Together, these results establish learning functionally structured intermediate features as a reusable principle across model-specific action formulations, with especially large gains on articulated-object tasks and high-precision real-world manipulation under unseen visual and spatial perturbations. Project page: https://openphoenix-team.github.io/GIFT-pages.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自Kang-Kashiwara-Kim-Oh与Hernandez-Leclerc以来，量子簇代数与量子Grothendieck环的关联为量子仿射代数的表示理论提供了重要工具。此前辫簇X(β)的量子簇代数结构及其与表示论范畴的等价关系尚未被完整建立，特别是局部化level≥1子代数的量子形变性质与实际Lusztig参数的给定缺乏系统性构造。本文旨在填补这一空白，从Q数据构建完整的范畴化理论，并验证量子形变与经典坐标环的平坦性关系。

Method: 利用Q数据定义完整强对偶datum，构造辫簇X(β)的量子簇代数；将该代数同时嵌入局部化的量子Grothendieck环（基于量子仿射代数的实单模）与玻色子扩展代数的level≥1子代数，从而获得基于幺半范畴化；通过构造q^{1/2}=1处的特化同态检验整形式的平坦性；利用双字符串表示引入内蕴Lusztig参数，识别量子网格小行列式和广义量子T-系的模实现。

Result: 成功构造了辫簇对应的量子簇代数的完整基于幺半范畴化；证明了该代数与局部化量子Grothendieck环及玻色子扩展代数level≥1子代数的等价；验证了特化同态恢复经典坐标环且整形式构成平坦量子形变；给出了双字符串型簇变量的内蕴参数与对应的量子网格小行列式及广义量子T-系的精确表达式。

Conclusion: 该工作将辫簇X(β)的量子簇代数、量子仿射代数的实单模表示、整体基与经典坐标环的量子形变四者统一在基于幺半范畴化的框架之下，为量子簇代数理论与几何表示论之间提供了新的桥梁。

Abstract: Let \(G\) be a simple and simply connected algebraic group of simply-laced type. For each positive braid word \(β\), and for the complete strong duality datum attached to a \(Q\)-datum, we construct an explicit based monoidal categorification of the quantum cluster algebra of the braid variety \(X(β)\). We identify this algebra with both a localized quantum Grothendieck ring and a localized level-\(\geq1\) subalgebra of the bosonic extension algebra. Under these identifications, quantum cluster monomials correspond simultaneously to real simple modules and normalized global basis elements. We construct the specialization homomorphism at \(q^{1/2}=1\) and prove that it recovers \(\CC[X(β)]\); in particular, the resulting integral form is a flat quantum deformation. We also give intrinsic Lusztig parameters for cluster variables attached to double strings, identify the corresponding quantum grid minors, and establish generalized quantum \(T\)-systems.

Conclusion: 该工作建立了离散子空间上函数延拓与李群表示论中相干态及再生核理论之间的桥梁，为对称空间上的插值与延拓问题提供了新的构造性方法。

Abstract: For a given function $f$ on a discrete subspace $Γ$ of a noncompact Riemannian symmetric space $G/K$, we construct analytic, noncompactly supported functions $W$ on $G/K$ with $W|_Γ=f$ that, in addition, satisfy an invariant differential equation on $G/K$. The key step is to regard $W$ as an orthogonal projection onto a space of coherent states in a representation of the Lie group $G$ in a Hilbert space that admits a strictly positive definite reproducing kernel. The techniques are implemented for the heat kernel on $G/K$, and $W$ is expressed in terms of certain closed formulas for linearly independent coherent states.

</details>


### [81] [What is Smoothness?](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03246)
*Zachary P Bradshaw*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文提出了在非交换群上定义函数光滑度的一般框架：通过对称生成集S的Cayley图拉普拉斯算子的分块特征值均值来对不可约表示排序，从而定义Fourier系数的低频集中度作为光滑性度量。研究表明这种排序函数ω(G,S)满足自然公理时具有确定的自由度，其诱导的序恰好是共轭对上取相同值且平凡表示取零的实函数，且由共轭类的反转轨道生成的序构成其基。进一步引入类权重非负和均匀增量声明两个约束后，算子被唯一确定为Cayley-Laplacian（正尺度意义下）。该理论可推广至紧群和带传递群作用的有限集。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 实轴上函数的光滑性可以由Fourier变换的衰减来刻画，这暗示L²(G)中函数的光滑性应体现为Fourier系数在低频处集中。但对非交换群G，不可约表示没有规范的全序，因此需要给出一套基于群结构和生成集的排序方案，从而将"光滑性"从函数的固有属性扩展为函数+群+生成集共同决定的相对属性。

Method: 给定对称生成集S，构造Cayley图的拉普拉斯算子，该算子在群对偶上分块对角化；对每个块取特征值均值作为排序函数ω:Ĝ→R，其值域在[0,2]之间。作者随后从公理化角度刻画该构造的自由度：先证明满足自然公理的算子所诱导的排序恰为在平凡表示取零、在共轭对上取值一致的实函数，且共轭类反转轨道生成的排序构成线性基；再通过引入非负类权重和"均匀增量"声明两个附加条件，将排序算子锁定为Cayley-Laplacian（仅差正比例因子）。该框架进一步推广到紧群和带传递群作用的有限集。

Result: (1) 定义了依赖于(G,S)的排序函数ω:Ĝ→R，其值在[0,2]中，仅在平凡表示处取零，达到上界2当且仅当Cayley图是二分图；(2) 在满足自然公理的算子类中，诱导排序的空间被完整刻画，且共轭类反转轨道的排序构成一组基；(3) 添加非负类权重和均匀增量声明两个条件后，算子在正尺度意义下唯一确定为Cayley-Laplacian；(4) 该框架自然延续至紧群（尽管Cayley图不存在）以及等变有限集（传递群作用），说明该方法具有广泛的普适性。

Conclusion: 标题问题的答案是：光滑性不是一个函数自身的固有属性，而是一个函数与所选群和生成集共同定义的相对属性。该结论为谐波分析与几何分析中光滑性的概念提供了非交换群和更一般紧群框架下的统一理解。

Abstract: Smoothness of a function on the real line is reflected in the decay of its Fourier transform, which suggests that smoothness of a function in $L^2(G)$ for a group $G$ should mean concentration of the Fourier coefficients at low frequency. Such a reading presupposes an ordering of the irreducible representations of $G$, but for non-abelian $G$, no ordering is canonical. Given a symmetric generating set $S$, the Laplacian of the associated Cayley graph is block diagonal over the dual, and we order the irreps by the mean of the eigenvalues in each block. This produces an ordering function $ω:\widehat{G}\to\mathbb{R}$ that depends only on the pair $(G,S)$. This function is bounded between zero and two, vanishing only at the trivial representation and achieving the upper bound exactly when the Cayley graph is bipartite. We then ask how much freedom the construction has. Within the class of operators satisfying natural axioms, the induced orderings are exactly the real functions on the dual vanishing at the trivial representation and agreeing on conjugate pairs, and the orderings coming from inversion orbits of conjugacy classes form a basis for them. We cut the freedom down further by requiring two additional inputs: nonnegativity of the class weights and a declaration of which group elements count as uniform incremental changes, which pins the operator to the Cayley-Laplacian up to positive scale. We observe that the construction persists for compact groups even though the Cayley graph does not, and we extend the theory to finite sets carrying a transitive group action, where the acting group selects which frequencies exist and the generating set orders them. The answer to the title question is therefore that smoothness is a property of a function together with a choice of group and generating set, not of the function alone.

</details>

Method: 通过修改最终同调同构的定义，引入$n$-伴随型奇异等价（$n\ge2$）；利用双模的$\otimes$-(余)完美复形给出特征化；借助对偶代数与张量积代数构造实例；通过代数同态和幂等元构造例子；并建立标准最终同调同构与奇异范畴上带典范左伴随的忠实满全真三角函子之间的一一对应，证明本质满射的标准最终同调同构对应3-伴随型奇异等价。

Conclusion: 通过引入$n$-伴随型奇异等价和修正最终同调同构，建立了统一的框架，使得本质满射的标准最终同调同构可以被纳入3-伴随型奇异等价的框架之中，从而为同调猜想的约化与同调性质的保持提供了更系统的工具。

Abstract: We modify the definition of eventually homological isomorphisms so that it becomes more natural and fruitful. We introduce singular equivalences of $n$-adjoint type which can be viewed as standard singular equivalences of different levels and are more convenient for reducing various homological conjectures and transferring homological properties. We develop the theories of singular equivalences of $n$-adjoint type for $n\ge 2$ and standard eventually homological isomorphisms between derived categories by giving their characterizations in terms of $\otimes$-(co)perfect complexes of bimodules, two constructions via opposite algebras and tensor product algebras, examples induced by homomorphisms of algebras and idempotents, and applications to the reduction of homological conjectures such as the finitistic dimension conjecture, Gorenstein symmetry conjecture, Auslander-Reiten conjecture and Gorenstein projective conjecture, as well as to the preservation of homological properties such as Gorensteinness, the Fg condition, Happel's property and Han's property. Moreover, we show that standard eventually homological isomorphisms correspond to fully faithful standard triangle functors between singularity categories with canonical left adjoint, and essentially surjective standard eventually homological isomorphisms between derived categories correspond to singular equivalences of 3-adjoint type, thereby incorporating essentially surjective standard eventually homological isomorphisms into the framework of singular equivalences of 3-adjoint type.

</details>

  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Hovey三元组是同伦代数与表示论中的重要概念，其与三角范畴的结构密切相关。此前对Hovey三元组的分类主要关注遗传情形，而例外Hovey三元组（即(C∩F, C∩F∩W)不构成Frobenius对的情形）在自内射Nakayama代数等例子中广泛存在，其同伦范畴展现出新的现象，需要系统研究。

Method: 文章首先给出Hovey三元组的三种分类（遗传、例外、非遗传非例外），并通过自内射Nakayama代数kC_n/J^t上的模范畴作为例证说明例外Hovey三元组的存在性条件。作者利用Nakaoka-Palu定理给出的三角化结构，证明例外性等价于诱导的扩张三角结构不是典范三角化的；进一步通过函子范畴fp((P(C∩F)/(C∩F∩W))^op, Ab)的Serre子范畴来刻画介于扩张三角结构和三角化结构之间的中间扩张三角结构。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 对自内射Nakayama代数A=kC_n/J^t，其有限生成模范畴存在例外Hovey三元组当且仅当gcd(n,t)≥2且t≥3；(2) 在弱幂等完备的扩张三角范畴中，Hovey三元组是例外的当且仅当诱导的扩张三角结构(C∩F)/(C∩F∩W)不是典范三角化的；(3) 中间扩张三角结构与函子范畴fp((P(C∩F)/(C∩F∩W))^op, Ab)的Serre子范畴之间存在一一对应。

Conclusion: 本文建立了例外Hovey三元组的判定准则，揭示了其与"非典范三角化"之间的联系，并将中间结构的分类归结为函子范畴中Serre子范畴的分类。这为理解Hovey三元组的整体分类结构以及三角范畴与扩张三角范畴之间的关系提供了新的视角和方法。

Abstract: A Hovey triple $(\mathcal{C}, \mathcal{F}, \mathcal{W})$ is {\it exceptional}, if $(\mathcal{C} \cap \mathcal{F}, \ \mathcal{C} \cap \mathcal{F} \cap \mathcal{W})$ is not a Frobenius pair. One has a disjoint union $\{\text{Hovey triple}\} = \{\text{Hereditary Hovey triple}\} \ \dot\bigcup \ \{\text{Exceptional Hovey triple}\}$ \ $\dot\bigcup \ \{\text{non-hereditary and non-exceptional Hovey triple}\}.$ Exceptional Hovey triples appear widely. For a selfinjective Nakayama algebra $A= kC_n/J^t$, $A\mbox{-}{\rm mod}$ admits exceptional Hovey triples if and only if $\gcd (n, t) \ge 2$ and $t \geq 3$. Their homotopy categories reveal new phenomena. Nakaoka-Palu's Theorem implies that there is a triangulation on $\frac{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F}{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F\cap\mathcal W}.$ It is proved that a Hovey triple in a weakly idempotent complete extriangulated category $(\mathcal A, \mathbb E, \mathfrak s)$ is exceptional if and only if the induced extriangulated structure $\bigl(\frac{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F}{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F\cap\mathcal W}, \ \overline{\mathbb E}, \ \overline{\mathfrak s}\bigr)$ is not {\it canonically triangulated}. Between this extriangulated structure and the one arising from the triangulation, the intermediate extriangulated structures are in one-to-one correspondence with Serre subcategories of $\mathrm{fp}((\frac{\mathcal P(\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F)}{\mathcal C\cap\mathcal F\cap\mathcal W})^{\mathrm{op}}, \mathrm{Ab})$.

</details>


### [85] [Jantzen filtrations for graded affine Hecke algebras](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03651)
*Dan Ciubotaru*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了等参数分次仿射Hecke代数标准模的Jantzen猜想，适用于实中心特征标且沿主控形变方向的情况。Jantzen层被证明是半单的，其重数由对应轨道闭包的全局部交点上同调给出。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Jantzen猜想是表示论中长期悬而未决的核心问题，它将代数层的Jantzen滤过与几何对象的交点上同调联系起来。此前该猜想在一般情形下尚未被证明，本文旨在填补这一空缺。

Method: 证明的关键步骤包括：(1) 将代数层面的标准模到逆步模的映射与在收缩横截切片上的余茎到茎映射等同起来；(2) 利用Hard Lefschetz定理确定其初等因子（即Jantzen层的重数）；(3) 利用特化卷积代数上的残差分次性证明Jantzen层的半单性。文中还给出type A的反例说明主控条件（dominance）的必要性。

Result: 成功证明了几何领域中等参数分次仿射Hecke代数标准模的Jantzen猜想，具体条件为实中心特征标和主控形变方向。证明了Jantzen层半单性，并将其重数由轨道闭包的局部交点上同调显式给出。

Conclusion: 该结果建立了几何表示论中代数结构与几何结构之间的深刻联系，并揭示了Hard Lefschetz定理在理解Jantzen滤过结构中的核心作用。type A的反例表明主控条件是必要的，不能轻易放宽。

Abstract: We prove the Jantzen conjecture for standard modules of graded affine Hecke algebras with equal parameters, real central character in the dominant deformation direction. The Jantzen layers are shown to be semisimple, and the multiplicities are given by the full local intersection cohomology of the corresponding orbit closures. The proof identifies the algebraic standard-to-contragredient map with the costalk-to-stalk map on a contracting transverse slice. Hard Lefschetz theorem determines its elementary divisors, and a residual grading on the specialized convolution algebra proves semisimplicity. An example in type $A$ is included to show that dominance is necessary.

</details>

<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Granville和Ono证明了：当且仅当e≥4时，每个正整数n都存在一个n的e-核分拆。这一结果对应对称群在正特征下的亏数0块以及有限一般线性群在正非定义特征下的亏数0单胞块的存性。作者希望将此结果推广到有限经典群的块理论、非本原spetses和分圆Hecke代数等更广泛的背景下，因此需要研究带电荷多分拆的不同e-核变体的类似正性结果。

Method: 作者针对带电荷多分拆（charged multipartitions），证明关于其不同变体e-核的正性存在性命题。这些e-核变体对应于上述代数对象的表示论参数，通过分析这些变体核的存在条件，建立与Granville-Ono定理类似的充要性结果。

Result: 证明了带电荷多分拆的各种e-核变体满足类似的正性存在性语句，即对特定参数范围保证核的存在性，从而为有限经典群、非本原spetses和分圆Hecke代数的亏数0块的存在性提供了理论基础。

Conclusion: 本文的结果扩展了e-核分拆存在性理论，将其推广到带电荷多分拆的多类e-核变体情形，为块理论中的亏数0问题提供了更广泛的理论工具。

Abstract: Granville and Ono proved that there is an $e$-core partition of $n$ for every $n\in\mathbb{N}$ if and only if $e\geq 4$, which translates to a statement about the existence of defect $0$ blocks for symmetric groups in positive characteristic and defect $0$ unipotent blocks of finite general linear groups in positive, non-defining characteristic. Motivated by analogous applications to the block theory of finite classical groups, imprimitive spetses, and cyclotomic Hecke algebras, we prove similar positivity statements about different variants of $e$-cores for charged multipartitions.

</details>


### [87] [Universal Plücker positivity and the Octopus inequality](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03746)
*Haoran Zhu*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文证明了Karp和Purbhoo普遍Plücker坐标在单参数为负时的正性定理，其尖锐均匀阈值由最大Plancherel上跃迁概率控制；在临界特化下，(2,2)-坐标的正性与Octopus不等式等价，并通过Schubert calculus将其与Aldous谱隙猜想联系，同时给出了极大公共半线定理及两条超图不等式的新证明。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Karp和Purbhoo提出的普遍Plücker坐标的正性问题是理解Aldous谱隙猜想的现代代数途径之一。作者旨在回答Caputo和Aldous提出的开放问题，将Octopus不等式（Aldous谱隙猜想证明的核心工具）与Plücker坐标正性建立精确联系，并希望通过Schubert calculus提供新的视角和证明工具。

Method: 本文采用Plücker坐标理论结合Schubert calculus（舒伯特演算）的方法。核心策略包括：分析普遍Plücker坐标在单参数为负时的符号结构，利用Plancherel上跃迁概率刻画正性的最优阈值；通过临界特化将(2,2)-坐标正性与Octopus不等式建立等价；对具有不同实零点的Wronskian，研究所有分支平衡坐标的正半定性条件以确定极大公共半线；最后运用Plücker理论为Alon-Kozma-Puder的超图不等式提供新证明。

Result: 主要结果包括：(1) 普遍Plücker坐标单参数负的情形下的锐利正性定理；(2) 在临界specialization处，(2,2)-坐标正性与Octopus不等式的精确等价性，并通过Schubert calculus回答了Caputo和Aldous的问题；(3) 对Wronskian确定了极大公共半线的解析刻画；(4) 给出了两条超图不等式（属于Alon, Kozma和Puder）的纯Plücker理论证明。

Conclusion: 本文建立的Plücker坐标正性与Octopus不等式之间的桥梁表明，谱隙猜想的技术细节可以通过组合代数（Plücker理论）获得更深层次的理解，同时Schubert calculus为此提供了有效的工具支撑，并为相关问题（如超图不等式）提供了统一的方法论。

Abstract: We prove a positivity theorem for the universal Plücker coordinates of Karp and Purbhoo when exactly one parameter is negative, with a sharp uniform threshold governed by the largest Plancherel up-transition probability. At the critical specialisation, positivity of the normalised $(2,2)$-coordinate is equivalent to the Octopus inequality, the main technical tool in the proof of Aldous's spectral gap conjecture. By connecting the inequality with Schubert calculus, this answers questions raised by Caputo and Aldous. For a Wronskian with distinct real zeros, we determine the maximal common half-line on which all branching-balanced coordinates are positive semidefinite. We also give Plücker-theoretic proofs of two hypergraph inequalities of Alon, Kozma, and Puder.

</details>


### [88] [Additive diameters and covering complexity of irreducible representations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03882)
*Urban Jezernik,Špela Špenko*

Main category: math.RT

TL;DR: 本文研究紧致线性群G作用于不可约复向量空间V时，子空间的"群加直径"紧界。证明任意非零子空间的直径至多为⌈(dim V/dim U)·ln dim V⌉（即平凡下界乘一个对数因子），并引入覆盖复杂度C(V)量化偏差，系统刻画了各类表示（SL_2、SL_n对称/外幂、有限Heisenberg群、PSL_2(F_p)、共轭表示、SL_3的Sym^k C^3及李代数sl_3情形）在这个范围内的取值位置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的维度计数仅给出子空间和覆盖数目的平凡下界dim V/dim U，但该下界是否最优、偏差多少一直不清楚。作者希望系统度量"对数因子"的必然性，通过定义覆盖复杂度C(V)来回答trivial bound在多大程度上精确，并判定各类具体表示落在该范围的哪一段，从而揭示不同类型的表示在覆盖问题上的本质差异。

Method: 通过定义群加直径（最少平移数使U的和覆盖全空间）与覆盖复杂度C(V)（直径与平凡下界的最大比率），证明紧致不可约情形下整体上界C(V)≤2+ln dim V。针对不同表示类别分别建立复杂性估计：对SL_2(C)的不可约表示逐类验证C(V)=1；沿SL_n的对称/外幂族及小阶2-传递群（如PSL_2(F_p)）、有限Heisenberg群构造下界以表明对数因子确会出现；并对比李代数sl_3(C)的单项式直径与群直径的差别以显示二者行为的分离。

Result: 1) 对所有紧致G与不可约V，任意非零子空间直径≤⌈(dim V/dim U)ln dim V⌉；2) 1≤C(V)≤2+ln dim V；3) SL_2(C)所有不可约表示C(V)=1；4) 在SL_n对称/外幂族（n变化）、有限Heisenberg群、PSL_2(F_p)等2-传递小群中，对数因子真实存在；5) SL_n共轭表示与SL_3的Sym^k C^3上C(V)有常界；6) 任一固定连通约化群存在复杂性至少趋于旗簇维数的不可约表示族；7) 对sl_3(C)作用于Sym^k C^3，关于Sym^k X（平面X）的单项式直径最优，而对应SL_3(C)群直径不最优。

Conclusion: 紧致不可约线性群的子空间加性覆盖问题中，平凡下界在对数因子内是精确的，但该对数是否真正出现取决于具体表示结构：某些表示（如SL_2全部不可约表示、SL_3的对称幂、SL_n共轭表示）可实现常复杂度的精确性，而另一族表示则展示出与旗簇维度相关的内在对数损失，说明复杂性本质上编码了表示的几何结构。同时群作用与李代数作用在单项式直径上的最优性差异表明，离散平移与单参数子空间逼近之间存在结构性张力。

Abstract: Let a group $G$ act linearly on a finite-dimensional complex vector space $V$. The group-additive diameter of a subspace $U \leq V$ is the least number of translates of $U$ whose sum is all of $V$. Counting dimensions, it is at least $\dim V / \dim U$. We show that when $G$ is compact and $V$ is irreducible, the diameter of every nonzero subspace is at most $\lceil (\dim V / \dim U) \ln \dim V \rceil$, so the trivial bound is correct up to a logarithmic factor. We measure the discrepancy by the covering complexity $\mathsf{C}(V)$, the largest ratio between the diameter of any subspace and its trivial lower bound, so that $1 \leq \mathsf{C}(V) \leq 2 + \ln \dim V$, and we determine where in this range various representations lie. Every irreducible representation of $\mathrm{SL}_2(\mathbf{C})$ has $\mathsf{C}(V) = 1$. The logarithm can be genuinely present along families of symmetric and exterior powers of $\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbf{C})$ with $n$ varying, and it is present for finite Heisenberg groups and $2$-transitive groups of small order such as $\mathrm{PSL}_2(\mathbf{F}_p)$. The complexity is bounded above by a constant on the conjugation representations of $\mathrm{SL}_n(\mathbf{C})$ and on the representations $\operatorname{Sym}^k \mathbf{C}^3$ of $\mathrm{SL}_3(\mathbf{C})$. On the other hand, every fixed connected reductive group has a family of irreducible representations whose complexities tend to at least the dimension of the flag variety. Finally, for the Lie algebra $\mathfrak{sl}_3(\mathbf{C})$ acting on $\operatorname{Sym}^k \mathbf{C}^3$, the monomial diameter with respect to $\operatorname{Sym}^k X$ for a plane $X \leq \mathbf{C}^3$ is optimal, while the corresponding $\mathrm{SL}_3(\mathbf{C})$ diameter is not.

</details>

Method: 论文采用纯代数/表示论的方法，对Cartan表示建立同构判定条件，系统分析这些条件在特征零域与有限特征域上的差异；同时给出Coxeter群为晶体群的精确定义与判别条件，并辅以具体示例加以说明。

Result: 给出了Cartan表示彼此同构、与反射表示同构及与对偶表示同构的充要条件；在有限特征域上获得了部分结果；澄清了晶体群概念的精确含义，并建立了Kac-Moody代数Weyl群为晶体群的判别条件。

Conclusion: 本文系统地回答了Cartan表示的同构分类问题，弥合了表示论中关于这些表示之间关系的一些空白，并在一般域（包括有限特征）上给出了部分推广，为后续研究提供了理论基础。

Abstract: This dissertation studies Cartan representations: representations of certain Coxeter groups as the Weyl groups of Kac-Moody algebras. It sets out and proves conditions for these representations to be isomorphic to each other, the reflection representation of the Coxeter group, and their dual representations, and gives partial results for the same over fields of finite characteristic, as well as clarifying what it means for a Coxeter group to be crystallographic and giving conditions for the Weyl group of a Kac-Moody algebra to be such. Some illustrative examples are also provided.

</details>

### [91] [Structural Compatibility and Uniform Stability of Temporally Degenerate Parabolic Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03136)
*Amadou Cissé*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了时间退化抛物系统的反馈设计问题，提出了一种操作员兼容性条件来刻画可容许的反馈互连，将退化演化方程正则化为标准演化方程，并通过临界-残差分解建立一致指数稳定性证书，最终实现构造性的静态输出反馈综合。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间退化抛物系统的现有反馈设计假设闭环动力学构成良好定义的演化问题，但时间退化不仅影响演化方程的分析性质，还使反馈互连本身的数学表述变得非平凡。因此，需要系统性地刻画哪些反馈互连是可容许的，并建立相应的稳定性分析方法。

Method: 首先引入一个操作员兼容性条件，将奇异反应算子与执行器和观测器算子联系起来，从而完全刻画可容许的反馈互连。该条件可消除闭环动力学中的奇异分量，将退化演化方程约简为正则演化方程。随后基于正则化表述，通过临界-残差分解获得一致指数稳定性证书，并将其通过有限到无限提升定理延伸到原始无穷维演化。最后推导出构造性的静态输出反馈综合方法。

Result: 数值实验验证了正则化机制的有效性，证实了稳定性证书的正确性，并确认了有限到无限提升定理的适用性。推导出的静态输出反馈合成算法具有构造性，可直接用于实际设计。

Conclusion: 时间退化抛物系统的可容许反馈互连由奇异反应算子与执行器和观测器算子之间的兼容性条件完全特征化，该框架提供了统一的稳定性分析与反馈综合途径，为退化分布参数系统控制提供了新的理论工具。

Abstract: Modern feedback design for distributed parameter systems presupposes that the closed-loop dynamics define a well-posed evolution problem. This presupposition becomes nontrivial for temporally degenerate parabolic systems, where temporal degeneracy affects not only the analytical properties of the evolution equation but also the mathematical formulation of the feedback interconnection itself. It is shown that admissible feedback interconnections for temporally degenerate parabolic systems are completely characterized by an operator compatibility condition linking the singular reaction operator with the actuator and observation operators. This characterization removes the singular component of the closed-loop dynamics and reduces the degenerate evolution equation to a regular evolution equation. Building upon this regularized formulation, a critical--residual decomposition yields a uniform exponential stability certificate, which is subsequently extended to the original infinite-dimensional evolution through a finite-to-infinite lifting theorem. A constructive static output feedback synthesis is finally obtained as a consequence of these results. Numerical experiments illustrate the regularization mechanism, validate the stability certificate, and confirm the finite-to-infinite lifting.

</details>


### [92] [Gradient-Free Optimization for Matrix functions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03170)
*Sawyer Allen,Cash Cherry,Aidan Eck,Stephen Becker,Daniel McKenzie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究仅通过方向导数（而非完整梯度）优化矩阵变量的平滑（可能非凸）函数的问题，该场景出现在消费级硬件上微调大型神经网络时。作者将梯度估计框架化为结构化恢复问题，提出了基于伪逆的随机梯度估计器替代方案，并利用矩阵感知技术在高斯-低秩梯度条件下构建高精度梯度估计器，并与谱下降等矩阵感知优化器结合摊销计算成本。实验表明该方法收敛更快。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在消费级硬件上微调大型神经网络时，权重为矩阵形式，内存限制无法使用反向模式自动微分，但前向模式的向导师数仍然可得。因此需要一种仅依赖方向导数的高效梯度估计方法，以支持一阶优化算法的运行。

Method: 本文提出三个贡献：(1) 引入一种替代标准随机梯度估计器的新方案，关键区别在于将采样算子的伴随替换为其伪逆；(2) 当梯度满足近似低秩条件时，借用矩阵感知技术构建一族高精度梯度估计器，可即插即用到任何一阶方法；(3) 将该估计器与矩阵感知优化器（如谱下降）结合，由于梯度估计器已计算出梯度的因子分解，谱下降的投影步骤无需额外成本，从而实现计算开销的摊销。

Result: 在两个具有近似低秩梯度的合成函数上进行了数值实验，表明利用梯度低秩性质可以获得比标准方法更快的收敛速度，并能获得更好的近似解。

Conclusion: 利用矩阵梯度的近似低秩结构，可以在仅依赖方向导数的约束下实现高效的梯度估计，并显著加速一阶优化方法的收敛。该框架为大规模神经网络在受限硬件上的微调提供了新的理论视角和实用方案。

Abstract: We consider the task of optimizing smooth, possibly non-convex functions of a matrix variable given access only to directional derivatives rather than full gradients. This setting arises when fine-tuning large neural networks on consumer-grade hardware: the network's weights are matrices, memory constraints rule out backward-mode automatic differentiation, but directional derivatives remain available through forward mode.
  We frame gradient estimation in this setting as a structured recovery problem, in the spirit of signal processing. From this perspective we provide three contributions. First, we introduce an alternative to the standard random gradient estimator; the difference corresponds to replacing the adjoint of the sampling operator with its pseudoinverse. Second, when the gradient satisfies an approximate low-rank condition, techniques from matrix sensing yield a family of highly accurate gradient estimators that drop into any first-order method. Third, we note that while the computational cost of such estimators is high, this can be amortized by combining them with a matrix-aware optimizer such as spectral descent. Specifically, the gradient estimator computes a factorization of the gradient, allowing for the projection step of spectral descent to be done at no extra cost.
  We demonstrate our findings with two careful numerical experiments on synthetic functions with approximately low-rank gradients. We show that by exploiting this low-rank property one obtains much faster convergence to good approximate solutions.

</details>


### [93] [An Exact Combinatorial Branch-and-Bound Algorithm for the Job Sequencing and Tool Switching Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03219)
*Alberto Locatelli,Jean-François Côté,Leandro C. Coelho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种名为组合分支定界（C-B&B）的精确算法用于求解作业排序与刀具切换问题（SSP），该算法结合两种各具创新特点的分支定界算法，辅以预处理和启发式初始解，在计算实验中大幅提升了可证明最优解的实例数量并显著降低了计算开销，解决了一些尘封数十年的基准实例。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: SSP是柔性制造中的经典组合优化问题，尽管已有大量精确和启发式方法，但数十年前提出的仅含20个作业的基准实例至今仍未被证明最优解，这构成了显著的算法性能鸿沟，亟需更强的精确求解方法。

Method: 提出C-B&B算法，融合两种分支定界算法：(1) 第一种采用新型分支方案以缩减隐式枚举树规模，并配备系列新定界函数；(2) 第二种基于Laporte等人（2004）的分支方案，增强新的定界函数和两条支配规则。此外，增加预处理阶段，其中包含基于分支定界的启发式方法以快速生成高质量初始可行解。

Result: 大量计算实验表明，C-B&B相对已有方法取得重大突破：在显著更少的计算开销下证明了更多实例的最优性，并解决了多个数十年来保持未解状态的基准实例。

Conclusion: C-B&B算法代表了SSP精确求解的重要进展，通过创新的分支策略、定界函数与支配规则的有效组合，成功缩小了理论与计算实践之间的差距。

Abstract: The Job Sequencing and Tool Switching Problem (SSP) is a well-known combinatorial optimization problem arising in the context of flexible manufacturing. Since the seminal work of Tang and Denardo (1988), the SSP has received significant attention in the literature, leading to the development of numerous exact and heuristic approaches. Despite these efforts, several benchmark instances proposed decades ago and containing only 20 jobs have remained unsolved to proven optimality. In this work, we propose an exact algorithm for the SSP, namely the Combinatorial Branch-and-Bound (C-B\&B) algorithm, which combines two distinct branch-and-bound algorithms, each introducing novel features compared with the existing literature. The former relies on a new branching scheme designed to reduce the size of the implicit enumeration tree, together with a collection of new bounding functions. The latter builds on the branching scheme introduced by Laporte et al. (2004) and strengthens it with a new bounding function and two dominance rules. Within C-B\&B, these exact algorithms are complemented by a preprocessing phase that incorporates a new branch-and-bound-based heuristic capable of rapidly generating a high-quality initial incumbent solution. Extensive computational experiments show that C-B\&B represents a strong breakthrough over previously published approaches, proving optimality for more instances with significantly less computational effort and closing several benchmark instances that have remained open for decades.

</details>


### [94] [Accelerated Prox-Level Methods for Unknown Piecewise-Smooth Optimization II: Function-constrained Optimization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03251)
*Zhenewei Lin,Zhe Zhang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种名为 Restarted Penalty APEX 的任意时刻、几乎无需参数调整的算法，用于解决目标函数和约束函数均为未知分段光滑的函数约束优化问题。该算法在满足二次增长条件的问题上，无需知道增长模量或精确惩罚系数，即可实现最优的预言机复杂度。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的函数约束优化算法通常需要预先知道问题的增长模量（growth modulus）或精确惩罚系数，这在实际应用中往往难以获取。此外，大多数算法无法提供可验证的收敛证书（certificate）来同时约束最优性间隙和约束违反量。这些局限促使研究者探索一种几乎无参数、且能生成可验证证书的算法。

Method: Restarted Penalty APEX 是一种基于惩罚方法的加速束级方法（accelerated bundle-level method），结合了重启（restart）策略来适应分段光滑结构。该算法通过惩罚途径将约束优化问题转化为无约束问题，并利用加速技术和束方法进行求解，同时避免了对外部参数（如增长模量和惩罚系数）的先验依赖。

Result: 对于满足二次增长条件的问题，Restarted Penalty APEX 是首个在无需知道增长模量或精确惩罚系数的情况下达到最优预言机复杂度的算法。此外，该算法能够生成可验证的证书，同时界定最优性间隙和约束违反量，这一证书在现有文献中似乎是新的贡献。

Conclusion: Restarted Penalty APEX 提供了一种高效且实用的求解分段光滑凸函数约束优化问题的框架，克服了对问题参数（如增长模量和惩罚系数）的依赖，显著提升了算法的适用性和鲁棒性，并首次引入了可验证的双重界证书，为该领域的研究提供了新的工具和思路。

Abstract: We introduce an anytime, almost parameter-free algorithm for convex function-constrained optimization, in which the objective and constraint functions are unknown piecewise-smooth. Our algorithm, Restarted Penalty APEX (Penalty Accelerated Prox-level method for Exploring Piecewise Smoothness), is an accelerated bundle-level method based on the penalty approach. For problems satisfying quadratic growth, the proposed method is the first to achieve optimal oracle complexity without knowing the growth modulus or the exact penalty coefficient. Furthermore, Restarted Penalty APEX generates a verifiable certificate that bounds both the optimality gap and the constraint violation. This certificate also appears to be new to the literature.

</details>


### [95] [Mean-field optimal stopping with endogenous quantile cutoffs](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03277)
*Erhan Bayraktar,Ibrahim Ekren,Xihao He*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个具有内生群体层面停摆的均场最优停时问题：当存留群体数量低于预设阈值时，所有剩余智能体同时停止。将不连续目标函数重述为存留质量位于{0}∪[α,1]的奇异非凸约束，证明了强值与弱值的等价性及最优策略的存在性，并建立了动态规划原理。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在群体决策场景中（如银行挤兑、疫情隔离下的集体退出等），停摆阈值本身是由群体状态内生触发的，这类均场最优停时问题具有不连续的目标函数和非凸约束结构，给理论分析（如值函数的正则性、有限近似收敛性）带来根本困难，需要严格的数学框架加以刻画。

Method: 作者将不连续目标转化为生存质量服从{0}∪[α,1]的奇异非凸约束；通过逼近方法证明强值与弱值等价；利用紧性与罚函数方法证明最优规则存在；建立动态规划原理；在严格初始可行条件下，通过恢复序列与经验测度紧性分析有限群体值收敛到均场值。

Result: 值函数在临界边界之外连续，但在边界本身处可能不连续。在严格初始可行条件下，有限群体的值收敛到均场值；近优经验测度满足紧性，且每个均场最优解都具有恢复序列。然而在阈值处，有限群体的收敛可能失败。

Conclusion: 该研究为内生群体停摆的均场最优停时问题提供了完整的理论框架，阐明了值函数的正则性边界以及有限-无限群体逼近的适用条件，指出临界阈值处可能出现有限群体收敛失败这一重要负结果。

Abstract: We study a mean-field optimal stopping problem with an endogenous population-level shutdown. All remaining agents stop when the survival mass falls below a prescribed threshold. We recast the discontinuous objective as the singular, nonconvex constraint that the survival mass lie in $\{0\}\cup[α,1]$. We prove the equivalence of strong and weak values via an approximation and the existence of an optimal rule via compactness and penalization. We also prove a dynamic programming principle. The value is continuous away from the critical boundary but may be discontinuous at the boundary itself. Under strict initial feasibility, finite-population values converge to the mean-field value. In the same regime, the laws of near-optimal empirical measures are tight and every mean-field optimizer admits a recovery sequence. At the threshold, however, finite-population convergence may fail.

</details>
