<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典的Cramer-Lundberg风险模型假设保费和索赔均服从泊松过程且环境不变，这与现实中保险公司面临的复杂运营环境（随机保费、非平稳风险流、外生随机环境变化）不符。论文旨在扩展经典模型以更真实地刻画保险资本演化，并解决在这些推广模型下不破产（破产）概率计算的数值精度问题，提高精算计算的准确性。

Method: 论文对经典Cramer-Lundberg模型的多种推广形式进行建模分析：（1）引入可变确定性或随机保费；（2）考虑非泊松的保费与索赔流；（3）引入随机马尔可夫环境。针对这些推广，推导出不破产概率关于初始资本的积分方程；对于随机马尔可夫环境下的过程，建立对应不同初始状态的积分方程组。随后建立解的存在唯一性条件（一般充要条件及具体充分条件），提出逐次逼近法进行数值与解析求解，并建立其一致收敛性及收敛速率；通过构造精确解的上下近似估计解的精度。

Result: 论文从理论和实践两方面验证了逐次逼近方法的有效性：确立了该方法的统一收敛性及其收敛速率；通过上下近似技术开发了近似解精度的估计方法；将所述方法应用于数值示例，并与蒙特卡洛方法及已有近似解法进行了对比。结果表明该方法能以任意给定精度计算破产概率，可迭代验证并改进经验近似，并可估计和修正蒙特卡洛模拟的精度与参数。

Conclusion: 该研究为更广泛的风险过程模型提供了统一的积分方程框架和可靠的数值求解方法，系统工程化了逐次逼近法在非破产概率计算中的应用。相比蒙特卡洛等方法，所提方法能显著提高精算计算的精度和可靠性，同时具备理论上的收敛保障，具有重要的精算理论与实践价值。

Abstract: The dissertation considers various generalizations of the classical risk process describing the stochastic evolution of the capital of an insurance company (Cramer Lundberg model), including processes with variable deterministic or random premiums, non Poisson flows of premiums and claims, and a stochastic Markovian environment. Integral equations for the probability of nonruin as a function of initial capital are derived for these generalizations. For processes in a stochastic Markovian environment, systems of integral equations for nonruin probabilities corresponding to different initial states are obtained. General necessary and sufficient, as well as specific sufficient, conditions for the existence and uniqueness of solutions are established. A successive approximation method for numerical and analytical solution of the integral equations is theoretically and practically validated; its uniform convergence and rate of convergence are established. A technique for estimating the accuracy of approximate solutions is developed by constructing upper and lower approximations to the exact solution. The method is tested on numerical examples and compared with the Monte Carlo method and known solution approximations. The developed method increases the accuracy of actuarial calculations: it allows the probability of ruin to be calculated with any prescribed accuracy, empirical approximations to be verified and improved iteratively, and the accuracy and parameters of Monte Carlo simulations to be estimated and corrected.

</details>


### [97] [A Unified Theory of H-Duality in First-Order Methods](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03281)
*Kevin Shu,Alex L. Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了两种互补的H-对偶性解释：一种基于具有极值曲率性质的实例上FSFOM迭代的标量不变性；另一种基于性能估计证书的显式变换。作为应用，作者证明了ITEM算法的H-对偶收敛保证，匹配了猜想中的最优梯度到梯度保证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: H-对偶性指许多定步长一阶方法（FSFOMs）的最坏情况性能恰好等于其"时间反转"对应方法在配对性能设定下的最坏情况性能。这一现象可用于自动将具有最终次优性保证的算法转换为具有最终梯度范数保证的算法，但缺乏通用的理论解释。

Method: 第一种解释：证明在具有极值曲率性质的实例类上，FSFOM产生的最终迭代是初始迭代的标量倍数，且该标量在算法时间反转下不变；结合许多FSFOM以这类极值曲率实例为最坏情形的经验观察，解释H-对偶性的成因。第二种解释：利用性能估计证书（performance estimation certificate），展示一种显式的证书变换，该变换保持证书的核心属性，并证明该变换在许多数值和分析实例中将算法的收敛证书变换为其时间反转的证书。

Result: 提供了两种互补的H-对偶性解释机制，并作为应用，证明了ITEM算法H-对偶版本的收敛保证，这与推测的最优梯度到梯度保证相匹配。

Conclusion: 两项互补的解释共同为H-对偶现象提供了系统的理论依据，同时也为自动生成具有梯度范数保证的算法提供了理论支撑。

Abstract: We provide two complementary explanations of H-duality in smooth strongly convex optimization and contractive fixed-point problems. H-duality refers to the phenomenon where the worst-case performance of many fixed-step first-order methods (FSFOMs) for a given performance setup are exactly equal to the worst-case performance of their "time-reversed" counterparts and a paired performance setup. This concept gained interest as a mechanism for automatically converting an algorithm with guarantees on the final suboptimality to algorithms with guarantees on the final gradient norm. Since its initial discovery, additional H-dual performance setup pairings have been discovered empirically, yet a general explanation for these phenomena remained elusive.
  Our first explanation of H-duality shows that, on a class of instances with extremal curvature properties, FSFOMs produce a final iterate that is a scalar multiple of the initial iterate, and that this scalar is invariant under the time reversal of the underlying FSFOM. This, together with the empirical observation that many FSFOMs have such an instance with extremal curvature as their worst case, gives one explanation for why H-duality often occurs. The second explanation is complementary and uses the notion of a performance estimation certificate of the convergence rate of a FSFOM. We exhibit an explicit transformation of such certificates that preserves many of the properties that define such a certificate, and we show that in many numerical and analytical examples, this transformation in fact transforms certificates of the convergence rate of an algorithm into certificates for its time-reversal. As an application, we prove a convergence guarantee for the H-dual of ITEM matching the conjectured optimal gradient-to-gradient guarantee.

</details>


### [98] [On the Complexity of Recognizing SDP Exactness for the Maximum Cut Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03508)
*Avinash Bhardwaj*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文解决了最大割问题中SDP松弛精确性判定问题的计算复杂度。通过构造多项式有界整数权重的对偶证书，证明加权图情形的强NP困难性；再通过将受限Not-All-Equal 4-SAT几何嵌入边不相交团结构，将困难性扩展到简单无权重图。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Delorme和Poljak曾证明判定SDP精确性是NP困难的，但其归约依赖于指数级缩放边权重，仅建立了弱NP困难性，并明确留下了无权重简单图情形复杂度未解决的问题。

Method: 第一个证明采用和平方对偶证书构造，使用权重的多项式有界整数实现强NP-hard归约。第二个证明通过几何嵌入受限NAE-4-SAT到边不相交团结构中，结合几何锁定机制迫使连续SDP松弛达到绝对全局最小值，将组合困难性与连续界解耦。

Result: 证明了加权图SDP精确性判定是强NP困难的，并将该困难性扩展到简单无权重图。

Conclusion: 完整解决了SDP精确性判定问题的计算复杂度，填补了Delorme和Poljak留下的开放问题，表明该问题即使在简单的无权重图上也具有计算困难性。

Abstract: The Semidefinite Programming (SDP) relaxation of the Maximum Cut (Max-Cut) problem is exact when its optimal value equals the integer maximum cut, geometrically corresponding to a rank-1 optimal solution. While the pioneering work of Delorme and Poljak established that recognizing exactness is NP-hard, their reduction relied on exponentially scaling edge weights. This established only weak NP-hardness and explicitly left open the complexity for simple, unweighted graphs. In this paper, we resolve the computational complexity of the exactness property. First, we prove that deciding SDP exactness for weighted graphs is strongly NP-hard by constructing a sum-of-squares dual certificate with polynomially bounded integer weights. Second, we extend this hardness to simple, unweighted graphs via a geometric embedding of restricted Not-All-Equal 4-SAT into edge-disjoint clique structures. Both proofs utilize geometric locking mechanisms that force the continuous SDP relaxation to an absolute global minimum, decoupling the continuous bounds from the underlying combinatorial hardness.

</details>


### [99] [Beyond Higher-Pulse Rectification: Operational Harmonic Coordination in Renewable P2H Systems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03531)
*Yangjun Zeng,Yiwei Qiu,Jie Zhu,Yi Zhou,Tao Wu,Xin Meng,Shi Chen,Buxiang Zhou,Kaigui Xie*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一种运行谐波协调方案，使低成本12脉波晶闸管整流器（TR）通过多电解槽协调运行满足电网谐波要求，替代传统高价24脉波方案，在可再生能源制氢系统中同时优化制氢与谐波治理，实验表明利润提升31%，整流级成本降低37.5%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 晶闸管整流器（TR）是可再生能源制氢（ReP2H）系统中经济有效的电解电源，但其产生的谐波可能违反电网规范限值。传统解决依赖高脉波（如24脉波）整流器，成本高。因此需要探索让低成本12脉波TR通过运行层面协调满足谐波要求的方案。

Method: 首先建立谐波模型，量化整流变压器（RCT）分接头位置和电解电流对谐波的影响，实现多个电解槽（ELZ）间的谐波抵消。随后建立两层协调框架：小时级调度在可再生不确定性下采用随机规划和改进的渐进对冲算法，在谐波可行域内确定ELZ启停；分钟级控制跟踪可再生出力并抑制谐波。另设计分解算法，将生产调度与谐波抑制解耦，提升计算效率。

Result: 基于实际工程案例的研究表明，所提方法相较仅调节电流的方案利润提升31%。年仿真进一步显示，协调运行的12脉波TR可替代24脉波整流器满足谐波合规要求，通过以额外RCT分接头动作换取更低的变压器投资，整流级成本降低37.5%。

Conclusion: 所提运行谐波协调方案能有效使低成本12脉波晶闸管整流器满足电网谐波要求，同时改善系统经济性，为ReP2H系统提供了一种兼顾制氢效益与谐波合规的低成本工程解决方案。

Abstract: Thyristor rectifiers (TRs) are cost-effective electrolysis power supplies for renewable power-to-hydrogen (ReP2H) systems, but their harmonics may violate grid-code limits. In contrast to conventional solutions that rely on higher-pulse (such as 24-pulse) rectifiers, this paper proposes an operational harmonic coordination scheme that enables low-cost 12-pulse TRs to meet harmonic requirements through coordinated operation. First, a harmonic model quantifies the effects of rectifier transformer (RCT) tap positions and electrolytic currents, enabling harmonic cancellation among multiple electrolyzers (ELZs). A two-layer framework then coordinates hydrogen production and harmonic mitigation. Hourly scheduling determines ELZ commitment within the harmonic feasible region under renewable uncertainty using stochastic programming and a modified progressive hedging algorithm, while minute-level dispatch tracks renewable power and mitigates harmonics. A decomposition algorithm separates production dispatch from harmonic mitigation to improve computational efficiency. Case studies based on real-life projects show that the proposed method increases profit by 31% over current-only regulation. Annual simulations further show that coordinated 12-pulse TRs can replace 24-pulse rectifiers for harmonic compliance by exchanging additional RCT tap actions for lower transformer investment, reducing rectification-stage cost by 37.5%.

</details>


### [100] [Well-posedness of Filtering Equations in Weighted Sobolev Spaces with Unbounded System Coefficients](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03549)
*Zeju Sun,Songlin Zhou,Stephen S. -T. Yau*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文在统一的缓冲加权框架下，研究连续时间非线性滤波中三个基本演化方程（稳健DMZ方程、随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程）的适定性问题。通过引入指数型权重函数和加权Sobolev空间，在系数允许无界多项式增长的更一般条件下，建立了三个方程的弱解存在唯一性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 非线性滤波是现代控制理论的核心课题之一，其三个基本演化方程的适定性研究对于滤波理论的严格基础至关重要。现有研究通常要求系统的系数有界或满足较强正则性条件，这限制了理论的适用性。作者希望在更一般的框架下（允许系数具有多项式增长的无界性）统一处理三个滤波方程，填补这一理论空白。

Method: 首先引入指数型权重函数及相应的加权Sobolev空间，为滤波方程的变分处理提供更一般的基础框架；在这些加权空间中证明稳健DMZ方程弱解的适定性；随后利用规范（指数）变换及其逆变换，将稳健DMZ方程的结果转移至随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程，从而在缓冲加权Sobolev空间中建立三个方程解的存在唯一性。

Result: 在温和且易于验证的假设下，建立了稳健DMZ方程弱解在加权空间中的适定性；并通过规范变换将结果推广到随机DMZ方程和Kushner-Stratonovich方程，证明它们在缓冲加权Sobolev空间中解的存在唯一性，实现了对三个滤波方程的统一处理。同时总结了适定性的充分条件，验证了该框架对一般非线性滤波系统的广泛适用性。

Conclusion: 该研究为连续时间非线性滤波方程建立了统一的适定性理论框架，放宽了对系统系数的有界性要求，使得在系数可为无界多项式增长的条件下三个基本滤波方程仍然有良好定义的唯一解。这一结果为非线性滤波理论在更广泛实际系统中的应用提供了严格的数学基础。

Abstract: Nonlinear filtering problem is one of the core subjects in modern control theory. In this paper, we will study the well-posedness of the three fundamental evolution equations arising in continuous-time nonlinear filtering--the robust Duncan-Mortensen-Zakai (DMZ) equation, the stochastic DMZ equation, and the Kushner-Stratonovich equation--within a unified buffered weighted formulation. An exponential-type weight function and the corresponding weighted Sobolev spaces are introduced to enable a variational treatment of the filtering equations in a more general setting, in which the coefficients of the filtering system may be unbounded with polynomial growth. Under mild and easily verifiable assumptions, we first establish the well-posedness of the weak solution to the robust DMZ equation in these weighted spaces. Using the gauge (exponential) transformation and its inverse, these results are then transferred to the stochastic DMZ equation and the Kushner-Stratonovich equation, whose solutions are shown to exist and be unique in buffered weighted Sobolev spaces, yielding a unified treatment of all three filtering equations. Sufficient conditions for the well-posedness are also summarized, which illustrate the wide applicability of the proposed framework to general nonlinear filtering systems.

</details>


### [101] [The Traveling Tournament Problem: An Overview](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03612)
*David Van Bulck,Fan Yang,Dries Goossens,Michael Trick*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文对旅游巡回赛问题（TTP）进行了全面的文献综述，回顾了25年来该问题的主要变体、基准实例、下界、近似保证以及精确和启发式优化算法的最新进展，并接手了Michael Trick教授建立的基准仓库，持续跟踪和验证上下界，同时指出了若干开放问题和未来研究方向。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: TTP是体育赛事调度中最受广泛研究的优化问题之一，源自美国职业棒球大联盟的实际赛程安排需求，过去25年积累了丰富的研究成果，需要系统性地梳理文献并维护统一的基准追踪体系。

Method: 采用综合文献综述的方法，系统回顾TTP的主要变体和基准实例，总结下界构造方法、近似算法、精确求解算法和启发式算法的最新现状，同时接管并延续原有的基准仓库，对已知上下界进行跟踪和验证。

Result: 该研究全面总结了TTP领域的当前研究状态，维护并更新了基准实例的上下界记录（延续RobinX项目框架），识别了领域中尚未解决的关键问题。

Conclusion: TTP领域虽经过25年发展已取得显著进展，但仍在精确算法扩展、大规模实例求解、新变体建模等方面存在开放挑战，需要进一步研究。

Abstract: Over the past 25 years, the Traveling Tournament Problem (TTP) has become one of the most extensively studied optimization problems in sports scheduling. At its core, the TTP seeks to minimize the total travel distance incurred by teams that travel directly between opponents' venues during consecutive away games. The problem originated from the scheduling challenges faced by Major League Baseball, where it was identified as the central computational difficulty. This paper provides a comprehensive overview of the literature on the TTP. We review the principal problem variants and benchmark instances, and summarize the current state of the art in lower bounds, approximation guarantees, and exact and heuristic optimization algorithms. Moreover, we contribute to the continued development of the field by tracking and validating lower and upper bounds, while succeeding the repository originally established by Prof. Michael Trick as part of the RobinX sports timetabling project. Finally, we identify several open questions and outline promising directions for future research.

</details>


### [102] [Nash-Bargaining HalpernSGD via Limited-Memory MultiLRSGA: A Two-Phase Optimizer for Multi-Objective Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03616)
*Katherine Rossella Foglia,Francesco Sergio Pisani,Vittorio Colao*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 提出NB-HalpernSGD via LM-MultiLRSGA，一种两阶段多目标优化器。第一阶段用有限内存变体的MultiLRSGA（即LM-MultiLRSGA）近似Nash均衡作为分歧点基准；第二阶段基于该基准点，将Nash讨价还价问题重写为对数最小化替代目标，并用HalpernSGD求解，最终导向Pareto优化解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 多目标优化中需要在相互冲突的损失间取得平衡。现有的方法如PCGrad、MultiAdam、DualConeGD等难以同时兼顾竞争性均衡与Pareto最优性。作者希望通过将竞争阶段（Nash均衡）与讨价还价阶段（Nash bargaining）结合，获得既有理论依据又性能优异的多目标优化算法。

Method: 1) 第一阶段：提出LM-MultiLRSGA，即MultiLRSGA的有限内存变体，在保留旋转校正机制的同时降低内存开销，通过求解与多目标问题关联的博弈来逼近Nash均衡；2) 第一阶段得到的Nash均衡点作为讨价还价阶段的基准（分歧点）；3) 第二阶段：将原始多目标问题对应的Nash bargaining problem的Nash积改写为对数最小化替代目标，用HalpernSGD在该基准点基础上求解，向Pareto最优解方向推进。

Result: 在PINN启发的神经模型上进行了验证，该方法在性能上超过了PCGrad、MultiAdam和DualConeGD等成熟的多目标优化器。另外，文中讨论了LM-MultiLRSGA阶段的收敛性和稳定性性质（HalpernSGD本身已有充分的理论研究）。

Conclusion: 通过将Nash均衡的竞争性参考点与Nash讨价还价框架相结合，该方法实现了从竞争性平衡到Pareto导向解的两阶段优化路径，在PINN任务上展现出优于现有基准方法的性能，同时LM-MultiLRSGA在降低内存开销的同时保持了收敛特性。

Abstract: We propose NB-HalpernSGD via LM-MultiLRSGA, a two-phase optimizer for multi-objective optimization. The process starts with a competitive optimization phase by applying a limited-memory variant of MultiLRSGA, named LM-MultiLRSGA, designed to reduce the memory footprint of the original optimizer while preserving its rotational correction mechanism. It addresses multi-objective tasks by considering the competitive game associated with the original multi-objective problem, approximating a Nash equilibrium. This point defines a Nash-equilibrium-induced disagreement point: we formulate the Nash bargaining problem associated with the original losses and rewrite its Nash product as a logarithmic minimization surrogate. This minimization problem is solved using HalpernSGD, anchored at the computed competitive reference point. Therefore, the method uses the Nash equilibrium as a principled reference point for the bargaining stage and then moves toward a Pareto-oriented solution of the original multi-objective problem. We validate the proposed optimizer on a PINN-inspired neural model, where it outperforms established multi-objective optimizers, including PCGrad, MultiAdam, and DualConeGD. Finally, while the convergence properties of HalpernSGD have been extensively studied, we discuss the convergence and stability properties of the proposed LM-MultiLRSGA phase.

</details>


### [103] [On the $Ω(n)$ SDP relaxation gap for Submodular Box-Constrained Quadratic Programming](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03617)
*Junyu Zhang,Guanyi Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了子模盒约束二次规划（BCQP）的SDP松弛的最坏情况松弛间隙，证明在维度n≥4时，任何具有有限线性割族的SDP松弛都存在严格正的松弛间隙实例，并给出了显式的下界估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 子模BCQP是一类重要的非凸优化问题，具有全局保证，其SDP松弛是常用的凸松弛方法。然而，理解SDP松弛（即使加上有限个线性有效割）能有多好（即松弛间隙的下界）对于设计更优的凸近似方法至关重要，目前对这一根本性局限缺乏量化理解。

Method: 通过构造对抗性实例来证明SDP松弛的局限性。首先证明一般维度下任何有限线性割族SDP松弛都存在正间隙实例；随后对归一化BCQP实例，分别针对仅有BQP有效割（含McCormick和三角不等式）的情况以及任意有限m个线性割族的情况，给出松弛间隙的下界估计。

Result: 在维度n≥4时，(1) 任何含有限线性割族的SDP松弛对子模BCQP都存在严格正松弛间隙的实例；(2) 对于只含BQP有效割的SDP松弛，建立了Ω(n)的松弛间隙下界；(3) 对于任意有限m个有效线性割的SDP松弛，建立了Ω(n/m²)的松弛间隙下界。

Conclusion: 这些结果揭示了SDP松弛（特别是含BQP有效割的SDP）在近似子模BCQP时的根本局限，为设计更有效的凸松弛（需要哪些额外约束条件）提供了指导性见解。

Abstract: Continuous submodular optimization is an important class of nonconvex problems with global guarantees, for which the submodular box-constrained quadratic programming (BCQP) forms a fundamental subclass. It arises naturally as quadratic subproblems of general twice-differentiable submodular problems and also has direct business and management applications such as pricing. This paper studies the worst-case relaxation gap of semidefinite programming (SDP) relaxations with a finite family of valid linear cuts for submodular BCQP. We show that in dimension $n\geq4$, for any SDP relaxation formulated with a finite family of linear cuts, there always exists a submodular BCQP instance with a strictly positive relaxation gap. By restricting to the normalized BCQP instances that remove the problem's scaling factor, we further quantify this limitation with explicit lower bounds. In general dimension $n\geq4$, when the SDP relaxation is formulated with only Boolean-quadric-polytope (BQP) valid cuts, including the commonly used McCormick and triangle inequalities, we establish an $Ω(n)$ relaxation gap lower bound. For an arbitrary family of $m$ valid linear cuts, we establish an $Ω(n/m^2)$ lower bound. These results capture the fundamental limitations of SDP relaxations, especially SDP with BQP valid cuts, and provide guidance on what additional constraints may be needed for more effective convex approximations of submodular BCQP.

</details>


### [104] [Bi-objective Two Phase Transportation Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03671)
*Prabhjot Kaur,Kalpana Dahiya,Vanita Verma*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了一个双目标两阶段运输问题（BTPTP），其中所有源-目的地链路被划分为两个不相交的阶段集合，阶段II的运输必须在阶段I完成之后进行，各阶段内部并行运输。目标是同时最小化两阶段的总运输时间和总运输成本。作者提出了一个多项式时间迭代算法BTPTP-Algorithm来求解该问题，该算法通过求解受限最小成本运输问题，系统地剔除支配解并记录所有非支配解，并给出了算法完备性的证明及数值实验。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统运输问题通常假设所有货物可同时在单一阶段从产地运往销地，但在实际应用中（如物流分拣、多层次供应链等）运输往往分阶段进行，且阶段之间存在先后顺序约束。同时，实际运输决策通常涉及多个相互冲突的目标（如时间与成本），因此需要研究同时优化时间和成本的两阶段运输问题。

Method: 首先将双目标两阶段运输问题形式化为同时最小化两阶段总运输时间和总运输成本的多目标优化模型。由于目标相互冲突，作者开发了BTPTP-Algorithm——一个多项式时间的迭代算法。在每次迭代中，算法采用前一阶段得到的Phase-I和Phase-II运输时间作为限制条件，构造并求解受限的成本最小化运输问题，获得相应的最优可行解。算法系统地排除受支配解并保留所有非支配点。文中还给出了算法能记录全部非支配解的完备性证明，并通过数值算例验证。

Result: 提供了BTPTP-Algorithm能够系统性记录所有非支配解的数学证明；通过数值实例说明了该算法的理论正确性；针对不同规模的BTPTP实例，报告了算法在CPU时间方面的计算表现，验证了其计算效率与实用性。

Conclusion: 本文成功设计了一个多项式时间的迭代算法来解决双目标两阶段运输问题，该算法能够完整枚举所有非支配解，兼顾了运输时间与成本的折中优化，并通过研究与数值实验验证了其有效性和计算可行性，为具有多阶段约束的多目标运输优化问题提供了有效的求解途径。

Abstract: This paper discusses a bi-objective two-phase transportation problem (BTPTP) in which transportation takes place in two phases. The whole set of source-destination links is partitioned into two disjoint sets namely, Phase-I and Phase-II. The transportation among the source-destination links of Phase-II set is done only after the transportation among the source-destination links of Phase-I set is complete. The transportation among the source-destination links in each phase is done in parallel. The problem discussed in this paper concentrates on minimizing two objectives viz., sum of transportation times and sum of transportation costs of both the phases, simultaneously.
  Due to conflicting nature of the objectives, a polynomial time iterative algorithm named as BTPTP-Algorithm is developed that finds all of its non-dominated solutions. At each iteration, the BTPTP-Algorithm solves some specific restricted cost minimizing transportation problems and finds corresponding optimal feasible solutions. The restrictions imposed on the source-destination links of Phase-I and Phase-II sets, in each of these problems, depend upon their corresponding Phase-I and Phase-II transportation times obtained at the previous step. The algorithm is developed in such a way that it expels all the dominated solutions and records all the non-dominated solutions of the problem, systematically. A proof of the BTPTP-Algorithm's capability of recording all the non-dominated points is provided. Further, a numerical illustration is given in support of the theory and computational behavior of the BTPTP-Algorithm for various BTPTP instances of different sizes, is provided in terms of CPU time.

</details>


### [105] [Automated Priority Rule Design for the Resource-Constrained Project Scheduling Problem: A Large Language Model-Guided Population-Based Search](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03754)
*Jingyu Luo,Mario Vanhoucke,José Coelho*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一个基于大语言模型（LLM）引导的种群搜索框架，用于自动化设计资源受限项目调度问题（RCPSP）的优先规则。LLM在离线搜索中生成和迭代修改候选规则，训练项目的调度质量作为适应度反馈。实验表明，LLM设计的规则在测试集上优于传统单一规则和同等搜索代价下的遗传规划（GP）规则，与大规模GP搜索得到的规则相当。在大型高并行项目上，选定的LLM设计规则优于所有传统规则和两种遗传算法配置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: RCPSP旨在在满足优先关系和可再生资源约束下最小化完工时间。优先规则启发式方法计算成本低且决策逻辑清晰，被广泛应用于实践。但传统规则在不同项目上表现不一致，现有自动化优先规则设计主要依赖GP超启发式，需要在训练项目上评估大量候选规则，代价高昂。最近的LLM进展使得利用性能反馈生成和迭代修正优先规则成为可能，因此作者探索LLM引导的自动化规则设计方法。

Method: 本文提出一个LLM引导的种群进化框架用于自动化优先规则设计。具体地：（1）在离线搜索阶段，LLM负责生成和修正候选规则；（2）候选规则在训练项目上的调度质量被用作适应度指标，反馈给LLM以指导后续的规则修订；（3）搜索结束后，返回最优规则，可直接应用于未见过的测试项目，无需进一步搜索或LLM调用。实验还包含消融研究来分析各主要搜索组件对性能的影响，以及对LLM设计规则的结构和决策行为进行分析。

Result: 实验结果表明：（1）LLM设计的规则在测试集上优于传统单一规则；（2）在可比搜索代价下，LLM设计规则优于GP设计规则；（3）在较大规模GP搜索下得到的规则面前仍具有竞争力；（4）在大型高并行项目上，选定的LLM设计规则优于所有传统规则和两种遗传算法配置；（5）消融研究揭示了各搜索组件的贡献，规则分析刻画了LLM设计规则的结构与决策行为。

Conclusion: LLM引导的种群搜索框架为RCPSP的自动化优先规则设计提供了一种有效的替代方案，其搜索效率优于传统GP方法，产出的规则泛化能力强，在实际应用中无需在线搜索或额外LLM调用即可直接部署。

Abstract: The objective of the resource-constrained project scheduling problem (RCPSP) is to minimize makespan while satisfying precedence and renewable-resource constraints. Priority-rule heuristics construct feasible schedules with low computational cost and explicit decision logic, making them widely used in practice and an attractive alternative to more computationally intensive methods. However, no traditional rule performs consistently well across projects, and researchers have therefore investigated automated priority-rule design. Genetic programming (GP) hyper-heuristics have been the predominant approach to this task, but evolving a high-performing rule may require evaluating many candidate rules on the training projects. Recent advances in large language models (LLMs) make it possible to generate and iteratively revise priority rules using performance feedback.
  This paper presents an LLM-guided population-based framework for automated priority-rule design. During the offline search, an LLM generates and revises candidate rules, while schedule quality on training projects determines candidate fitness and guides subsequent revisions. At the end of the search, the best rule is returned and applied directly to unseen projects without further search or LLM calls.
  Experiments show that the LLM-designed rules outperform traditional single rules across the test sets and outperform GP-designed rules obtained under comparable search effort, while remaining competitive with rules obtained from a substantially larger GP search. On large projects, selected LLM-designed rules outperform all considered traditional rules and two genetic algorithm configurations on highly parallel projects. An ablation study examines how changes to the main search components affect performance, while rule analyses describe the structure and decision behavior of the LLM-designed rules.

</details>


### [106] [Lifetime Profit-Maximising Co-optimisation of Multi-Service Stacking for Battery Storage](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03767)
*Yuxin Xia,Frederik Schiele,Yihong Zhou,Volkan Kumtepeli,David Howey,Iacopo Savelli,Thomas Morstyn*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种考虑电池老化的再ceding-horizon优化框架，用于电网级储能系统在多市场（电能交易与频率响应服务）中的服务堆叠策略协同优化，并在英国市场（含Enduring Auction Capability平台）的实际数据上验证，显示老化建模可将终身收益提升最高32%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 电网级电池储能的利润最大化依赖于跨电能与频率响应市场进行服务堆叠，但由于服务决策通过共享电池容量相互耦合、受系统运营商能量管理规则约束，并受产品特定技术要求（影响运行、老化和终身盈利）影响，识别终身利润最大化的堆叠策略仍具挑战性。

Method: 构建一个老化感知的receding-horizon（滚动时域）优化框架，显式纳入产品特性与充放电状态（State-of-Energy）合规规则，对多服务堆叠进行协同优化。该框架应用于英国市场，储能运营商可在National Energy System Operator的能量管理约束下，将电能交易与多种通过Enduring Auction Capability平台采购的动态频率响应服务进行堆叠，使用真实市场数据评估。

Result: 考虑老化可使终身收入较不考虑老化的基准提高最多32%；更高保真的老化模型可较简化模型进一步带来最多16%的收入提升。较低的折现率倾向于平衡日历老化与循环老化的策略，较高折现率则偏好激进运行。所有策略中，响应正频率偏差的服务持续优于响应负频率偏差的服务。终身利润最大化需随电池老化动态调整最优服务组合。

Conclusion: 老化建模质量、折现率和电池老化状态共同决定终身价值与最优堆叠策略；随电池老化自适应调整服务组合是最大化终身利润的关键。

Abstract: Grid-scale battery energy storage can generate revenue by stacking services across electricity and frequency response markets, yet identifying the lifetime profit-maximising stacking strategy remains challenging. Decisions across services are coupled through shared battery system capacity, constrained by system operator energy management rules, and further shaped by product-specific technical requirements that govern system operation, degradation, and lifetime profitability. This paper presents an ageing-aware receding-horizon framework for co-optimising multi-service stacking that explicitly captures product-specific characteristics and state-of-energy compliance rules. The framework is applied to the Great Britain market, where storage operators can stack electricity trading with multiple dynamic frequency response services procured through the newly introduced 'Enduring Auction Capability' platform under energy management requirements imposed by the National Energy System Operator. Using real market data, we demonstrate that degradation modelling, discount rate, and battery ageing jointly govern both lifetime value and optimal stacking strategy. Accounting for ageing increases lifetime revenue by up to 32% relative to a degradation-agnostic benchmark, while higher-fidelity ageing modelling can provide a further revenue improvement of up to 16% over simpler formulations. Lower discount rates favour strategies that balance calendar and cycling ageing, while higher discount rates favour aggressive operation. Across all strategies, services responding to positive frequency deviations are consistently preferred over those responding to negative deviations. Lifetime profit is maximised by adapting the optimal service mix as the battery ages.

</details>


### [107] [Data-Driven Brownian Reflection Control](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03870)
*Guodong Pang,Dacheng Yao,Hao Yin*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了漂移和波动率未知的布朗运动模型的反射控制问题，提出了学习-优化（LTO）算法实现O(√T)期望遗憾界，以及自适应更新（AU）和全历史自适应更新（AU-FH）算法实现O(log T)遗憾界，后者利用全部历史数据在数值模拟中表现更优。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有反射控制问题研究多假设系统参数（漂移、波动率）已知，但实际应用中这些参数往往未知，需要从数据中学习。如何在参数未知的情况下设计高效且具有严格遗憾界保证的控制策略是一个关键挑战。

Method: 本文提出三类算法：(1) LTO算法——先估计策略关键参数再代入最优性方程求解策略；(2) AU算法——持续更新参数估计和反射水平；(3) AU-FH算法——利用全部历史数据持续更新。理论上将遗憾分解为探索、瞬态和学习三部分：瞬态遗憾通过过渡半群与平稳性的时间积分偏差来界定，并借助Foster-Lyapunov不等式获得受控反射布朗运动（RBM）的指数收敛保证；学习遗憾通过建立估计量的一致性和均方误差界来控制；利用移动边界Skorokhod映射的单调性推导切换状态的矩界。

Result: LTO算法达到O(√T)的有限时间期望遗憾界；AU和AU-FH算法达到改进的O(log T)遗憾界。在数值模拟中，利用全部历史数据的AU-FH算法表现出更好的性能。

Conclusion: 该研究为参数未知的布朗反射控制问题提供了系统性的数据驱动解决方案，证明持续自适应更新策略比先学习后优化的两阶段方法具有更优的理论保证和实际性能，为反射控制领域的在线学习与优化提供了完整理论框架。

Abstract: We study a data-driven reflection control problem for a Brownian model with unknown drift and volatility. We first propose a learn-then-optimize (LTO) algorithm: it estimates the policy-relevant parameter during exploration, plugs the estimate into the optimality equation, and exploits the resulting policy---achieving an $O(\sqrt{T})$ finite-time expected regret bound. We further propose two algorithms, adaptive-updating (AU) and full-history adaptive-updating (AU-FH), which continuously update the estimator and reflecting level, attaining an improved $O(\log T)$ regret bound. Notably, AU-FH algorithms leverages all historical data, yielding better performance in numerical simulations. Our analysis decomposes regret into exploration, transient, and learning components. Transient regret from nonstationarity is bounded by the time-integrated deviation of the transition semigroup from stationarity evaluated on the holding cost, which can be further bounded via a Foster-Lyapunov inequality for exponential convergence of the controlled reflected Brownian motion (RBM). For learning regret, we establish local regularity properties together with consistency and mean-squared error bounds for the estimator, which control the stationary cost gap between the learned and optimal reflection policies. In addition, we leverage the monotonicity of the moving boundary Skorokhod map to derive moment bounds for the AU algorithms' switching states, via pathwise comparison with fixed-boundary RBMs.

</details>


### [108] [Asymptotic Analysis of Gradient Mapping-type Stationarity Measure for the Sum of Nonconvex Nonsmooth Functions and Applications to Proximal Gradient-type Algorithms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03950)
*Keita Kume,Isao Yamada*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种适用于两个可能非光滑非凸函数之和的梯度映射型平稳性度量，证明了在适当正则性假设下，Fréchet或近端平稳性可通过该度量沿某个收敛序列的渐近消失来刻画。文章还给出了该度量的平滑变体的渐近结果，并作为应用，肯定地回答了Olikier-Waldspurger提出的一个关于近端正则函数序列收敛性的开放问题，同时提出了一个带非单调线搜索的近端变量平滑算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 研究动机在于为两个非光滑非凸函数之和提供一个可行的平稳性度量，用于刻画Fréchet平稳点和近端平稳点，并解决已有文献中关于近端梯度法生成的聚类点是否一定是近端平稳点的开放问题。此外，在较低正则性条件下，需要一个有效的算法框架来求解此类问题。

Method: 本文方法包括：(1)构造梯度映射型平稳性度量，在Fréchet/近端正则性假设下证明其渐近消失刻画平稳性；(2)建立该度量的平滑变体的渐近等价结果；(3)将度量分析应用于回答Olikier-Waldspurger的开放问题；(4)提出近端变量平滑算法结合非单调线搜索，处理一个分量满足较低正则性、另一个分量满足近端正则性的情形。

Result: 主要结果包括：平稳性度量满足可刻画的渐近性质；肯定地回答了开放问题，即在一个分量局部Lipschitz光滑条件下，近端梯度法生成的每个聚类点都是近端平稳点；所提出的算法在平稳性度量消失的子序列中，每个聚类点都是Fréchet平稳点。

Conclusion: 本文建立了一套统一的平稳性度量理论框架，既可用来验证已有算法生成序列的收敛性质，也可用于指导新算法的设计，为非光滑非凸优化提供理论基础和方法工具。

Abstract: We propose a gradient mapping-type stationarity measure for the sum of two possibly nonsmooth nonconvex functions. Under suitable regularity assumptions, we show that Fréchet or proximal stationarity can be characterized through asymptotic vanishing of the proposed measure along some convergent sequence. We also establish analogous asymptotic results for a smoothing-based variant of the measure, which enables us to combine the proposed framework with smoothing techniques developed for nonsmooth optimization. As an application of our analysis of the stationarity measure, we provide an affirmative answer to an open question raised by [Olikier-Waldspurger, SIAM J. Optim., 2025] on whether every cluster point of a sequence generated by a proximal gradient method is a proximal stationary point under local Lipschitz smoothness of one component of the cost function. As a second application, we propose a proximal variable smoothing algorithm with a nonmonotone linesearch for minimizing the sum of two nonsmooth nonconvex functions under lower regularity of one component and prox-regularity of the other. For the proposed algorithm, we show that every cluster point of a subsequence such that the stationarity measure vanishes is a Fréchet stationary point.

</details>


### [109] [Heavy-Tailed First-Order Optimization for Polyak-Łojasiewicz Condition: High-Dimensional Minimax Bounds, High-Probability Guarantee, and Fixed-Dimensional Improvements](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03990)
*Weiming Ou,Xiao Wang*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了在α阶矩条件下（1<α≤2）平滑Polyak–Łojasiewicz（PL）优化的噪声自适应复杂度下界与上界。当维度可能依赖于查询预算时，得到噪声自适应下界，恢复了无噪声PL下界；在镜像-PL条件下，提出中心截断镜像下降法达到匹配的高概率上界。同时刻画了固定维度d=1,2,3及d>3在特定条件下的最优随机复杂度，并分析了互补区域的剩余差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有PL优化研究通常假设梯度噪声为次高斯或具有有界梯度/域约束，而这些假设在参数维度随预算增长或噪声重尾（仅α阶矩有限的Levy噪声）时不成立。作者希望建立噪声自适应（即无需预知噪声参数）的复杂界，并完整刻画在固定维度下PL优化的随机复杂度。

Method: 理论分析上，作者证明了维度可能依赖于预算T时PL优化满足α阶矩（1<α≤2）噪声条件的下界。算法上采用中心截断镜像下降方法（centered-clipped mirror-descent），该截断中心化使得在噪声重尾条件下也能获得匹配的高概率上界。对固定维度情形，通过判断α/(α-1)与d-1的大小关系将维度分为不同区域，小维度（d=1,2,3）或α/(α-1)≥d-1时获得匹配阶，互补区域引入曲面熵因子作为上界并识别剩余差距。

Result: 推导出噪声自适应下界[κlog(Δ0/ε)+κ(σ²/(με))^{α/(2(α-1))}]，恢复无噪声情形。给出中心截断镜像下降法达到匹配高概率上界（含对数因子），无需有界域/梯度或次高斯假设。在固定维度d=1,2,3及d>3但α/(α-1)≥d-1时最优随机项为Θ̃_α[(σ²/(με))^{α/(2(α-1))}]；互补区域给出含曲面熵因子的上界并明确剩余差距。

Conclusion: 该工作首次在α阶矩（重尾）噪声设定下对PL优化给出了匹配（至高数因子）的噪声自适应上下界，并系统刻画了固定维度中随机复杂度的行为，表明噪声项维数无关性取决于α与维度d的关系。

Abstract: We study smooth Polyak--Łojasiewicz (PL) optimization with conditionally unbiased stochastic gradients satisfying \[ \mathbb E\!\left[ \|G_t-\nabla f(x_t)\|^α\mid\mathcal F_{t-1} \right]\le σ^α, \qquad 1<α\le2. \] When the dimension may depend on the oracle budget, we prove the noise-adaptive lower bound \[ T_ε=
Ω_α\!\left[ κ\log\frac{Δ_0}ε +
κ\left( \frac{σ^2}{με} \right)^{\fracα{2(α-1)}} \right], \] which recovers the noiseless PL lower bound when $σ=0$. Under the appropriate mirror-PL condition, we give a centered-clipped mirror-descent method attaining the matching high-probability upper bound up to logarithmic factors, without bounded-domain, bounded-gradient, or sub-Gaussian assumptions.
  We further characterize the stochastic complexity in prescribed fixed dimensions. For $d=1,2,3$, the optimal stochastic term is \[ \widetildeΘ_α\!\left[ \left( \frac{σ^2}{με} \right)^{\fracα{2(α-1)}} \right]. \] For every fixed $d>3$, the same characterization holds whenever \[ \fracα{α-1}\ge d-1. \] In the complementary regime, we provide an upper bound with an additional surface-entropy factor and explicitly identify the remaining gap.

</details>


### [110] [DREAM: Deep-Reparametrization of Adaptive Regularization Maps for Fast Zero-Shot Self-Supervised Learning](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04019)
*Thanh Trung Vu,Ander Biguri,Christoph Kolbitsch,Luca Calatroni,Kostas Papafitsoros,Andreas Kofler*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出DREAM方法，通过算法展开在零样本自监督设置下学习深度参数化的自适应正则化映射（用于TV和TGV），无需大规模配对数据，在二维图像去噪和动态MRI重建中验证了其高效性、可解释性和与监督方法相当的性能。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 传统的变分重建方法受限于固定正则化参数，灵活性不足；而深度学习方法虽然性能优越但缺乏可解释性且依赖大规模配对数据集。作者旨在结合两者的优势，在无需配对数据的条件下学习自适应正则化映射，同时保持算法的收敛性质和可解释性。

Method: 提出DREAM（Deep-Reparameterized Adaptive Regularization Maps）框架，通过算法展开（algorithm unrolling）将原始-对偶算法作为基础模块，用CNN参数化TV和TGV的正则化参数映射，并在零样本、自监督设置下训练，无需访问大规模配对数据集。

Result: 在二维图像去噪和动态MRI重建任务上，DREAM能高效学习正则化参数映射，所需权重更新次数远少于现有最优方法。CNN参数化起到隐式先验和降维作用，优于直接优化自适应参数。零样本与监督训练之间的性能差距比端到端深度学习方法更小，在动态心脏MRI重建中零样本训练甚至达到与监督模型相当的性能。

Conclusion: DREAM提供了一种理论上有保证、可解释的替代方案，尤其在难以获取参考数据的场景下，比纯深度学习方法更具吸引力，同时保留了展开算法的收敛性质。

Abstract: Adaptive regularization is an effective means of improving the flexibility of classical variational reconstruction methods while retaining their interpretability and mathematical structure. In this work, we propose an approach for learning deep-reparameterized adaptive regularization maps (DREAM) for Total Variation (TV) and Total Generalized Variation (TGV) through algorithm unrolling in a zero-shot, self-supervised setting, requiring no access to large paired datasets. We first validate DREAM on a two-dimensional image-denoising problem and then apply it to large-scale dynamic MRI reconstruction. The resulting adaptive models are theoretically grounded, as they retain the convergence properties of the primal-dual algorithm used as the building block of the unrolled architecture. Our results show that the regularization parameter maps can be learned efficiently, requiring substantially fewer weight updates than state-of-the-art methods to achieve comparable performance. We further demonstrate that the CNN parametrization of the maps acts as an implicit prior and provides effective dimensionality reduction, outperforming direct optimization of the adaptive parameters. Across both applications, the performance gap between supervised and zero-shot training is smaller for DREAM than for the end-to-end deep-learning methods considered for comparison. Remarkably, in dynamic cardiac MRI reconstruction, zero-shot training matches the performance of the supervised model. Moreover, the learned adaptive maps are highly interpretable, providing a compelling alternative to purely deep-learning-based methods when reference data are difficult to obtain.

</details>


### [111] [Transversality Conditions for Boundary Constraints Defined by Differential Equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04084)
*Isaac M Ross*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对边界条件由微分方程（带附加条件）定义的最优控制问题，开发了通用的初始和终端时间横截性条件。文中提出差分边界条件的数学定义、协调/非协调时钟时间以及弱伴随协向量等基础概念，并揭示了非协调时钟时间下弱伴随协向量与边界微分方程向量场正交的特殊情形，这与经典端点流形正交条件显著不同。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于N体系统中的轨迹优化问题，但更广泛地适用于非可积动力系统。当最优控制问题的边界条件不是由传统端点（点边界）定义，而是由微分方程定义时，经典的Pontryagin横截性条件不再适用，需要重新发展理论基础。

Method: 本文首先为微分边界条件建立了数学定义基础；随后引入协调/非协调时钟时间和弱伴随协向量的概念，在此新框架下推导出通用的一阶初始时间和最终时间横截性条件。在非协调时钟时间情形下，证明了一个特殊结果：弱伴随协向量与边界微分方程的向量场正交，区别于经典的端点流形正交条件。

Result: 推导出了通用的一阶横截性条件，适用于边界条件由微分方程定义的初始时间和最终时间。发现非协调时钟时间下存在特殊情形，即弱伴随协向量与边界微分方程向量场正交，与经典正交条件形成鲜明对比。定理具有通用性，已在三体问题的多个场景中应用（详见相关论文）。

Conclusion: 本文建立了微分边界条件下最优控制问题横截性条件的完整理论基础，这一贡献不仅服务于天体动力学中的轨迹优化，也适用于一般非可积动力系统，为更广泛的动态优化问题提供了新的数学工具。

Abstract: What are the transversality conditions for an optimal control problem when the boundary conditions are defined by differential equations? This seemingly bizarre question is motivated by trajectory optimization problems in the $N$-body system. The question, however, is more fundamental and goes beyond problems in astrodynamics to nonintegrable dynamical systems in general. The main contribution of this paper is the development of generic initial- and final-time transversality conditions for optimal control problems whose boundary conditions are defined in terms of differential equations with side conditions. The mathematical definition of differential boundary conditions are part of the foundations developed in this paper. To support the new fundamentals, the concept of coordinated/uncoordinated clock times and weak adjoint covectors are introduced. In the case of uncoordinated clock times, the new transversality conditions reveal that there exists a special situation where a weak adjoint covector is orthogonal to the vector field of the boundary differential equation. This condition is sharply different from the classical statement of orthogonality with respect to the endpoint manifold. The theorems developed in this paper are generic. An application of the theorems to several cases in the three-body problem are described in separate papers.

</details>


### [112] [Stronger Lower Bounds for (Non-)Anytime Acceleration of Gradient Descent](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04032)
*Minchan Jung,Hanseul Cho,Chulhee Yun*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对L-Lipschitz光滑凸目标函数下梯度下降（GD）的收敛率，在非任意时刻和任意时刻两种设置下分别建立了更强的下界：Ω(N^{-1.450})和Ω(N^{-1.184})，缩小了现有上下界之间的差距。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 梯度下降在固定步长下求解L-Lipschitz光滑凸目标函数的收敛率被认为是Θ(N^{-1})，但近期研究表明，通过非恒定、非自适应、确定性的步长调度可以加速vanilla GD。目前已知的最佳上界（非任意时刻O(N^{-1.271})，任意时刻O(N^{-1.119})）与最佳下界（非任意时刻Ω(N^{-1.635})，任意时刻Ω(N^{-1.241})）之间存在显著间隙，作者旨在通过建立更强的下界来缩小这些间隙。

Method: 作者通过构造更精细的反例或分析框架，推导出GD在两种设置下更强的收敛率下界。具体地，在非任意时刻设置下建立了Ω(N^{-1.450})的下界，在任意时刻设置下建立了Ω(N^{-1.184})的下界，分别较此前的最佳结果Ω(N^{-1.635})和Ω(N^{-1.241})有所改进（即下界数值增大，表示对性能的悲观估计更紧）。

Result: 在非任意时刻设置下建立了更强的收敛率下界Ω(N^{-1.450})，较此前最优下界Ω(N^{-1.635})提升了非指数级的紧致度；在任意时刻设置下建立了更强的收敛率屏障Ω(N^{-1.184})，较此前最优屏障Ω(N^{-1.241})也更为紧致，从而显著缩小了两个设置中上下界之间的差距。

Conclusion: 作者通过更强的下界结果，进一步收窄了关于梯度下降在光滑凸优化中步长调度加速现象的理论上界与下界之间的间隙，为理解非自适应确定性步长调度对vanilla GD的加速极限提供了更精确的刻画。

Abstract: The rate-optimal convergence rate of gradient descent (GD) with a fixed step-size is well known to be $Θ(N^{-1})$ for $L$-Lipschitz smooth convex objectives in the prior art in convex optimization. Surprisingly, several recent works show that we can accelerate vanilla GD by applying a nonconstant, nonadaptive, deterministic step-size schedule. The best-known upper bounds so far in the non-anytime & anytime setups are $O(N^{-1.271})$ [Altschuler and Parrilo, 2025, Grimmer et al., 2023] and $O(N^{-1.119})$ [Zhang et al., 2025], respectively. On the other hand, the best reported lower bounds (or barriers) up to date in the non-anytime & anytime setups are $Ω(N^{-1.635})$ and $Ω(N^{-1.241})$[Ye and Liu, 2026], respectively. We narrow these gaps by establishing stronger lower bounds for GD's convergence rate in both settings: $Ω(N^{-1.450})$ for the non-anytime rate bound and $Ω(N^{-1.184})$ for the anytime rate barrier.

</details>


### [113] [Factor-parity Hall sets and controllability](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04056)
*Karine Beauchard,Frédéric Marbach*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文提出一类称为"因子奇偶Hall集"的Hall集合，其元素分为好括号和坏括号。证明了好的括号可以在短时间内同时且任意地控制，从而为控制仿射系统的小时间局部可控性提供了充分条件；同时证明了每个坏括号都会产生可控性障碍，从而提供了一族必要条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 控制仿射系统的小时间局部可控性（STLC）是非线性控制理论的核心问题之一。作者希望给出统一的充分条件和必要条件，通过Hall集（李代数结构）的括号分类来刻画可控性。

Method: 作者引入了因子奇偶Hall集（factor-parity Hall sets）这一新概念，将Hall括号划分为好括号与坏括号两类。充分性部分借鉴了Kawski、Agrachev-Gamkrelidze和Krastanov的构造方法，证明好括号可在短时间内被同时且任意地操控；必要性部分则证明每个坏括号都会对可控性产生结构性障碍。

Result: 获得了小时间局部可控性的充分条件（基于好括号的任意操控能力）和必要条件（基于坏括号的障碍性质），形成了可控性判定的双向刻画框架。

Conclusion: 因子奇偶Hall集提供了一个统一的理论框架，将控制仿射系统的STLC充分条件和必要条件统一在同一括号分类之下，深化了Kawski等前人在该方向的研究成果。

Abstract: We introduce a class of Hall sets, which we call factor-parity Hall sets, whose elements split into good and bad brackets. We prove that the good brackets can be steered simultaneously and arbitrarily in small time, which yields sufficient conditions for the small-time local controllability of control-affine systems. This positive result draws on constructions of Kawski, Agrachev-Gamkrelidze and Krastanov. Conversely, we prove that each bad bracket generates an obstruction to controllability, hence a family of necessary conditions.

</details>


### [114] [Zero sum two-player differential game under three regimes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04100)
*Brahim El Asri,Magnoudéwa Paka*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究了具有三个不同制度的状态切换零和随机微分博弈，通过粘性解方法推导Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs方程的显式解，并分析制度间交互作用对值函数结构的影响。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在制度切换环境下的随机微分博弈中，值函数的显式求解通常十分困难。作者希望建立一个系统性框架，在已知切换区域定性结构的前提下，获得两人零和博弈的显式值函数表示与最优策略。

Method: 采用粘性解方法分析三制度切换系统中的Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs(HJBI)方程，考察不同制度间的相互作用如何影响值函数结构，并假设已知切换区域的定性结构，据此推导值函数的显式表达式。

Result: 针对切换系统的多种配置得到了值函数的显式解，并系统地刻画了两位玩家的最优策略以及不同制度间相互作用的机制。

Conclusion: 所提出的框架为三制度切换随机微分博弈提供了系统性的显式求解途径，在已知切换区域结构的前提下能够有效地表征值函数和最优策略。

Abstract: This paper investigates a two player zero-sum stochastic differential game characterized by three distinct regimes, reflecting the regime switching dynamics within the system. We aim to derive an explicit solution for a number of configurations of the switching system by means of the viscosity solutions approach. In particular, we analyze the associated Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman-Isaacs equations and examine how the interactions between the different regimes affect the structure of the value function. The proposed framework provides a systematic approach to characterize the optimal strategies of the two players and to obtain explicit representations of the value function un der suitable assumptions. We also illustrate how to derive the value functions in case we know the qualitative structure of switching regions.

</details>


### [115] [A Stochastic Riemannian Alternating Descent Ascent Method for Nonsmooth Composite Expectation Optimization on Riemannian Manifolds](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04116)
*Meng Xu,Bo Jiang,Ya-Feng Liu,Anthony Man-Cho So*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文针对一类黎曼流形上的非光滑复合期望优化问题，提出了一种名为StoRADA-RM的随机黎曼交替下降上升方法（带递归动量），在O(ε⁻³)迭代次数和O(ε⁻³)随机一阶预言机调用次数内达到ε-黎曼随机平稳点，复杂度为已知最优。




<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 一类黎曼非光滑复合期望优化问题在机器学习、信号处理和统计中广泛出现，但这些问题的求解算法在此之前缺乏最优的效率保证。该问题可结构化为极小极大重新表述，现有方法在迭代复杂度和随机一阶预言机（SFO）复杂度方面尚非最优。

Method: StoRADA-RM在每轮迭代中执行一步或多步黎曼随机梯度下降步，随后执行一个近端梯度上升步。为计算黎曼随机梯度，本文提出了一种无需向量传输（vector transport-free）的递归动量估计器，每次迭代仅需O(1)次随机梯度评估。该方法利用问题的极小极大重构，将黎曼下降与近端上升相结合，并通过递归动量技巧降低方差。

Result: 理论分析证明，StoRADA-RM在O(ε⁻³)次迭代内返回ε-黎曼随机平稳点，同时仅需O(ε⁻³)次SFO调用。这两项复杂度均是同类问题中已知最优的结果，其中SFO复杂度甚至与光滑非凸随机优化（一阶算法）的最优下界相匹配。在稀疏主成分分析和坐标无关稀疏估计的数值实验中，该方法展现了优越的性能。

Conclusion: StoRADA-RM为黎曼非光滑复合期望优化问题提供了首个达到最优迭代和SFO复杂度的算法，其SFO复杂度匹配下界表明算法在随机一阶信息利用上是渐近最优的，且数值实验验证了实际有效性。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider a class of Riemannian nonsmooth composite expectation optimization problems, which arises in various machine learning, signal processing, and statistics applications. Noting that these problems admit structured minimax reformulations, we propose an efficient algorithm, named stochastic Riemannian alternating descent ascent method with recursive momentum (StoRADA-RM), to tackle them. StoRADA-RM performs one or multiple Riemannian stochastic gradient descent steps and then a proximal gradient ascent step at each iteration. To compute the Riemannian stochastic gradient, we propose a vector transport-free recursive momentum estimator that requires only $O(1)$ stochastic gradient evaluations per iteration. We prove that StoRADA-RM returns an $ε$-Riemannian-stochastic-stationary point of a given problem in the said class in $O(ε^{-3})$ iterations while making $O(ε^{-3})$ calls to a stochastic first-order oracle (SFO). Both the iteration complexity and SFO complexity bounds are the best known in the literature for the said class of problems. The latter even matches the optimal lower bound for smooth nonconvex optimization with stochastic first-order algorithms. We then present numerical results on sparse principal component analysis and coordinate-independent sparse estimation to demonstrate the superior performance of our proposed method.

</details>


### [116] [Computation of Strong Solutions to Stochastic Variational Inequalities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04188)
*Yao Ji,Guanghui Lan,Jason Zhu*

Main category: math.OC

TL;DR: 本文研究单调变分不等式（VI）强解的累加正则化（AR）计算框架，在Lipschitz连续算子与随机无偏oracle假设下，实现了残差约简的近似最优随机oracle复杂度Ō(LD₀/ε + σ²/ε²·(log)³)，较已有O(σ²/ε⁴)有本质提升，并扩展至强单调与状态相关噪声模型设置。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 单调变分不等式是涵盖优化、博弈与均衡问题的统一数学框架。现有随机求解方法在残差约简指标下的复杂度高达O(σ²/ε⁴)，远未达到理论下界，尤其在噪声方差与精度要求交互的场景下效率低下。如何设计一种无需问题结构先验（如强单调模量）且复杂度近乎最优的通用框架，是亟待解决的核心问题。

Method: 该方法基于累加正则化（Accumulative Regularization, AR）思想构建通用VI求解框架。具体而言：(1) 在随机设置下使用无偏随机oracle并利用累加正则化逐步逼近单调问题的解；(2) 对于强单调VI，当强单调模量已知时可达到最优oracle复杂度，未知时仅按单调问题处理仍能保持近乎最优；(3) 提出状态相关噪声模型，适用于一般单调VI（可含非唯一解），并引入增强的随机算子外推（SOE）方法使得随机项复杂度只依赖解处的方差。

Result: AR在残差约简指标下达到约O((LD₀/ε)+(σ²/ε²)·(log)³)的oracle复杂度，匹配下界（至多差对数因子），较之现有O(σ²/ε⁴)大幅改进。强单调情形下，模量已知时取得最优复杂度；模量未知时仍接近最优。在状态相关噪声模型下，配合SOE方法亦达到近乎最优复杂度，随机项依赖解处方差而非最坏方差。

Conclusion: 本文提出的累加正则化框架为单调VI的随机求解建立了近乎最优的复杂度保证，统一了单调与强单调设置下的分析，并首次将状态相关噪声分析推广到非强单调、可含非唯一解的一般单调VI场景。该工作为后续VI算法研究提供了新的理论基准与分析范式。

Abstract: This paper studies the computation of strong solutions of monotone variational inequalities (VIs) with Lipschitz continuous operators. Building on the idea of accumulative regularization, we develop a general framework for VIs, with particular emphasis on stochastic settings. Under unbiased stochastic oracles with uniformly bounded variance $ σ^2$, AR computes an approximate solution with expected operator residual bounded by $\varepsilon$ using at most $ \widetilde{O}\left(\tfrac{LD_0}{\varepsilon}+\tfrac{ σ^2}{\varepsilon^2}(\log\tfrac{LD_0}{\varepsilon})^3\right) $ stochastic oracle calls, where $L$ is the Lipschitz constant and $D_0$ bounds the initial distance to the solution. It substantially improves the existing $\mathcal{O}( σ^2/\varepsilon^4)$ complexity for residual reduction and matches the lower bound up to logarithmic factors. For strongly monotone VIs, measured by the distance to the solution, AR achieves the optimal oracle complexity when the strong monotonicity modulus is known. By treating the problem as merely monotone, AR still achieves nearly optimal complexity without knowledge of this modulus. We further introduce a state-dependent noise model applicable to general monotone VIs with potentially nonunique solutions, extending state-dependent noise analysis beyond the strongly monotone setting. Under this model, AR, when equipped with an enhanced stochastic operator extrapolation (SOE) method, achieves nearly optimal complexity with the stochastic term depending on the variance at a solution.

</details>

# hep-ex [[Back]](#toc)

### [117] [Hadronic Mono-Z Dark Matter Sensitivity with Flow Matching on CMS Open Data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02923)
*Hitesh Rasineni,Bhavishya Chebrolu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文基于CMS Run 2015D公开数据，对强子单Z暗物质产生过程进行了预期灵敏度研究，使用条件流匹配连续归一化流对背景建模，并在三个简化模型基准下取得2.89σ、7.62σ和7.41σ的预期显著性，消融实验表明额外喷注运动学信息对于区分信号与背景至关重要。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 强子单Z衰变通道是探测暗物质产生的重要渠道，但传统方法在处理背景建模和喷注运动学特征时存在挑战，本文旨在展示利用公开数据和生成式模型进行此通道灵敏度研究的可行性。

Method: 使用CMS公开数据中1439523个强子单Z候选事件；通过条件流匹配连续归一化流（conditional flow-matching CNF）对背景进行建模，并在留出验证集上重加权至完整选择总体进行评估；采用哨兵插值处理未定义的角特征，持久化训练/验证分割索引以避免样本内评分偏差，并设置最小背景产量阈值（20事件）来选择工作点；对模拟信号应用离线触发代理。

Result: 基线分析在三个简化模型基准下分别取得2.89σ、7.62σ和7.41σ的预期显著性；消融研究中去除额外喷注详细运动学信息后，预期显著性降低53-71%，表明额外喷注拓扑结构携带了重要的判别能力。

Conclusion: 研究证明利用公开数据配合生成式归一化流背景建模可以实现预期的暗物质灵敏度探测；额外喷注运动学在强子单Z通道中具有显著的判别力，未来实际应用中应重视该特征的利用。

Abstract: We present a projected sensitivity study for hadronic mono-$Z$ dark-matter production using CMS Run~2015D HTMHT open data corresponding to 2.256382381~\invfb, from which 1{,}439{,}523 events satisfy the hadronic mono-$Z$ selection. Backgrounds are modelled with a conditional flow-matching continuous normalizing flow trained on the selected HTMHT events and evaluated on a held-out validation split reweighted to the full selected population. To mitigate artifacts from missing-object features and avoid in-sample scoring bias we apply sentinel imputation for undefined angular features, persist the train/validation split indices, and enforce a minimum reported background yield of 20 events when selecting the working point. A signal-side offline trigger proxy is applied to the simulated signal before scoring. Under this procedure the baseline analysis yields expected significances of 2.89$σ$, 7.62$σ$, and 7.41$σ$ for three simplified-model benchmarks. An ablation study that removes the detailed extra-jet kinematics reduces the expected significance by 53--71\%, indicating that extra-jet topology carries substantial discriminating power in the hadronic mono-$Z$ channel. These results are projected sensitivities (no unblinding performed); the limitations and reproducibility of the study are discussed in Sections limitations and reproducibility.

</details>


### [118] [Comment on arXiv:2608.29450: "First Measurement of Solar Neutrinos through Elastic Neutrino-Electron Scattering at the keV Scale"](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02950)
*Ke Han,Xiangdong Ji,Jianglai Liu,for the PandaX Collaboration*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文评论XENONnT合作组在太阳pp中微子通量测量中，其汇总图中存在一个重要遗漏。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: XENONnT合作组最近通过液体氙探测器中的中微子-电子弹性散射，测量了太阳pp中微子通量，但他们的汇总图（summary plot）中遗漏了某些关键信息，本文旨在指出这一遗漏。

Method: 作为评论性文章（comment），本文对XENONnT合作组发布的实验结果的汇总图进行审查，指出其中缺失的重要元素。

Result: 指出XENONnT汇总图中存在一个重要的遗漏项（具体遗漏内容需从正文获取）。

Conclusion: 需要对XENONnT的汇总图进行修正或补充，以完整呈现相关物理信息。

Abstract: The XENONnT Collaboration recently reported a new measurement of the solar \(pp\) neutrino flux via neutrino-electron elastic scattering using a multi-ton-scale liquid xenon detector. We comment here on an important omission in their summary plot.

</details>

Zhukov,J. Zhuo,Q. Zou,D. Zuliani,G. Zunica*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用LHCb探测器在13 TeV质心能量、5.4 fb⁻¹积分亮度下采集的质子-质子碰撞数据，测量了η和η'(958)介子的反常衰变分支比。其中η→μ⁺μ⁻的分支比被精确测定为(5.30±0.14±0.18±0.26)×10⁻⁶，成为迄今为止最精确的测量；对η'(958)→μ⁺μ⁻衰变的搜索则是首次实验尝试，并给出了90%置信水平的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: η和η'介子的反常衰变（如双轻子衰变和含π⁺π⁻的双轻子衰变）对检验低能量子色动力学、赝标量介子衰变机制以及可能的新物理贡献具有重要意义。此前这些衰变道的测量精度有限，部分衰变道（如η'(958)→μ⁺μ⁻）甚至从未被实验搜寻过，因此需要利用LHCb实验的大统计量数据进行测量。

Method: 利用LHCb实验在√s=13 TeV下采集的质子-质子碰撞数据（积分亮度5.4 fb⁻¹），测量η和η'(958)介子的四个反常衰变道。测量以φ(1020)→μ⁺μ⁻衰变作为归一化通道，通过拟合相应衰变道的信号/本底分布提取分支比数值或设置上限。不确定度来源包括统计不确定度、系统不确定度及外部归一化分支比引入的不确定度。

Result: 测量得到：B(η→μ⁺μ⁻)=(5.30±0.14±0.18±0.26)×10⁻⁶（迄今最精确测量）；B(η'(958)→μ⁺μ⁻)<1.9×10⁻⁷（90% CL，首次实验搜寻）；B(η→π⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻)<5.4×10⁻⁷（90% CL）；B(η'(958)→π⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻)=(2.73±0.14±0.13±0.14)×10⁻⁵。

Conclusion: 该工作完成了对η→μ⁺μ⁻分支比的最精确测量，并首次对η'(958)→μ⁺μ⁻衰变进行了实验搜寻，同时对η和η'的π⁺π⁻μ⁺μ⁻衰变道给出了新的测量或更严格的上限，为后续理论和实验研究提供了宝贵的数据基准。

Abstract: Branching fractions of $η$ and $η'(958)$ anomalous decays are measured using $pp$ collision data at a center-of-mass energy of $\sqrt{s}=13\,\text{TeV}$ collected by the LHCb experiment and corresponding to an integrated luminosity of $5.4\,\text{fb}^{-1}$. The measured values and upper limits are \begin{align*} \cal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-) &= (5.30\pm0.14\pm0.18\pm0.26)\times10^{-6} , \\ \cal{B}(η'(958)\toμ^+μ^-) &<1.9\times10^{-7}\textrm{ at 90% CL} , \\ \cal{B}(η\toπ^+π^-μ^+μ^-) &<5.4\times10^{-7}\textrm{ at 90% CL}, \\ \cal{B}(η'(958)\toπ^+π^-μ^+μ^-) &= (2.73\pm0.14\pm0.13\pm0.14)\times10^{-5} , \end{align*} where the uncertainties are, respectively, statistical, systematic, and due to the external branching fraction of the $φ(1020)\toμ^+μ^-$ decay, which is used for normalization. This marks the first experimental search for the $η'(958)$ decay, and the most precise measurement of the branching fraction $\cal{B}(η\toμ^+μ^-)$ to date.

</details>


### [120] [Measurement of Differential Cross Sections and Integrated Luminosity using Proton-Proton Elastic Scattering with HADES at T = 4.53 GeV and T = 1.60 GeV](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02989)

Prościński,A. Prozorov,W. Przygoda,B. Ramstein,N. Rathod,J. T. Rieger,J. Ritman,A. Rost,A. Rustamov,S. Sahu,P. Salabura,J. Saraiva,K. Scharmann,N. Schild,K. Schönning,E. Schwab,F. Seck,I. Selyuzhenkov,J. Smyrski,M. Sobiella,S. Spies,A. Sreejith,A. Strach,H. Ströbele,J. Stroth,P. Subramani,K. Sumara,O. Svoboda,K. Swientek,J. Taylor,P. E. Tegner,P. Tlusty,M. Traxler,S. Treliński,I. C. Udrea,F. Ulrich-Pur,V. Wagner,A. A. Weber,C. Wendisch,D. Wielanek,P. Wintz,A. Władyszewska,H. P. Zbroszczyk,M. Zieliński,P. Zumbruch*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文报道了基于升级后的HADES探测器在T=4.53 GeV和T=1.60 GeV质子束轰击液氢靶上开展的弹性质子-质子散射研究。这是升级后探测器（含为PANDA实验设计的前向探测器和丝管径迹平面）的首次测量成果。利用弹性散射的两体运动学约束研究了探测器对准、束流对准并确定了束流能量；通过与国际上已有实验重叠的动量转移平方t区间归一化确定了实验亮度。从大范围t中提取的微分截面得到斜率参数B在4.53 GeV和1.60 GeV下分别为8.98±0.3 (GeV/c)^{-2}和7.3±0.1 (GeV/c)^{-2}。该结果补充了世界弹性质子-质子散射数据库，为理论建模提供输入并为未来同能区实验的亮度标定提供参考。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 弹性质子-质子散射是研究强子间相互作用基本性质的重要探针，世界数据库中在同能区的数据仍有不足。同时，升级后的HADES探测器需要首次全面验证其性能，并通过弹性散射这一运动学约束良好的过程来标定探测器对准、束流参数和实验亮度，为后续物理分析奠定基础。

Method: 利用HADES升级后的探测器系统（包含为PANDA实验设计的前向探测器与丝管径迹平面），以T=4.53 GeV和1.60 GeV质子束轰击液氢靶，收集弹性质子-质子散射数据。利用弹性散射两体运动学约束研究探测器与束流对准并标定束流能量及实验亮度；在大范围四动量转移平方t内确定微分截面，并从中提取斜率参数B。

Result: 在T=4.53 GeV和1.60 GeV下分别提取得到斜率参数B = 8.98±0.3 (GeV/c)^{-2}和7.3±0.1 (GeV/c)^{-2}。微分截面在广泛的t范围内被精确确定，实验亮度通过与全球已有实验数据在重叠t区间比较来标定。

Conclusion: 该工作提供了升级后HADES探测器的首次物理测量，补充了世界弹性质子-质子散射数据库，为理论模型提供了重要输入，同时为未来同能区实验的亮度确定提供了可靠参考基准。

Abstract: This work presents an investigation of elastic proton-proton scattering based on data collected with the recently upgraded HADES detector using beams of protons with kinetic energies of $T = 4.53$ GeV and $T = 1.60$ GeV impinging on a liquid hydrogen target. This is the first measurement emerging from the upgraded detector setup, including a Forward Detector with straw-tube tracking planes designed for the PANDA experiment. The well-constrained two-body kinematic relations of elastic scattering have been exploited to study the detector alignment, the beam alignment, and to provide the final measurement of the beam energy. In addition, the recorded luminosity of the experiment has been determined by normalizing the HADES data in a range of the squared four-momentum transfer $t$ that overlaps with measurements by previous experiments worldwide. Furthermore, the differential cross sections have been determined over a large range of $t$ from which the slope parameter $B$ is extracted, yielding $B = 8.98 \pm 0.3$ (GeV/$c$)$^{-2}$ and $7.3 \pm 0.1$ (GeV/$c$)$^{-2}$ at $T = 4.53$ GeV and $1.60$ GeV, respectively. This contribution to the world database on elastic proton-proton scattering provides input to theoretical modeling and serves as a reference for luminosity determinations by future experiments in the same energy range.

</details>

Zhuge,J. H. Zou,J. Zu*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 利用BESIII探测器在BEPCII对撞机上收集的质心能量3.773 GeV、积分亮度7.93 fb⁻¹的e⁺e⁻湮灭数据，测量了D⁰→K_S⁰ω和D⁰→K_L⁰ω的绝对衰变分支比，分别为(11.79±0.19±0.26±0.47)×10⁻³和(10.84±0.14±0.23±0.44)×10⁻³，并测得K_S⁰-K_L⁰分支比不对称度R=(4.2±1.0±0.9±2.8)%。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 精确测量D介子衰变的分支比及其K_S⁰-K_L⁰不对称性，以检验标准模型中的CP违逆（间接CP破坏）预言，并为理解D⁰衰变为K⁰ω过程的强子动力学提供实验输入。

Method: 基于BESIII探测器收集的ψ(3770)→DD̄样本（积分亮度7.93 fb⁻¹），利用单标记（single-tag）技术对D⁰→K_S⁰ω和D⁰→K_L⁰ω衰变进行绝对分支比的测量；通过两分支比的差值构造R(D⁰,K_S,L⁰ω)不对称度观测量，并考虑来自非共振过程D⁰→π⁺π⁻ωK_S,L⁰的干涉效应作为第三类不确定度来源。

Result: 测得B(D⁰→K_S⁰ω)=(11.79±0.19±0.26±0.47)×10⁻³，B(D⁰→K_L⁰ω)=(10.84±0.14±0.23±0.44)×10⁻³；K_S⁰-K_L⁰分支比不对称度R=(4.2±1.0±0.9±2.8)%，R值与零在约1.4倍标准差内一致（统计与系统不确定度主导下）。

Conclusion: 该测量首次在D⁰→K_S,L⁰ω衰变通道中给出精确的绝对分支比和K_S⁰-K_L⁰不对称度，结果与CP守恒预言在测量精度范围内一致，为后续更精确的CP破坏敏感性检验提供了关键的实验基准。

Abstract: Based on $e^+ e^-$ annihilation data corresponding to an integrated luminosity of 7.93~$fb^{-1}$ collected at a center-of-mass energy of 3.773 GeV with the BESIII detector at the BEPCII collider, the absolute branching fractions of the decays $D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω$ and $D^0 \to K_{L}^{0} ω$ are measured to be $(11.79 \pm 0.19 \pm 0.26 \pm 0.47) \times 10^{-3}$ and ($10.84 \pm 0.14 \pm 0.23 \pm 0.44) \times 10^{-3}$, respectively.
  The $K_{S}^{0}- K_{L}^{0}$ branching-fraction asymmetry of these two decays is $R(D^0,K_{S,L}^{0} ω) = \frac{\mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω) - \mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{L}^{0}ω)}{\mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{S}^{0} ω) + \mathcal{B}(D^0 \to K_{L}^{0} ω)} =(4.2 \pm 1.0 \pm 0.9 \pm 2.8)\%$.
  Here, the first uncertainties are statistical, the second systematic, and the third arise from the interference between $D^0 \to K_{S,L}^{0} ω$ and the non-resonant $D^0 \to π^+ π^- π^0 K_{S,L}^{0}$ processes.

</details>


### [122] [Multi-tesla operation of high-temperature superconducting cavities for accelerated axion dark matter searches](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03879)
*Danho Ahn,Jinsu Kim,Seongtae Park,Jiwon Lee,Ohjoon Kwon,Woohyun Chung,HeeSu Byun,Sergey Uchaikin,Arajan Ferdinand van Loo,Yasunobu Nakamura,Dojun Youm,SungWoo Youn,Yannis K. Semertzidis*

Main category: hep-ex

TL;DR: 本文提出一种基于应变控制、机械剥离的REBCO超导带材构建的新型轴向三维腔体结构，用于轴子射电频率腔体探测实验。通过去除有损耗的金属基底、利用铜稳定层作为导电背衬，并将纵向装配间隙转化为截止波导，有效抑制了接缝射频泄漏。在8T磁场下实现了5.4 GHz腔体Q0=1.4×10^7，较铜腔提升两个数量级；同时开发了2.3 GHz可调、体积最大化的探路者腔体，部署于CAPP-PACE实验中，获得180 mK噪声温度和5倍Q值提升，累积扫描速率加速约8.4倍。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 轴子暗物质探测需要多特斯拉磁场下的射频腔体，但标准超导体在强磁场中会产生严重的涡旋耗散，而铜腔则受反常趋肤效应限制（Q≲10^5），两者均构成技术瓶颈。

Method: 采用应变控制、机械剥离的REBCO（稀土钡铜氧）超导带材，构建极对极（pole-to-pole）三维腔体架构。选择性去除有损耗金属基底，同时利用铜稳定层充当"导电背衬"，将纵向装配间隙转化为截止频率以下的波导，从而抑制跨接缝射频泄漏。采取双轨策略：一是用5.4 GHz谐振腔表征材料高场本征潜力；二是面向实际实验开发2.3 GHz可调、体积最大化的探路者腔体。

Result: 5.4 GHz腔体在8T磁场中实现无载品质因数Q0=1.4×10^7，比常规铜腔基线高出两个数量级。2.3 GHz探路者腔体在CAPP-PACE实验中部署，实现180 mK噪声温度和5倍Q值增强，累计带来约8.4倍扫描速率加速。

Conclusion: 该REBCO基腔体架构成功克服了高场超导涡旋耗散与铜腔反常趋肤效应的双重限制，为轴子暗物质探测提供了兼具高性能与实际集成可行性的技术路径。

Abstract: Axion haloscopes use radio-frequency cavities immersed in a magnetic field to search for dark-matter axions, which could resolve two central puzzles in fundamental physics: the strong charge-parity problem in quantum chromodynamics and the nature of dark matter. Multi-tesla fields trigger axion-to-photon conversion but induce severe vortex dissipation in standard superconductors, whereas copper cavities are limited by the anomalous skin effect ($Q \lesssim 10^5$). Here, we overcome these barriers by introducing a pole-to-pole 3-dimensional cavity architecture constructed from strain-controlled, mechanically delaminated rare-earth barium copper oxide (REBCO) tapes. By selectively stripping the lossy metallic substrate while utilizing the copper stabilizer as a "conductive backing" we convert the longitudinal assembly gaps into waveguides below cutoff, effectively suppressing cross-seam RF leakage. Employing a two-track strategy, we first unveiled the intrinsic high-field potential of the material with a 5.4 GHz resonant cavity, achieving an unloaded quality-factor ($Q_0$) of $1.4 \times 10^7$ in an 8 T magnetic field, exceeding conventional copper baselines by two orders of magnitude. Second, prioritizing practical haloscope integration, we engineered a tunable, volume-maximized 2.3 GHz pathfinder cavity. Deployed in the Pilot Axion Cavity Experiment at the Center for Axion and Precision Physics Research (CAPP-PACE), this system achieved a 180 mK noise temperature and a 5-fold $Q$ enhancement over copper, cumulatively delivering a $\sim$8.4-fold scan-rate acceleration.

</details>
