# astro-ph.HE [[Back]](#toc)

### [123] [Unlocking the QPE Mystery: Star-Disk Collisions in Realistic AGN Disks](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03011)
*Zhaohuan Zhu,Xiaoshan Huang,Yan-Fei Jiang,Shunquan Huang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过二维多频率辐射流体动力学（RH）模拟研究了恒星与吸积盘碰撞机制如何解释准周期喷发（QPEs）的观测趋势，发现更真实的、膨胀的环核盘结构（z/r约1）使弓形激波在不同时刻突破，产生持续的软X射线热发射，其持续时间约为轨道周期的10%，且更倾斜的碰撞产生更长更亮的喷发，可解释观测到的占空比和光度-周期关系等多项QPE趋势。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 准周期喷发（QPEs）是低质量星系核中观测到的周期性软X射线爆发，展现出两个显著趋势：爆发持续时间约为复发周期的10-20%，且更长持续时间的爆发更明亮。恒星与超大质量黑洞周围吸积盘的极端质量比旋进（EMRI）轨道碰撞被认为是一种有前景的理论，但尚不清楚该模型如何复现这些观测趋势，因此需要更精细的模拟研究。

Method: 作者采用二维、多频率辐射流体动力学（RH）模拟方法研究恒星-吸积盘碰撞过程。关键改进在于采用了比以往更真实的环核盘结构——该结构取自先前亚爱丁顿吸积盘的辐射MHD模拟结果（表面密度Σ∝r²），而非假设的简化盘模型。通过模拟弓形激波在稠密膨胀的盘大气中（z/r约1）的产生、传播与突破过程，计算X射线热发射的时变特征。

Result: 模拟结果表明：1）厚实膨胀的盘大气使得弓形激波的不同部位在不同时刻突破，产生延长的热辐射，激波在z/r约0.7处突破；2）X射线耀发的持续时间由激波穿过光学厚盘所需的传播时间决定，约为轨道时间尺度的10%，可复现观测到的占空比；3）更倾斜的恒星-盘相互作用产生更长、更明亮的耀发；4）真实AGN盘模型中表面密度Σ∝r²给出碰撞能量E∝P^(2/3)，可解释光度-周期趋势，尤其是较弱的QPEs。

Conclusion: 更真实的环核盘结构（膨胀的厚盘大气）可以解释多项QPE观测趋势，包括占空比、光度-持续时间关联和光度-周期关系；反之，QPE观测也可用于约束吸积盘的结构性质，表明QPEs可作为探测超大质量黑洞周围吸积盘结构的工具。

Abstract: Quasi-periodic eruptions (QPEs) are luminous, recurring soft X-ray outbursts observed in the nuclei of low-mass galaxies. They display two remarkable trends: outburst durations are $\sim$10-20% of the recurrence timescale, and longer bursts are more luminous. A promising theory that naturally explains the quasi-periodicity invokes collisions between a star on an extreme mass-ratio inspiral (EMRI) orbit and the accretion disk around the supermassive black hole. However, it remains unclear how this model reproduces the observed trends. We therefore carry out two-dimensional, multi-frequency radiation hydrodynamic (RH) simulations of star--disk collisions. Crucially, we adopt a more realistic circumnuclear disk structure from previous Radiation MHD simulations of sub-Eddington accretion disks. We find that the thick, puffed-up disk atmosphere, extending to $z/r\sim1$, causes different portions of the bow shock to break out at different times, producing prolonged thermal emission as the shock emerges through the breakout surface at $z/r\sim0.7$. The X-ray flare duration is set by the shock propagation time through the optically thick disk--a $\sim$10% of the orbital timescale, reproducing the observed duty cycle. A more oblique star-disk interaction yields a longer, more luminous flare. The realistic AGN disk models also exhibit a surface density $Σ\propto r^2$, giving a collisional energy $E\propto P^{2/3}$ that may explain the luminosity--period trend, especially for the weaker QPEs. Overall, we suggest that a more realistic circumnuclear disk structure can explain several observed QPE trends-and QPEs may, in turn, constrain the disk structure.

</details>


### [124] [Multi-epoch Detection of an Ultra-fast Inflow in ESP 39607: Evidence for an Accretion Cascade](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03019)
*Alessandro Peca,Michael J. Koss,Roberto Serafinelli,C. Megan Urry,Claudio Ricci,Keigo Fukumura,Massimo Gaspari,Elias Kammoun,Alessia Tortosa,Giulia Cerini,Peter G. Boorman,Richard Mushotzky,Quirino D'Amato*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用XRISM、XMM-Newton和NuSTAR对Seyfert 2星系ESP 39607（z=0.201）进行联合观测，在4.7和4.9 keV处探测到红移的Fe XXV He-α和Fe XXVI Ly-α吸收线，对应于速度约0.16c的超快内流（UFI）。结合三个历元的观测，该UFI的联合探测显著性达5.3σ，表明物质持续补充形成吸积"级联"而非单一长寿云团，质量内流率约为0.3-1.4 M☉/yr，可为活动星系核提供可观的吸积物质。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解活动星系核（AGN）中心超快内流（UFI）的性质及其对黑洞吸积的贡献，特别是检验内流是否持续存在以维持AGN所需吸积物质，从而揭示AGN吸积物质的物理来源和动力学过程。

Method: 采用多波段联合观测策略：使用XRISM/Resolve的高分辨率光谱（首次用于此类探测）结合XMM-Newton和NuSTAR数据。通过多种连续谱模型和不同的分析方法检测吸收特征，测量速度约0.16c的超快内流。应用光致电离建模确定电离参数（log ξ≈3.7-3.8）和柱密度（log NH,abs≈23.2-23.8），并与之前两个NuSTAR历元数据对比以评估时间变异性。通过推断吸积半径（R≈49-77 Rg）和动力学时标来讨论UFI的物理本质。

Result: 在4.7和4.9 keV（观测系）探测到与Fe XXV He-α/Fe XXVI Ly-α对应的速度0.16c的红移超快内流，单次探测显著性约3-3.7σ，联合三个历元后达5.3σ。光致电离建模给出电离参数log ξ≈3.7-3.8，柱密度log NH,abs≈23.2-23.8（依赖金属丰度假设）。内流位于R≈49-77 Rg处，动力学时标仅数天，但观测跨越时间至少2.2年（源静止系），表明物质被持续补充形成吸积"级联"。质量内流率估算为0.3-1.4 M☉/yr，与黑洞吸积率之比约0.2-1.0。

Conclusion: ESP 39607中的超快内流在多个历元中被持续探测到，而非单一瞬态事件。多年代际的持续存在性支持吸积"级联"模型，即内流物质被不断补充。该内流可能为AGN提供约20%-100%所需吸积物质，在推动中心黑洞吸积过程中起重要作用。这为理解AGN中物质从吸积盘外向内流动的物理机制及其与超大质量黑洞增长的联系提供了新的观测约束。

Abstract: We present simultaneous XRISM, XMM-Newton, and NuSTAR observations of ESP 39607, a Seyfert 2 galaxy at $z = 0.201$. XRISM/Resolve reveals two absorption features near 4.7 and 4.9 keV in the observed frame, consistent with redshifted Fe XXV He-$α$/Fe XXVI Ly-$α$ absorption from gas inflowing at $v_{\rm in} \simeq 0.16c$. The high velocity identifies the absorber as an ultra-fast inflow (UFI), which is detected at $\sim 3$-$3.7σ$ across different methods and continuum models. Photoionization modeling yields $\log ξ/{\rm erg\,s^{-1}\,cm} \simeq 3.7$-$3.8$ and a column density $\log N_{\rm H,abs}/{\rm cm^{-2}} \simeq 23.2$-$23.8$, the latter depending on the assumed metallicity. The inflow velocity and line properties are consistent with those reported from two earlier NuSTAR epochs, indicating that similar inflowing material was present over a baseline of at least 2.2 yr in the source rest frame. Across all three epochs, the combined detection significance of the UFI is $5.3σ$. Given the dynamical timescale of a few days at the inferred radius, $R \simeq 49$-$77\,R_{\rm g}$, the multi-year evidence favors a scenario in which the inflow is continuously replenished, forming an accretion "cascade", rather than a single long-lived cloud. With an estimated mass inflow rate of $\dot{M}_{\rm in} \simeq 0.3$-$1.4\,M_\odot$ yr$^{-1}$, depending on metallicity, and a ratio $\dot{M}_{\rm in}/\dot{M}_{\rm acc} \simeq 0.2$-$1.0$, the inflow could supply a substantial fraction of the accretion needed to power the central active galactic nucleus.

</details>


### [125] [Single-pulse reanalysis of the 2024 Vela glitch and new observations of PSR~J0437$-$4715 and PSR~J1644$-$4559](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03056)
*Carlos O. Lousto,Ruby Shrestha,Ezequiel Zubieta,Susana B. Araujo Furlan,Guillermo Gancio,Federico García,Santiago del Palacio,Ryan Missel,Linwei Wang*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 阿根廷PuMA合作组利用机器学习单脉冲流水线（Isolation Forest异常剔除、β-VAE去噪、自组织映射聚类）对Vela脉冲星2024年大耀变及两颗其他射电脉冲星进行脉冲级再分析。Vela在耀变前后八天的平均轮廓稳定在1%内，而排除恢复项会因折叠频率误差模拟出高达55%的伪展宽；PSR J0437-4715的聚类分解提示约3.5倍的聚类定时精度提升。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 系统监测南天黑暴脉冲星（特别是Vela），验证机器学习单脉冲管道的可推广性，并在全新观测数据上测试其性能。相比先前窄带ETTUS接收机结果，Vela数据由400 MHz带宽ROACH后端提供，带宽提升七倍，旨在验证并拓展之前的脉冲聚类与耀变行为分析，同时探索对毫秒脉冲星（脉冲星定时阵列目标）在定时精度上的潜在改进。

Method: 采用脉冲逐脉冲（pulse-per-pulse）重分析方法。处理流水线包含三层机器学习组件：Isolation Forest孤立森林用于脉冲异常剔除、β-变分自编码器（β-VAE）用于去噪、Self-Organizing-Map自组织映射用于脉冲聚类。Vela耀变周围八天数据按4-簇与6-簇分解；耀变时间解中纳入跳变及两个指数恢复项以检查平均轮廓稳定性。对于PSR J0437-4715，先按最高峰主导（peak-dominance）得分保留顶部10%脉冲再行5-簇分解。

Result: Vela的4簇与6簇分解复现窄带接收机此前的行为——高幅值簇在相位上更早、更窄、更偏斜、数量更少。耀变跳变与两项指数恢复项纳入定时解后，八天平均轮廓稳定至1%（耀变前后宽度变化-0.5±0.9%）；若忽略恢复项，折叠频率误差在10^-7量级即可伪造成高达55%的耀变后展宽。PSR J1644-4559的簇几乎仅幅值有差异，与散射主导轮廓预期一致。PSR J0437-4715中，仅保留峰主导得分最高的10%脉冲可使信噪比翻倍；5簇分解得到比平均轮廓窄3.6±0.4倍的相位有序窄群，预示约3.5倍的簇基定时精度提升（待后续论文确认）。

Conclusion: 该工作证明了基于机器学习的单脉冲聚分析在宽带数据上的可复现性与可扩展性，再次验证了Vela耀变期间脉冲轮廓中簇行为的一致性。定时分析警示研究者在耀变期间忽略恢复项会在低至10^-7的折叠频率误差下引入显著的伪轮廓展宽。对毫秒脉冲星PSR J0437-4715的初步结果提示聚类技术有望显著提升脉冲星定时阵列目标的定时精度，值得在后续论文中进行更严格验证。

Abstract: The Pulsar Monitoring in Argentina (PuMA) collaboration systematically monitors southern glitching pulsars, maintaining high-cadence single-pulse records of the Vela pulsar. We present a pulse-per-pulse reanalysis of the 2024 major glitch of Vela (PSR~J0835$-$4510) with the 400~MHz-bandwidth ROACH backend of the Argentine Institute of Radioastronomy, and extend our machine-learning single-pulse pipeline---Isolation Forest outlier rejection, $β$-Variational-AutoEncoder denoising, and Self-Organizing-Map clustering---to new observations of PSR~J1644$-$4559 and the millisecond pulsar PSR~J0437$-$4715. For Vela, the 4- and 6-cluster decompositions of the eight days bracketing the glitch reproduce, with seven times the previous bandwidth, the behavior found with the narrow-band ETTUS receivers: higher-amplitude clusters peak earlier in phase, are narrower, more skewed, and less populated. With the glitch jump and its two exponential recovery terms included in the timing solution, the mean profile is stable to 1\% across all eight days (width change $-0.5\pm0.9$\% from pre- to post-glitch); omitting the recovery terms would mimic a post-glitch broadening of up to 55\% through a folding-frequency error at the $10^{-7}$ level. The clusters of PSR~J1644$-$4559 differ almost exclusively in amplitude, as expected for a scattering-dominated profile. For PSR~J0437$-$4715, retaining the 10\% of pulses with the highest peak-dominance score doubles the signal-to-noise ratio, and a five-cluster decomposition yields narrow, phase-ordered groups a factor $3.6\pm0.4$ narrower than the average profile, suggesting a $\sim$3.5-fold improvement in cluster-based timing precision for this pulsar-timing-array target, to be confirmed in a follow-up paper.

</details>


### [126] [Thermal and non-thermal emission from supermassive black hole circumbinary disks: Disks, Coronae, Streams, and Cavities](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03140)
*Chris Nagele,Julian H. Krolik,Brooks E. Kinch,Jeremy D. Schnittman,Scott Noble*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过对吸积双超大质量黑洞(SMBH)系统的HARM3D模拟快照进行后处理，首次自洽计算了其在10^6–10^8 M⊙质量范围内的辐射光谱，发现约40%的观测光度以软X射线幂律(Γ=2.3)形式辐射，并识别出两种具有π/2相位差的方位角变化模式：X射线连续谱约10%的变化（源于螺旋激波温度不对称性）和Fe Kα等效宽度约25%的下降（源于团块对视线遮挡）。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探测SMBH双星系统的电磁信号是多信使天体物理学的基石之一，可补充脉冲星计时阵列和LISA的引力波观测。然而，尽管已有大量模拟研究双SMBH系统吸积的辐射时变（bolometric luminosity），但光谱预测相对匮乏，且缺乏超越简单发射模型的自洽光谱计算，这正是本文拟填补的空白。

Method: 作者对20 M分离、以0.01 Eddington吸积的HARM3D模拟快照进行后处理。针对黑洞质量分别为10^6、10^7和10^8 M⊙的三种情形，基于稳态辐射转移、热平衡和电离平衡自洽求解辐射光谱，同时包含所有相关相对论效应以及发射和吸收过程。

Result: 1) 尽管大部分bolometric光度由磁盘热辐射贡献，但双体四极矩抽空形成的低密度区域产生了丰富的X射线辐射，观测光度中约40%表现为软X射线幂律(Γ=2.3)。2) 识别出两种方位角变化模式：X射线连续谱约10%的变幅源自磁盘螺旋激波气体温度的不对称性；Fe Kα等效宽度约25%的下降发生在视线部分被团块遮挡内盘时。3) 两种效应具有相同周期但相位差约π/2，周期在典型AGN质量和20 M分离下约为数天到数周。

Conclusion: 该研究表明双SMBH双星系统可以在紫外/光学热辐射之外产生显著的软X射线功率谱辐射，并呈现出可观测的、具有特征相位关系的方位角调制信号。这些X射线变率及其与Fe Kα等效宽度变化的相位关系可作为识别此类系统的独特电磁特征，为多信使探测提供重要诊断工具。

Abstract: The search for electromagnetic signals from supermassive black hole (SMBH) binary systems is one of the cornerstones of multi-messenger astrophysics, complementing gravitational wave observations of such systems by pulsar timing arrays and LISA. Although extensive simulations have been run to understand the time variability of the bolometric luminosity from accreting binary SMBH systems, comparatively few spectral predictions have been made, and none that go beyond simple emission models. In this paper, we post-process a \texttt{HARM3D} simulation snapshot of a binary at $20 M$ separation accreting at 0.01 Eddington. For black hole masses $10^6$, $10^7$, and $10^8\, M_\odot$, we self-consistently solve for the radiated spectrum on the basis of time-steady radiation transfer, thermal balance, and ionization equilibrium, including all relevant relativistic effects as well as emission and absorption processes. Although most of the bolometric luminosity is radiated thermally by the disk, the low density regions evacuated by the binary's quadrupole moment produce copious X-rays, with $\sim40\%$ of the observed luminosity in a soft X-ray power law ($Γ= 2.3$). We identify two modes of observed azimuthal variation. The X-ray continuum varies by $\sim10\%$ due to an underlying asymmetry in the gas temperature of spiral shocks in the disk. The Fe~K$α$ equivalent width dips by $\sim25\%$ when the line of sight to the inner disk is partially obscured by the lump. These two effects share the same period and are $\sim π/2$ out of phase; the period is order days to weeks for typical AGN masses and a 20$M$ separation.

</details>


### [127] [Observational selection effects on radio pulsars are minimal for masses, but significant for orbits and spins](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03157)
*Lisa V. Drummond,Katerina Chatziioannou,Emmanuel Fonseca*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该论文重新审视了通过射电计时测量的银河系脉冲星双星质量分布，通过模拟射电脉冲星系统和计时观测，将观测选择效应（可探测性和可测量性）纳入层级化群体推断，联合推断脉冲星质量、伴星质量、轨道偏心率、轨道周期和自旋周期的内在天体物理分布。研究发现选择效应对质量分布影响很小，但显著影响其他参数：圆轨道与偏心系统比率从观测的1:1修正为天体物理的2:1，真实群体偏向更长的轨道周期和自旋周期。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 通过射电计时测量的银河系脉冲星质量分布（双峰结构，峰值约1.3和1.6太阳质量，截止约2太阳质量）受观测选择效应影响，但现有目录中脉冲星质量测量存在可探测性和可测量性两方面的选择偏差，需要校正这些效应以恢复内在的天体物理分布。

Method: 模拟射电脉冲星系统及其计时观测，追踪系统进入观测数据集的过程，估计双星和脉冲星参数对选择的影响；将选择函数纳入层级化群体推断，联合推断脉冲星质量、伴星质量、轨道偏心率、轨道周期和自旋周期等参数的内在分布。

Result: 选择效应对质量分布影响最小，但对其他参数的推断影响显著。圆轨道与偏心系统的观测比率从1:1修正为选择校正后的2:1；真实群体相比观测结果偏向更长的轨道周期和自旋周期。

Conclusion: 观测选择效应在脉冲星群体推断中不可忽略，尤其影响轨道参数和自旋参数的推断，但对质量分布（关乎致密物质和双星形成过程的关键诊断）影响有限，因此此前基于质量分布双峰结构的物理结论仍然稳健。

Abstract: The masses of Galactic pulsars in binaries measured through radio timing point to structure in the mass distribution with implications for dense matter and astrophysical formation processes: a bimodal shape with peaks at ${\sim} 1.3\,M_\odot$ and ${\sim} 1.6\,M_\odot$, with a cutoff above ${\sim}2\,M_\odot$. However, this observed population of radio pulsars is shaped not only by intrinsic pulsar properties, such as birth masses, binary evolutionary pathways and dense-matter constraints, but also by observational effects. In existing catalogs of pulsar mass measurements, observational selection effects influence (i) which systems are detected in the first place (detectability) and (ii) which systems yield well-measured masses (measurability). In this work, we revisit the radio pulsar population distribution in the context of observational effects associated with pulsar radio timing. Simulating systems of radio pulsars and timing observations, we track the process by which a given system ends up in an observational dataset and estimate the impact of the binary and pulsar parameters. We fold this selection function into hierarchical population inference and extract the intrinsic astrophysical distribution of pulsars observed with radio timing. We jointly infer the populations of pulsar mass, companion mass, orbital eccentricity, orbital period and pulsar spin period. Selection effects have minimal impact on the mass distributions, but more significantly affect other parameters. The ratio of circular-to-eccentric systems shifts from an observed $1\!:\!1$ to an astrophysical, selection-corrected $2\!:\!1$ ratio, while the true population is shifted toward longer orbital and spin periods than observed.

</details>


### [128] [Radiative Efficiency Enhancement by Electromagnetic Energy Dissipation in Strongly Magnetized Supercritical Accretion Flows around Kerr Black Holes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03250)
*Hiroyuki R. Takahashi,Ken Ohsuga,Ryohei Kobayashi,Akihiro Inoue,Yuta Asahina,Akira Nukada,Taisuke Boku*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文通过广义相对论辐射磁流体动力学(GRRMHD)模拟，研究了黑洞自旋和磁通量对超临界吸积流中电磁能量耗散和辐射输运的影响。研究发现高自旋黑洞的MAD态模型中，Blandford-Znajek机制提取的电磁能量在黑洞附近盘面处耗散，显著增强辐射效率（η_rad=0.60），并使有效俘获半径缩小至r_trap=2.5r_g（接近ISCO半径），远小于零自旋和非磁性状态的情况。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 超临界吸积流的辐射特性对理解高光度活动星系核和潮汐瓦解事件等天文现象至关重要。然而，黑洞自旋和磁通量（即MAD与SANE两种磁吸积状态）如何影响电磁能量耗散及辐射输运尚未被系统探究，以往的研究往往忽略了磁通量这一关键参数对辐射性质的影响。

Method: 采用广义相对论辐射磁流体动力学（GRRMHD）数值模拟方法，对MAD（磁阻截盘）和SANE（标准正常演化）两种磁吸积态的吸积流进行模拟，并系统变化黑洞自旋参数与初始磁通量水平，通过对比不同参数组合下的能量耗散分布和辐射输运行为来揭示其物理机制。

Result: 在高自旋MAD模型中，BZ机制提取的部分电磁能量在黑洞附近盘面耗散，显著贡献了辐射能量并增强了光度。时间平均辐射效率达到η_rad=0.60，远高于非旋转黑洞情况的0.088和弱磁通（SANE）情况的0.21。有效俘获半径r_trap=2.5r_g，接近ISCO半径，明显小于非旋转情况的12r_g和SANE态的8.5r_g。

Conclusion: 黑洞上积累的磁通量能显著影响超临界吸积流的辐射特性。在解释观测到的光度和光谱时，除黑洞质量、自旋和质量吸积率外，磁通量也应作为重要参数纳入考量。

Abstract: We investigate how black hole spin and the amount of magnetic flux affect electromagnetic energy dissipation and radiation transport in supercritical accretion flows. For this purpose, we perform general relativistic radiation magnetohydrodynamic simulations of MAD and SANE accretion flows with different black hole spins. In the high-spin MAD model, we find that a fraction of the electromagnetic energy extracted by the Blandford--Znajek mechanism is dissipated near the disk surface in the vicinity of the black hole. This dissipation significantly contributes to the generation of radiative energy and enhances the luminosity. The time-averaged radiative efficiency reaches $η_{\rm rad}=0.60$, which is much larger than $0.088$ for the non-spinning black hole case and $0.21$ for the weak-magnetic-flux case, corresponding to the SANE state. As a result, the effective trapping radius, defined as the radius at which the outward radiative luminosity becomes equal to the inward radiative luminosity, is $r_{\rm trap}=2.5r_{\rm g}$, comparable to the ISCO radius. This value is significantly smaller than $12r_{\rm g}$ for the non-spinning case and $8.5r_{\rm g}$ for the SANE state. These results suggest that the amount of magnetic flux accumulated on the black hole can affect the radiative properties of supercritical accretion flows and should therefore be considered, in addition to black hole mass, spin, and mass accretion rate, when interpreting observed luminosities and spectra.

</details>


### [129] [The Jet Properties and Accretion Regime for the Blazar Sequence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03435)
*Qingchen Long*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究通过分析136个FSRQ、64个LBL、39个IBL和105个HBL的宽波段谱指数平面，发现blazar在α_ro-α_ox平面上呈现"∠"形演化序列，即(FSRQs+LBLs)→IBLs→HBLs，其中射电多普勒因子、喷流功率和爱丁顿比率沿此轨迹递减。分布主要由吸积率和不同波段的多普勒聚束效应比(δ_i/δ_j)调控，而非喷流形状或谱指数。FSRQs+LBLs是耦合辐射有效吸积的强喷流源，HBLs是耦合辐射无效吸积的弱喷流源，IBLs为过渡态。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索blazar在宽波段谱指数平面(α_ro-α_ox)上呈现的双峰分布的物理起源，这是当前仍未解决的重要问题。

Method: 编译了一个包含136个FSRQ、64个LBL、39个IBL和105个HBL的大样本，基于α_ro-α_ox平面分析各类源的谱指数分布特征，并结合射电多普勒因子、喷流功率和爱丁顿比率等物理参数研究其演化关系。

Result: 发现(FSRQs+LBLs)→IBLs→HBLs遵循"∠"形演化序列，伴随射电多普勒因子、喷流功率和爱丁顿比率的递减。α_ox分布差异较小，而α_ro和α_rx的分布差异显著。分布主要由吸积率和多普勒聚束效应在波段间的比值δ_i/δ_j调控，其中δ_i/δ_j起关键作用，而喷流形状和谱指数作用次要。

Conclusion: FSRQs+LBLs为强喷流源耦合辐射有效吸积，喷流被加速到离核心较远距离并在射电区仍保持相对论性速度；HBLs为弱喷流源耦合辐射无效吸积，喷流在光学区开始减速并在射电区呈亚相对论速度；IBLs作为两者之间的过渡态。

Abstract: The puzzling bimodality displayed by blazars on the broad-band spectral indices ($α_{\rm ro}-α_{\rm ox}$) plane is still an open question. To investigate its physical origin, we compiled a sample comprising 136 flat-spectrum radio quasars (FSRQs), 64 low-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects (LBLs), 39 intermediate-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects (IBLs), and 105 high-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects (HBLs). Our results show that (FSRQs+LBLs)$\rightarrow$IBLs$\rightarrow$HBLs follow a \reflectbox{$\angle$}-shaped evolutionary sequence on the $α_{\rm ro}-α_{\rm ox}$ plane, with the average 5\,GHz radio Doppler factor, jet power, and accretion Eddington ratio decreasing along this \reflectbox{$\angle$}-shaped track. Additionally, the distributions of $α_{\rm ox}$ exhibit only slight differences among blazar subclasses, whereas those of $α_{\rm ro}$ and $α_{\rm rx}$ differ significantly. Our results can be naturally explained if the distribution of blazars on the $α_{\rm ro}-α_{\rm ox}$ plane is regulated by the accretion rates and the Doppler beaming effect across different bands ($δ_i^{q_i}/δ_j^{q_j}$). However, $δ_i/δ_j$ plays a pivotal role in the distribution, while jet shape ($q=2(3)+α_ν$) and spectral indices ($α_ν$) play a minor role. Our results suggest that FSRQs+LBLs are strong-jet sources coupled with radiatively efficient accretion, and their jets are accelerated to a larger distance from the core and still maintain relativistic speeds down to the radio region; HBLs are weak-jet sources coupled with radiatively inefficient accretion, and their jets may begin to decelerate in the optical region and exhibit sub-relativistic speeds as they propagate to the radio region. IBLs are regarded as transitional objects between FSRQs+LBLs and HBLs.

</details>


### [130] [Gamma-Ray Bursts and Optical Brightness: Insights from Swift Satellite](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03452)
*Istvan I. Racz,Yasmin Nehme,Lajos G. Balazs*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 该研究利用生存分析方法（可靠性建模）分析了Swift卫星观测期间测量到的200多个伽马射线暴（GRBs）的光学性质数据，发现此前报道的持续时间、伽马注量与峰值通量对光学亮度分布的显著影响不再成立，表明这些关联可能不如先前结论所示的那样显著，需要更多研究来理解光学亮度与伽马性质之间的潜在机制。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Swift卫星同时测量伽马射线暴的伽马、X射线和光学数据，但许多情况下光学波段仅能确定上限值而非精确观测值。因此需要生存分析工具来处理这些存在边界限制的数据集，以检验光学亮度与伽马射线暴中央引擎属性之间的关联是否如先前研究所示的那样显著。

Method: 采用生存分析（可靠性建模）方法处理具有上限束缚的样本数据。该方法适用于数据点仅有上界或下界而非精确观测值的情况，能充分利用这些边界信息进行统计分析。研究分析了超过200个Swift卫星观测期间的GRB数据点。

Result: 此前研究认为GRB的持续时间、伽马注量和峰值通量对光学亮度分布有显著影响（可能源于GRB中央引擎射束能量），但经过对200多个数据点的重新分析后，这一关联不再明显，与先前结论产生分歧。

Conclusion: 先前报道的伽马性质对光学亮度的显著影响可能被高估，并非如之前推导的那样重要。需要进一步研究以更好地理解决定光学亮度与伽马射线性质之间关系的物理过程。

Abstract: During the examination of optical properties of gamma-ray bursts (GRBs) measured during the life of the Neil Gehrels Swift Observatory satellite, significant discoveries were made. While the satellite measures gamma, X-ray, and optical data simultaneously, in many cases, only an upper limit is established in the optical domain. Hence, survival analysis, a.k.a. dependability modelling, was necessary, serving as a tool for studying samples where some cases are bound only by the upper limit, as mentioned earlier. This method is used to analyse datasets that may have upper or lower bounds rather than precise observations, but nevertheless contain relevant information. It was previously established that the duration, gamma fluence, and peak flux all have a substantial effect on the optical brightness distribution, probably due to the energy of the beam from the GRB's central engine. However, after analysing more than 200 data points, we no longer see this association. This disagreement shows that the previously reported impact may not be as significant as previously derived, needing more research to better understand the underlying processes that determine the optical brightness in connection to gamma-ray properties.

</details>


### [131] [KM3NeT/ARCA stacking search for high-energy neutrino point sources: the case of ultra-luminous infrared galaxies and blazars](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03609)
*The KM3NeT Collaboration*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用KM3NeT/ARCA中微子望远镜336天数据（19-21个探测单元配置，1544个中微子候选径迹事件），对超高光度红外星系、高同步峰BL Lac天体（HSP）和极端blazar三类候选天体源开展了堆叠搜索，未发现统计学显著的高能天体物理中微子超出，并设置了90%置信水平的上限。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该研究旨在探索高能中微子与特定天体源类别（ULIRG、HSP BL Lacs及极端blazar）之间的关联，以检验这些被认为可能是宇宙射线加速器或极高能中微子发射源的候选天体是否确实产生可探测的天体物理中微子信号。

Method: 采用堆叠搜索方法，基于KM3NeT/ARCA探测器在336天观测期内（配置19-21个探测单元）收集的1544个径迹状中微子候选事件。对75个ULIRG源假设常规幂律谱进行信号建模；对232个HSP BL Lac天体和88个极端同步峰辐射源，则利用受多波段观测数据约束的数值工具计算其预期中微子通量，随后进行统计堆叠分析以检验超出大气背景的显著性。

Result: 三个候选源目录（ULIRG、HSP BL Lacs、极端blazar）均未在堆叠分析中发现相对于大气背景的统计学显著中微子超出，并给出了90%置信水平的通量上限。

Conclusion: 结果表明在当前观测数据下，这三类候选源群体尚未展现出可被探测的堆叠中微子发射特征，为理解这些天体作为高能中微子源的贡献提供了新的观测约束。

Abstract: A stacking search for high-energy astrophysical neutrino emission is presented using three candidate source populations: ultra-luminous infrared galaxies and two categories of blazars, specifically high-synchrotron-peaked BL Lacs and extreme blazars. The analysis is based on 336 days of data from the Astroparticle Research with Cosmics in the Abyss component of the KM3NeT research infrastructure (KM3NeT/ARCA), collected with detector configurations comprising 19-21 detection units, yielding 1544 selected track-like neutrino candidates. The sample of ultra-luminous infrared galaxies consists of 75 sources, for which conventional power-law spectra are assumed in modelling their signal emission. The blazar samples comprise 232 high-synchrotron-peaked BL Lac objects and 88 extreme synchrotron-peaked emitters, respectively. Their expected neutrino fluxes are obtained from numerical tools constrained by multi-wavelength observational data. No statistically significant excess above the atmospheric background is found for the three catalogues and 90% confidence level upper limits are set.

</details>


### [132] [NuSTAR View of the 2025 Mini-Outburst of the Black Hole X-ray Binary GRS 1739-278: Spectral and Timing Evolution in the Soft State](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03624)
*Arshad Hussain,Umananda Dev Goswami*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 对银河系黑洞候选体GRS 1739-278在2025年9月微型爆发衰减期的三次NuSTAR观测进行了定时与光谱分析。源处于软态/盘主导态，谱参数（内盘温度、内半径）稳定，未探测到周期信号；联合光谱拟合揭示了显著的约10-12 keV曲率特征（疑似盘反射/返回辐射），支持高自旋黑洞在低且稳定衰减的吸积率下于典型软态工作的图像。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该论文旨在通过高灵敏度X射线观测（NuSTAR）对GRS 1739-278在2025年最新一次迷你爆发衰减阶段的谱定时特性进行详细刻画，以检验源是否处于典型软态、内盘是否锚定在最内稳定圆轨道（ISCO），并利用内半径测量对黑洞自旋进行约束。

Method: 对三次NuSTAR观测进行定时分析（功率谱与Lomb-Scargle周期图）与光谱分析；采用FPMA+FPMB联合拟合，模型为吸收的盘黑体+热康普顿化连续谱，另加一个强力证认（11.7-15.0σ）的宽曲率特征（约10-12 keV）作为盘反射/返回辐射的经验代理。

Result: 源全程处于软盘主导态，3-79 keV计数率从112.4降至99.4 cts s⁻¹，硬度比约0.14-0.16，分数变率由3.2%降至1.1%，无周期/准周期信号。内盘温度从1.021稳定冷却至1.002 keV，内半径约27.7 km（约0.95 R_ISCO），恒定在0.3%以内。4-30 keV未吸收光度从1.35×10³⁷降至1.15×10³⁷ erg s⁻¹（0.67%至0.57 L_Edd）。

Conclusion: GRS 1739-278在2025年迷你爆发衰减期表现出典型软态特征：内盘固定在ISCO处、吸积率低且稳定下降；测量到的内半径与高自旋黑洞（R_in≈0.95 R_ISCO）的预期一致。

Abstract: We present a dedicated timing and spectral analysis of three NuSTAR observations of the Galactic black hole candidate GRS 1739-278 obtained during the decay of its 2025 mini-outburst (September 18-30, 2025). The source is found in a soft, disk-dominated state throughout, with a declining $3$-$79$ keV count rate (from $112.4$ to $99.4$ cts s$^{-1}$), a low and only mildly evolving hardness ratio ($\approx0.14$-$0.16$) and a fractional variability that drops from $3.2\%$ to $1.1\%$; no periodic or quasi-periodic signal is detected in the power spectrum or the Lomb-Scargle periodogram of any observation. Joint FPMA+FPMB spectral fits with an absorbed disk-blackbody plus thermal-Comptonization continuum, together with a broad, high-significance ($11.7$-$15.0σ$) curvature feature near $10$-$12$ keV (an empirical proxy for a strong disk-reflection/returning-radiation component) describe the $4$-$30$ keV spectra well ($χ^2/{\rm dof}=1.04$-$1.15$). The inner-disk temperature cools steadily from $1.021\pm0.001$ to $1.002^{+0.003}_{-0.002}$ keV while the disk normalization and hence the derived inner radius, $R_{\rm in}\simeq27.7$ km $\simeq0.95\,R_{ISCO}$ remains constant to within $0.3\%$, consistent with a disk anchored at the innermost stable circular orbit throughout. The unabsorbed $4$-$30$ keV luminosity declines from $1.35\times10^{37}$ to $1.15\times10^{37}$ erg s$^{-1}$ ($0.67\%$ to $0.57 L_{Edd}$). These results of the spectral-timing characterization of the 2025 mini-outburst are broadly consistent with a high-spin black hole accreting at a low, steadily declining rate in the canonical soft state.

</details>


### [133] [The Physics of the Nebular Phase in Black Hole X-ray Binaries: Modelling V404 Cygni](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03714)
*A. Ambrifi,T. Muñoz-Darias,J. A. Fernández-Ontiveros,J. Casares,D. Mata Sánchez,J. H. Matthews*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用光致电离代码Cloudy对银河系黑洞X射线暂现源爆发末期的星云相进行了定量建模，成功再现了巴尔默衰减和Hα等值宽度等关键可观测特征。模型表明星云相在较宽的密度和电离参数范围内保持稳定，支持该阶段源自先前抛射物在X射线照射减弱后膨胀并复合的物理解释，且抛射物总质量可能超过爆发期间吸积的总质量。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 银河系黑洞暂现源在爆发末期会经历一个星云相阶段，该阶段于2015年天鹅座V404爆发中首次被发现，以极强的光学发射线和巴尔默衰减显著增强为特征。理解这一阶段对于揭示抛射物性质及其与吸积过程的关联至关重要。

Method: 使用光致电离模拟代码Cloudy，对黑洞暂现源爆发末期的星云相进行建模。采用简化假设描述辐照连续谱和发射气体，通过改变气体密度（10^10.5至10^12.5 cm^-3）和电离参数（logξ从-1.5到1）等参数空间，定量复现巴尔默衰减和Hα等值宽度等关键观测特征，并与实际观测光谱进行定性比较。

Result: 模拟定量再现了巴尔默衰减和Hα等值宽度等关键可观测指标，并在多个量级的密度和电离参数范围内定性重现了观测光谱。巴尔默衰减在宽参数范围内保持大致稳定，支持星云相能在中等程度的抛射物膨胀和冷却过程中持续存在的观测结论。通过多种方法约束了星云相的一般性质，提出该阶段由团块状、部分电离的抛射物产生，其总质量可能超过爆发期间吸积的质量。

Conclusion: 星云相可由先前发射的团块状部分电离抛射物在X射线辐照减弱后膨胀和复合来解释。这一阶段在较宽的物理条件下均能稳定存在，且抛射物的质量可能超过了吸积质量，表明爆发过程中大部分物质可能被以外流形式抛射而非被黑洞吸积。

Abstract: Some of the most extreme galactic black hole transients exhibit a nebular phase at the end of their outbursts. This stage was first identified in the 2015 outburst of V404 Cygni, when it dominated the optical spectrum for ~4 days. It is characterised by exceptionally strong optical emission lines, together with a significant increase in the Balmer decrement. The emission features show broad wings reaching velocities consistent with outflow speeds derived from prominent P-Cygni profiles that preceded the nebular phase. They are thus interpreted as arising from previously launched ejecta that expand and recombine as X-ray irradiation declines.
  In this work, we modelled this phase using the photoionisation code Cloudy. Adopting simple assumptions for the irradiating continuum and emitting gas, we quantitatively reproduced key observables, such as the Balmer decrement and the H$α$ equivalent width, and qualitatively reproduced the observed spectra. Our simulations show that the Balmer decrement remains roughly stable within the observed range across orders-of-magnitude changes in the density and ionisation parameter, spanning $n_\mathrm{H} \sim 10^{10.5}-10^{12.5}\,\text{cm}^{-3}$ and $-1.5 \lesssim \logξ\lesssim 1$. This supports the scenario in which the nebular phase can persist through moderate ejecta expansion and cooling, consistent with observations. Finally, we used different approaches to constrain the general properties of the nebular phase, which we propose is produced by clumpy, partially ionised ejecta whose total mass likely exceeds the mass accreted during the outburst.

</details>


### [134] [A catalogue of insights from the fourth LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA observing run](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03859)
*Christopher P L Berry,Daniel Williams*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文综述了LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA（LVK）合作组第四次观测运行（O4）期间引力波探测的发现与进展，涵盖最新目录中约100个新探测事件、显著的单次发现，以及通过群体层面分析在致密双星天体物理分布、广义相对论检验和宇宙膨胀速率等方面取得的重要约束与证据。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 引力波科学已从单次探测的时代进入近乎日常发现的阶段，LVK合作组的主要科学成果集中体现在探测目录中。系统回顾O4运行期间的发现，有助于梳理该领域在单源发现与群体统计层面取得的最新突破，并展望未来观测计划。

Method: 通过对LVK合作组O4运行期间发布的最新探测目录进行回顾性综述，综合评估单次显著探测事件（如最强信号及新型源类型）和基于不断扩大的样本量进行的群体级统计分析，涉及对致密双星并合的天体物理分布、广义相对论的可能偏离以及宇宙膨胀速率的推断。

Result: O4期间最新目录各包含约100个新探测；发现了迄今最为响亮的引力波信号以及前所未见的源类型；群体分析揭示出多个源亚群的证据；在致密双星分布、广义相对论检验和哈勃常数测量方面获得了新的约束。O4最终目录预计于2026年12月发布。

Conclusion: 引力波观测正在从个案发现向群体统计推断转变，对理解大质量恒星演化和并合双星的形成提供了独特探针。随着更多观测数据的积累以及未来更高灵敏度的观测运行，引力波科学将持续深化。

Abstract: Over the last decade, gravitational-wave science has developed from an era where a single detection was a Nobel-winning achievement to an (almost) everyday occurrence. The LIGO-Virgo-KAGRA (LVK) Collaboration's flagship scientific results are now their catalogues of detections, which include a comprehensive set of analysis results characterising the astrophysical properties of sources, and their implications for fundamental physics and cosmology. We review discoveries from the fourth observing run (O4) of the LVK network. The most recent catalogues each include $\sim100$ new detections. Among these new detections are remarkable single discoveries: the loudest signals detected so far, and sources unlike those seen before. However, significant advances in the field are coming from population-level analyses, leveraging the increasing sample size to infer constraints on the astrophysical distribution of merging compact binaries, potential deviations to general relativity, and the expansion rate of the Universe. Analyses of the latest data have revealed evidence for multiple subpopulations of sources. These observations provide a unique probe into the evolution of massive stars and the formation of coalescing binaries. Our understanding of gravitational-wave signals and their sources will continue to deepen with more observations. The final catalogue from O4}is due for release in December 2026, and future observing runs with even greater detector sensitivity are planned. These will bring even larger catalogues, and additional loud signals, continuing to advance gravitational-wave science.

</details>


### [135] [Ultra-compact twin stars with hybrid equations of state from bosonic dark matter](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03964)
*Ishfaq Ahmad Rather,Sarah Louisa Pitz,Jürgen Schaffner-Bielich*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究具有强一阶夸克物质相变并包含自相互作用玻色子暗物质流体的致密星性质。发现暗物质的加入显著改变了质量-半径构型的稳定性，消除了强子分支与混合分支之间的不稳定段，使稳定序列连续；同时发现两类稳定的超致密天体（UCOs）：暗物质晕类（f_DM≳0.9）和暗物质核心类（f_DM≲0.02），二者可通过正常物质的表面红移区分。此外还发现了"终极双星"（相同质量和可见半径但暗物质含量不同的混合星解），可通过潮汐形变率和表面红移区分。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索暗物质与夸克物质一阶相变对致密星结构稳定性的联合影响，并寻求通过X射线和引力波观测来探测暗物质存在及其性质的新途径。

Method: 理论上研究包含强一阶夸克物质相变和自相互作用玻色子暗物质流体的致密星，利用广义相对论结构方程（TOV方程）计算质量-半径关系，分析稳定性判据、表面红移和潮汐形变率等可观测量的特征。

Result: 1) 暗物质改变了质量-半径构型的稳定性，消除了不稳定段使稳定序列连续；2) 发现两类稳定的超致密天体（C≥1/3）：暗物质晕类（f_DM≳0.9，红移<0.45）和暗物质核心类（f_DM≲0.02，红移0.73-0.77）；3) 发现"终极双星"解——质量和可见半径相近但暗物质含量不同，潮汐形变率和表面红移不同。

Conclusion: 未来对超致密中子星的X射线和引力波测量（超出允许中子星范围的半径和质量）可以探测暗物质的存在及其性质，同时验证夸克物质一阶相变的存在。

Abstract: The properties of compact stars with a strong first-order phase transition to quark matter and with an additional fluid of self-interacting bosonic dark matter (DM) are studied. We find that the inclusion of DM changes considerably the stability of mass-radius configurations relative to the naive one-fluid criterion. For compact star configurations with similar masses and different radii, so-called twin stars, the presence of DM removes the unstable segment between the hadronic and the hybrid branch, so that the stable mass-radius sequence becomes continuous after the onset of the phase transition to quark matter. We furthermore find stable ultra-compact objects (UCOs), defined by a total compactness $C = M_\text{tot}/R_\text{grav} \ge 1/3$. We observe two distinct classes of UCOs: a DM-halo class with $f_\text{DM} \gtrsim 0.9$, and a DM-core class at $f_\text{DM} \lesssim 0.02$. The two classes can be separated by the surface redshift of the normal matter, which reaches $z=0.73$--$0.77$ for the DM-core class and stays below $0.45$ for the DM-halo class. Finally, we find hybrid star solutions of 'ultimate twins' with similar mass and visible radius, but different dark matter content, leading to different tidal deformabilities and surface redshifts. Future X-ray and gravitational measurements of ultra-compact neutron stars with radii and masses outside the allowed neutron star range can thereby probe the presence and the properties of DM in addition to a first-order phase transition to quark matter.

</details>


### [136] [Sgr A* as a Galactic PeVatron: Multimessenger Signatures of the Magnetic Penrose Process](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04051)
*Marina Cermeño,Pedro De la Torre Luque,Cristina Fernández-Suárez,Viviana Gammaldi,Enrique Mier-Alonso,María J. Rodríguez,Miguel Á. Sánchez-Conde,Jaume Zuriaga-Puig*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文研究了人马座A*（Sgr A*，银河系中心超大质量黑洞）中的磁彭罗斯过程（MPP），通过计算吸积流中的中子产生谱并追踪其克尔时空中的轨迹，证明该过程可将质子加速至PeV能量，并预测了相应的伽马射线和中微子辐射，为多信使观测提供了可检验的MPP模型。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 虽然磁彭罗斯过程（MPP）被认为是磁化克尔黑洞提取旋转能的最有效机制之一，但此前缺乏从核反应运动学出发的系统研究，特别是对吸积流中子的产生及中子进入能层参与MPP的定量分析。作者希望将MPP从理论构想提升为可通过多信使观测检验的可观测机制，并验证Sgr A*作为银河系PeVatron候选体的可行性。

Method: 作者从核反应运动学出发直接计算吸积流中的中子产生谱，然后在克尔时空中追踪中子轨迹以确定能到达能层并经历MPP的中子群体。在此基础上推导加速质子的能谱，并计算逸出质子在中央分子云区（Central Molecular Zone）通过强子相互作用产生的伽马射线与中微子发射谱。最后与H.E.S.S.、HAWC、IceCube等观测数据及SWGO、CTAO、KM3NeT/ARCA和IceCube-Gen2的灵敏度进行比较。

Result: Sgr A*通过MPP可将质子加速至PeV能量，支持其作为银河系PeVatron候选体。预测的伽马射线通量具有独特的光谱特征，对H.E.S.S.和HAWC观测到的高能发射有不可忽略的贡献；中微子通量低于IceCube测得的弥散银河成分，但可能贡献银河系中心的高能发射。预测信号均在SWGO计划灵敏度范围内，部分模型仅低于CTAO标称灵敏度数倍，KM3NeT/ARCA和IceCube-Gen2可提供互补检验。

Conclusion: 磁彭罗斯过程是可在可观测层次验证的黑洞旋转能提取机制，为视界尺度物理与多信使观测之间建立了直接联系，该方法可推广至其他磁化黑洞系统。

Abstract: The magnetic Penrose process (MPP) is one of the most efficient mechanisms for extracting rotational energy from a magnetized Kerr black hole (BH), enabling charged particles to reach very-high energies. We investigate this process in Sagittarius A* (Sgr A*), the supermassive BH at the Galactic center (GC), focusing on its ultra-high-efficiency regime, in which neutrons undergo beta decay inside the BH ergosphere. As a key novelty, we compute the neutron production spectrum in the accretion flow directly from the nuclear reaction kinematics and follow neutron trajectories in the Kerr spacetime to determine the population that reaches the ergosphere and undergoes the MPP. From this population, we derive the spectrum of accelerated protons and show that Sgr A* can accelerate them up to PeV energies, strengthening its interpretation as a candidate Galactic PeVatron. We further compute the gamma-ray and neutrino emission produced through hadronic interactions of the escaping protons in the Central Molecular Zone. The predicted gamma-ray fluxes exhibit distinctive spectral features that could provide an observational signature of the MPP and may constitute a non-negligible contribution to the very-high-energy emission detected by H.E.S.S. and HAWC. The associated neutrino fluxes remain below the diffuse Galactic component inferred by IceCube, but may still contribute to the high-energy emission from the GC. Remarkably, the predicted signals lie within the projected sensitivity of SWGO for all scenarios considered here and, for some models, only a factor of a few below the nominal CTAO sensitivity, while KM3NeT/ARCA and IceCube-Gen2 will provide complementary tests. Our results establish the MPP as an observable mechanism for extracting BH rotational energy, providing a direct connection between horizon-scale physics and multimessenger observations that can be extended to other magnetized BHs.

</details>


### [137] [A targeted search for fast radio bursts from magnetars in elliptical galaxies](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04118)
*S. Paine,D. R. Lorimer,S. Sirota,G. M. Doskoch,S. Tabassum,M. Flanagan,J. Lituchy,A. Stone,J. W. Kania,M. Bhardwaj,S. Mehta,M. A. McLaughlin,B. Kharel,D. Agarwal,M. P. Surnis*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文利用绿岸望远镜（GBT）的GREENBURST单脉冲搜索管线在VEGAS后端上，对五个邻近大质量椭圆星系的球状星团系统进行了L波段（1.4 GHz）FRB高灵敏度定向搜索。在M49方向上探测到FRB 20240817A（脉宽15 ms、SNR 13、DM约983 cm⁻³ pc），但贝叶斯空间关联与前景电子柱密度修正（银河系+室女座星系团内介质+M49热气体晕合计约300-400 cm⁻³ pc）强烈反对其物理关联，判定为背景事件。同时发现来自M49方向的一簇三个低显著性重复脉冲（DM 150-160 cm⁻³ pc）。基于未发现明确宿主定位的暴发，对单个活跃源给出R₀<0.028 hr⁻¹的联合固有暴发率上限（M49积分上限<1.0 hr⁻¹），进一步压低此前上限，表明动力学球状星团中的磁星形成效率低于先前假设。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理论预测认为近邻巨型椭圆星系相关的球状星团是探测磁星源FRB的理想环境，但此前缺乏对该类系统的高灵敏度系统性搜索，需要对这些环境中FRB源的物理性质和暴发率进行实际约束。

Method: 利用GBT上的GREENBURST单脉冲搜索管线和VEGAS后端，对五个本地大质量椭圆星系开展L波段（1.4 GHz）FRB定向高灵敏度巡天。对探测到的候选暴发使用贝叶斯空间关联框架评估宿主关联概率，并仔细修正银河系、室女座星系团内介质及M49热气体晕的前景电子柱密度贡献来判别物理连接。最后考虑各系统球状星团扩展分布的被观测比例，对单个活跃源的固有暴发率施加联合上限约束。

Result: 在M49方向发现FRB 20240817A（脉宽15 ms、SNR 13、DM约983 cm⁻³ pc），但前景电子柱密度贡献的仔细核算（合计约300-400 cm⁻³ pc）强烈否定其与M49的物理关联，判为背景事件；另发现三个低显著性重复脉冲（DM 150-160 cm⁻³ pc）。联合固有暴发率上限为R₀<0.028 hr⁻¹，M49积分速率上限<1.0 hr⁻¹。

Conclusion: 该搜索未发现与目标椭圆星系球状星团明确物理关联的FRB，所得暴发率上限比此前更低，暗示球状星团内磁星的形成效率可能被先前低估的低效性所限制。

Abstract: Motivated by predictions that globular clusters associated with nearby giant elliptical galaxies are prime environments for detecting fast radio bursts (FRBs) from magnetars, we report on a targeted high-sensitivity search across five local massive elliptical galaxies. We utilized the GREENBURST single-pulse search pipeline and the VEGAS backend on the Green Bank Telescope (GBT) to conduct an L-band (1.4 GHz) survey for FRBs in each target environment. We discovered FRB 20240817A in the direction of M49 (NGC 4472) which has a pulse width of 15 ms, a signal-to-noise ratio of 13, and a dispersion measure (DM) of $983~\rm{cm}^{-3}$ pc. Although a Bayesian spatial association framework initially flags M49 as the host, carefully accounting for the foreground electron column contributions from the Milky Way and both the Virgo intracluster medium and M49's hot gaseous halo ($\sim300-400~\rm{cm}^{-3}$ pc combined) strongly disfavours a physical connection. We conclude that FRB 20240817A is a background event. We also identified a repeating cluster of three low-significance pulses from the direction of M49 at a physically plausible DM of $150-160~\rm{cm}^{-3}$ pc. Accounting for the fraction of each target's extended globular cluster distribution observed, the absence of unambiguous, host-localized bursts allows us to place a joint upper limit on the intrinsic burst rate of a single active source within these systems of $R_0 < 0.028~\rm{hr}^{-1}$ (and an integrated rate limit of $<1.0$~hr$^{-1}$ for M49), lowering previous upper limits and suggesting that magnetar formation within dynamical clusters is less efficient than previously assumed.

</details>


### [138] [Low-angular-momentum accretion shocks can power weak-to-moderate X-ray flares from SgrA*](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04145)
*Samik Mitra,Bożena Czerny,Michal Zajaček,Zach Sumners*

Main category: astro-ph.HE

TL;DR: 本文检验人马座A*（Sgr A*）的低角动量磁化吸积流中的驻立激波是否为X射线耀斑提供能量来源，通过半解析跨磁声激波解估算激波后的动能，并与25年Chandra X射线耀斑目录对比。

本文检验人马座A*（Sgr A*）的低角动量磁化吸积流中的驻立激波是否可作为观测到的X射线耀斑的能量来源，通过半解析计算能量效率并与25年Chandra数据比较。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Sgr A*的X射线耀斑在持续时间、辐射通量和光度上跨度很大，但其起源仍未有定论。作者希望探究低角动量磁化吸积流中的驻立激波能否作为这些辐射事件的能量储备来源。

Method: 使用半解析跨磁声激波解，估算激波下游动态流动中可用的动能E_sh。对每个理论解，计算所需的能量效率ε=E_data/E_sh（E_data为观测到的每个耀斑的辐射能量），并分别对弱自旋和强自旋情况进行分析。在基准吸积率和辐射热修正下进行系统比较。

Result: 弱耀斑需要ε约10^-3到10^-2的效率，而中等耀斑需要百分之几的效率。弱自旋与强自旋情况下的弱到中等耀斑能量预算基本不变。驻立激波的动能储备足以解释观测到的弱至中等耀斑群体，但最强的耀斑事件可能需要通过额外磁能耗散渠道来实现更高效率。

Conclusion: 低角动量磁化吸积流中的驻立激波能够为Sgr A*的弱至中等X射线耀斑提供充足的能量来源，但最强耀斑需要更高效率，可能涉及额外的磁能耗散机制。

Abstract: X-ray flares from Sgr~A* span broad ranges in duration, fluence, and luminosity, but their origin remains unsettled. We test whether standing shocks in low-angular-momentum magnetized accretion flows can provide a viable energy reservoir to account for these events. Using semi-analytic trans-magnetosonic shock solutions, we estimate the kinetic energy available in the downstream post-shock flow and compare it with the 25-year \textit{Chandra} X-ray flare catalog. For each theoretical solution, we compute the required efficiency $ε=E_{\rm data}/E_{\rm sh}$, where $E_{\rm data}$ is the observed radiated energy for each flare and $E_{\rm sh}$ is the available energy in the shocked flow. Notably, the weak flares require $ε\sim10^{-3}$--$10^{-2}$, while moderate flares require a few percent. We perform the analyses for weakly and highly spinning cases, and the resulting weak-to-moderate flare energy budget remains unchanged. For the fiducial accretion rate and bolometric correction, the kinetic energy reservoir in standing shock is sufficient for the observed weak-to-moderate flare population, whereas the strongest events likely require higher efficiency which can be mediated via additional magnetic energy dissipation channel.

</details>

### [139] [Estimating the asymptotics of integer partitions in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$)](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03034)
*Avinandan Mondal*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文通过蒙特卡洛马尔可夫链（MCMC）模拟，数值研究了中间维度（d=3,4,5,6）整数分拆的渐近增长速度，发现这些维度下分拆增长快于对应的MacMahon数，并给出了前导及部分次前导渐近项的数值估计。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有研究证明维度d≥7的整数分拆渐近增长严格快于MacMahon数，而d=1,2时两者一致。但中间维度d=3,4,5,6的渐近行为比较仍是开放问题，本文旨在填补这一空白。

Method: 采用自适应权重学习结合传统MCMC步骤的方法进行大规模模拟（N达15000），对每个中间维度分别估计log p_d(n)的前导渐近系数及部分次前导项的数值。

Result: 数值结果表明，在d=3,4,5,6维度下，整数分拆渐近增长均快于MacMahon数：前导系数估计值分别为1.8196±0.0019、1.7215±0.0045、1.6521±0.0059和1.652±0.021，均大于对应的MacMahon系数1.7898、1.6614、1.5737和1.509。此外还获得了部分次前导渐近项的估计。

Conclusion: 本文数值上确立了在中间维度d=3,4,5,6下整数分拆确实比MacMahon数增长更快，为相关开放问题提供了强数值证据，同时为进一步的理论证明指明方向。

Abstract: It was recently shown by Yeliussizov \cite{Yeliussizov} that integer partitions in dimensions $d \geq 7$ asymptotically grow strictly faster than MacMahon numbers. As MacMahon numbers match with integer partitions in dimensions $d = 1,2$, the comparison of asymptotics of integer partitions with MacMahon numbers in intermediate dimensions ($d = 3,4,5,6$) is an open question. In this work, we perform Markov chain Monte Carlo (MCMC) simulations till $N=15000$ by using adaptive weight learning followed by conventional MCMC steps to numerically estimate the asymptotics of integer partitions in these intermediate dimensions. We numerically establish that in these intermediate dimensions, partitions asymptotically grow faster than MacMahon numbers. More specifically, assuming that the limits exist, we show: $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-3/4}\log p_3(n) = 1.8196 \pm 0.0019$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-4/5}\log p_4(n) = 1.7215 \pm 0.0045$, $\lim_{n\to\infty}n^{-5/6}\log p_5(n) = 1.6521 \pm 0.0059$, and $\lim_{n\to\infty}\log n^{-6/7}p_6(n) = 1.652 \pm 0.021$ for partitions in dimensions $d=3,4,5,$ and $6$ respectively. These numbers are all larger than MacMahon leading order asymptotic coefficients of $1.7898, 1.6614, 1.5737,$ and $1.509$ respectively. Additionally, we also find estimates for some of the sub-leading asymptotic terms in $\log p_d(n)$ in each of the dimensions.

</details>


### [140] [Computational Algorithms for Invariant Reduction of Variational Forms](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03197)
*Kostya Druzhkov,Alexey Shevyakov*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文开发了用于偏微分方程（PDE）对称约减的显式计算算法，使得不变守恒律、变分结构和预辛（presymplectic）哈密顿结构能够通过不变约减机制从原始模型传递到约减模型。利用变分p-形式作为扩大的（度移位）正切系统中守恒律的诠释，将扰动变量视为反交换变量，从而将守恒律、变分1-形式和预辛结构的约减统一为单一算法流程。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: PDE的对称约减比其他类型的约减继承了更多的几何结构（如不变守恒律、变分结构及在适当条件下的哈密顿型结构），但如何显式地进行这些约减计算是一个挑战。作者希望通过统一的算法框架使得这类约减能够被系统地、明确地计算出来，而不只是理论上的存在性结果。

Method: 本文提出了三种算法：（i）基于同伦（homotopy）的约减算法，适用于演化方程系统，已在Maple中实现；（ii）适用于一般ℓ-正规系统（p>0）的下降（descent）约减算法；（iii）在适当条件下适用于点对称的简单约减算法，包括可用更少独立变量的系统来描述约减。核心思想是将变分p-形式视为扩大系统（度移位正切系统）的守恒律，其中扰动变量被处理为反交换（graded-commutative）变量，从而将守恒律约减、变分1-形式约减和预辛结构约减统一为同一程序。

Result: 示例涵盖了多个经典方程，包括一维和二维空间中的非线性演化方程、拉普拉斯方程、不可压缩欧拉方程以及Pavlov方程的余切（cotangent）系统，验证了所提出算法的有效性和适用性。

Conclusion: 通过将守恒律约减、变分1-形式约减和预辛结构约减统一为一套基于分级交换（graded-commutative）框架的算法流程，本文为对称约减中几何结构的显式继承计算提供了系统化的工具，并已在Maple中实现了演化方程的算法。

Abstract: Symmetry reductions of partial differential equations (PDEs) inherit more geometric structures than other types of reductions: invariant conservation laws, variational structures, and, under suitable conditions, Hamiltonian-type structures of the original model descend to the reduced model through the mechanism of invariant reduction. This paper develops computational algorithms that carry out such reductions explicitly. We use the interpretation of variational $p$-forms as conservation laws of an enlarged system --- the (degree-shifted) tangent system --- consisting of the original equations together with their linearizations, in which the perturbation variables are treated as anticommuting. This allows us to formulate the algorithms using well-known concepts from the theory of conservation laws, naturally adapted to the graded-commutative setting. The reduction of conservation laws, variational $1$-forms, and presymplectic structures thereby becomes a single algorithmic procedure. We present (i) a homotopy-based reduction algorithm for systems of evolution equations, implemented in Maple; (ii) a descent reduction algorithm applicable to general $\ell$-normal systems for $p>0$; and (iii) a simple reduction algorithm available for point symmetries under suitable conditions, including the description of reductions in terms of systems involving fewer independent variables. Examples include nonlinear evolution equations in one and two spatial dimensions, the Laplace equation, the incompressible Euler equations, and the cotangent system of Pavlov's equation.

</details>


### [141] [Optimal bounds for embedded eigenvalues of one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators with decaying potentials](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03224)
*Jifeng Chu,Wencai Liu,Kang Lyu*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究一维离散 Schrödinger 算子 $H = \Delta + V$ 在 $\ell^2(\mathbb{N})$ 上（带自伴边界条件），其中扰动 $V(n)=O(1)/(1+n)$。作者得到了决定嵌入特征值存在与不存在时 $V$ 的渐近系数的尖锐临界转变条件。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 为离散 Schrödinger 算子确定嵌入特征值存在性与渐近系数之间的尖锐阈值，理解哪些程度的势衰减仍允许嵌入本征态存在。

Method: 考虑一维离散 Schrödinger 算子，势满足 $V(n)=O(1)/(1+n)$ 的衰减条件，在自伴边界条件下分析嵌入特征值的出现条件，并确定渐近系数的临界值。

Result: 给出了 $V$ 的渐近系数关于嵌入特征值存在与不存在的尖锐 (sharp) 转换阈值。

Conclusion: 嵌入特征值的存在性由势的渐近系数是否超过某一临界值决定，该临界给出尖锐的相变边界。

Abstract: In this paper, we consider one-dimensional discrete Schrödinger operators \begin{align}
  Hu(n)=(Δ+V)u(n)\nonumber \end{align}
  on $\ell^2(\mathbb{N})$ with a self-adjoint boundary condition at $n=0$, where $Δ$ denotes the discrete Laplacian and $V(n)$ is a real-valued perturbation satisfying
  $$V(n)=\frac{O(1)}{1+n}.$$ We determine the sharp transition for the asymptotic coefficient of \(V\) governing the existence and nonexistence of embedded eigenvalues.

</details>


### [142] [Controllability, returning waves and scattering without reverberation in 3D acoustic dynamic system](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03536)
*Mikhail I. Belishev,Aleksei F. Vakulenko*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文研究三维空间中由波动方程u_tt-Δu+qu=0描述的动态声散射系统，发现当哈密顿算子H=-Δ+q具有束缚态时，系统在重新聚焦于某些空间点a时会失去可控性，从而产生一种物理效应：有限能量的波能够同时在过去和未来的锥中消失，且不留下混响地离开非均匀区域。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 探索动态声散射系统可控性的边界条件，特别是当势函数q存在束缚态时对系统可控性的影响，并揭示由此产生的物理效应与波前反转（时间反转镜）之间的数学联系。

Method: 基于波动方程u_tt-Δu+qu=0在三维欧氏空间中的动态散射理论框架，通过分析哈密顿算子H=-Δ+q的谱结构（尤其是束缚态的存在性），研究有限能量控制f所产生的波场u^f的聚焦与可控性性质。

Result: 证明了当哈密顿算子H=-Δ+q具有束缚态时，存在空间点a使得系统在重新聚焦于x=a时失去可控性。这导致有限能量的波可以在过去与未来锥同时消失，并在离开非均匀区域时不产生回声现象。

Conclusion: 该效应与波前反转（时间反转镜）现象有相似之处，但从数学角度更具深刻意义，为理解非均匀介质中波动系统的可控性边界提供了新的理论视角。

Abstract: The dynamic acoustic scattering system is governed by the wave equation $u_{tt}-Δu+qu=0$ in $\Bbb R^3,\,\,\,-\infty<t<\infty$, with a compactly supported potential $q$ and infinitely distant sources (controls) $f$, which initiate incoming spherical waves $u=u^f(x,t)$ provided $u^f\big|_{|x|<-t,\,\,\,t<0}=0$. These waves are focused at $x=0$ and fill up the whole space at the moment $t=0$. The system is {\it controllable} if the set of waves $u^f(\cdot,0)$ produced by all finite energy controls $f$, covers the space $L_2(\Bbb R^3)$.
  As we show, if the Hamiltonian $H=-Δ+q$ has the bound states, then in the space the points $a$ appear such that the system, being refocused at $x=a$, loses controllability. The latter leads to a physical effect: the waves $u^f$ of finite energy appear, which vanish simultaneously in the past and future cones $|x|<\pm\, t$ and leave the region of inhomogeneity of $q$ without reverberation. This effect has some similarities with the wavefront reversal (Time Reversing Mirror), but is more meaningful from a mathematical point of view.

</details>


### [143] [The conductivity matrix at the topological phase transition](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03832)
*Simone Fabbri,Giovanna Marcelli,Alessandro Giuliani*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 该论文通过欧几里得多体形式推导了二维非相互作用半金属电子系统的完整电导率矩阵公式，发现纵向电导率仅取决于费米能级处锥形带结构，而横向电导率同时取决于锥形结构和附近拓扑相的性质。在一般半金属态中电导率不量子化，但在费米点旋转对称性假设下恢复普适性。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在半金属相（两布洛赫带在费米能级处锥形相交）中，电导率矩阵的普适性尚不清楚。这类相通常出现在不同整数量子霍尔相之间的转变点，理解其电导率行为对于理解拓扑相变和量子输运具有重要意义。

Method: 采用欧几里得多体形式（Euclidean many-body formalism），为一般类二维非相互作用半金属晶格电子系统推导出完整电导率矩阵的显式公式，并对若干量子霍尔流体的物理模型进行具体计算与分析。

Result: 纵向电导率仅依赖费米能级处锥形相交的形状；横向电导率由锥形结构和附近拓扑相性质共同决定。一般半金属态下两种电导率均不以普适量子化，但在费米点处线性化哈密顿量存在涌现旋转对称性时电导率矩阵恢复普适性。在具体模型中，发现了横向电导率量子化为e²/h的半整数倍以及连续依赖于外部应变参数的两种情形。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了半金属拓扑相变点处电导率矩阵的普适性与非普适性特征：旋转对称性是其普适性的关键条件，而外部应变等参数可导致非普适的横向电导率行为。

Abstract: For a general class of two-dimensional non-interacting semi-metallic electron systems on a lattice, we derive an explicit formula for the whole conductivity matrix, by using Euclidean many-body formalism. The semi-metallic phase takes place whenever two Bloch bands touch at the Fermi energy with conical intersections and generally occurs at the transition between distinct integer quantum Hall phases. Unlike the longitudinal conductivity (which depends solely on the shape of the cones at the Fermi level), the transverse conductivity is remarkably determined both by the conical structures of the bands and by the nature of the two nearby topological phases. Generically, in the semi-metallic state, neither the longitudinal nor the transverse conductivities are quantized in integer multiples of a universal conductivity quantum; however, universality of the conductivity matrix is restored under the assumption of emergent rotational symmetry of the linearized Hamiltonian at the Fermi points. Using our formula, we compute the conductivity matrix for several physically relevant models of quantum Hall fluids at the topological phase transition, exhibiting cases where the transverse conductivity is quantized in half-integer multiples of $e^2/h$ and cases where it depends continuously on an external strain parameter, thus shedding light on its universality or non-universality features.

</details>


### [144] [Classification of abstract Bose field models](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03863)
*Yasumichi Matsuzawa*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文在量子场论中，对一类抽象的玻色场模型进行了酉等价分类，涵盖抽象自由玻色场模型、抽象van Hove–Miyatake模型以及红外重整化的van Hove–Miyatake模型，并作为应用对二次相互作用模型进行了分类，回答了Arai关于红外重整化van Hove–Miyatake模型分类的问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 量子场论中不同类型的玻色场模型（包括自由玻色场、van Hove–Miyatake模型及其红外重整化版本）之间的等价关系是理解其物理与数学结构的基础。Arai先前提出了一个关于红外重整化van Hove–Miyatake模型分类的开放问题，本文旨在回答这一问题。

Method: 本文通过建立一类抽象玻色场模型在酉等价意义下的分类框架，系统地对各类模型进行归类；利用该分类结果，进一步对二次相互作用模型进行分类，从而解决具体的开放问题。

Result: 成功对一类包含自由玻色场模型、van Hove–Miyatake模型和红外重整化van Hove–Miyatake模型的抽象玻色场模型进行了酉等价分类，并作为推论完成了二次相互作用模型的分类，解答了Arai提出的问题。

Conclusion: 本文建立了抽象玻色场模型完整的酉等价分类理论，不仅统一了多种重要的玻色场模型的分类问题，还解决了此前悬而未决的开放问题，为该领域提供了更清晰的数学结构认识。

Abstract: We classify a class of abstract Bose field models in quantum field theory up to unitary equivalence. The class includes abstract free Bose field models, abstract van Hove--Miyatake models, and infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models. Moreover, as an application of our classification, we classify quadratic interaction models. In particular, our results answer a question of A. Arai concerning the classification of infrared-renormalized van Hove--Miyatake models.

</details>


### [145] [Limits of Stochastic Semigroups and Block-Triangular Majorisation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04057)
*Fabio Deelan Cunden,Jakub Czartowski,Giovanni Gramegna,Marilena Ligabò*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究由不变分布定义的随机矩阵半群的极限。给定依赖于参数β的概率向量γ(β)，研究了当β→∞时对应的γ(β)-保持随机矩阵半群的收敛概念及极限结构。极限半群通常不同于保持极限分布的半群。针对Gibbs向量情形，证明极限半群由带子随机性约束的块上三角随机矩阵构成，由此引出Block-Triangular majorisation概念。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 理解由参数化不变分布族诱导的随机矩阵半群在参数趋于极限时的行为，揭示"取极限"与"保持分布构造半群"两种操作不可交换的现象，并建立一般性理论框架。

Method: 提出依赖于参数的γ(β)-保持随机矩阵半群的收敛概念；在逆温度β下Gibbs向量这一特例中，推导出极限半群的结构为块上三角随机矩阵并附加子随机性约束；刻画和枚举其极值元素；研究半群作用在概率向量上诱导的预序；将Block-Triangular majorisation与普通majorisation及上三角（无序）majorisation建立联系；用有限族单调量进行完全刻画，并分析Rényi α-熵在β→∞时的行为。

Result: 极限半群由满足子随机性约束的块上三角随机矩阵组成；Block-Triangular majorisation介于普通majorisation和上三角majorisation之间，且可由有限族单调量完全刻画；Rényi α-熵在β→∞时的极限行为得到分析。

Conclusion: 该工作建立了参数化不变分布族诱导的随机矩阵半群极限的一般框架，证明了极限半群与极限分布的保持半群不等价，并通过Block-Triangular majorisation对极限半群的结构、极值元素及诱导的预序给出了完整刻画。

Abstract: We investigate limits of semigroups of stochastic matrices defined by their invariant distribution. Given probability vectors $γ(β)$ depending on a parameter $β$, we introduce a notion of convergence as $β\to\infty$ for the corresponding semigroups of $γ(β)$-preserving stochastic matrices and investigate the structure of the resulting limit. In general, the limiting semigroup differs from the semigroup preserving the limiting distribution, showing that these two operations do not commute. We develop a general framework for such limiting semigroups and study in detail the case in which the invariant distributions are Gibbs vectors at the inverse temperature $β$. We show that the limiting semigroup consists of block-upper-triangular stochastic matrices subject to additional substochasticity constraints. We characterise and enumerate their extremal elements and determine the preorder on probability vectors induced by the action of the semigroup. The resulting notion of Block-Triangular majorisation interpolates between ordinary majorisation and upper triangular (aka unordered) majorisation. We show that it is completely characterised by a finite family of monotones and analyse the corresponding behaviour of Rényi $α$-entropies as $β\to\infty$.

</details>


### [146] [A simple derivation of the zero-temperature BCS functional from the reduced BCS Hamiltonian](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04060)
*Christian Hainzl,Riccardo Panza*

Main category: math-ph

TL;DR: 本文研究完整费米子Fock空间上的巨正则约化BCS哈密顿量，证明了精确的有限体积基态能量与相应有限体积BCS泛函的最小值之间仅相差一个与体积无关的误差，从而在热力学极限下BCS变分原理对基态能量密度是精确的。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 作者希望严格证明BCS变分原理的精确性，即验证在热力学极限下，BCS泛函给出的基态能量密度与精确的基态能量密度一致，这为BCS理论在凝聚态物理中的广泛应用提供了严格的数学基础。

Method: 论文在完整费米子Fock空间上考虑约化BCS哈密顿量，配对相互作用支持在费米面周围的能量壳层内，固定化学势条件下，通过精确的有限体积分析比较精确基态能量与BCS泛函最小值，证明二者误差与体积无关。

Result: 证明了精确有限体积基态能量与BCS泛函最小值之间的差是体积无关的误差项，进而推导出热力学极限下BCS变分原理对基态能量密度精确成立。

Conclusion: 该结果严格确立了BCS泛函变分原理在热力学极限下的精确性，为约化BCS模型的理论分析提供了严格的数学证明，支持了BCS理论在该模型框架下的有效性。

Abstract: We consider a grand-canonical reduced BCS Hamiltonian on the full fermionic Fock space with an attractive pairing interaction supported in an energy shell around the Fermi surface. For fixed chemical potential, we prove that the exact finite-volume ground-state energy differs from the minimum of the corresponding finite-volume BCS functional by a volume-independent error. As a consequence, the BCS variational principle is exact for the ground-state energy density in the thermodynamic limit.

</details>

### [147] [AnyGS2Mesh: Feed-Forward Mesh Reconstruction from 3D Gaussian Splatting with Arbitrary-Resolution Views](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03304)
*Yuxuan Song,Fan Gao,Yibo Zhao,Jiarui Wen,Youcheng Cai,Ligang Liu*

Main category: cs.CG

TL;DR: AnyGS2Mesh是首个前馈式3D网格重建框架，直接从3D Gaussian Splatting表示重建网格，支持任意输入分辨率。通过Gaussian-Guided Transformer架构利用显式3D几何先验，结合三个关键组件（高斯引导空间推理Transformer、流式分块几何编码器、尺度对齐混合深度精化器），实现接近实时的、高质量的网格重建，推理时间显著低于基于迭代优化的方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有的从3D高斯场景表示进行网格重建的方法主要依赖迭代优化，导致推理速度慢且难以扩展到高分辨率输入。这限制了3D重建在实际应用中的效率和可用性。

Method: 提出AnyGS2Mesh框架，包含三个关键组件：(1) Gaussian-Guided Spatial Reasoning Transformer将高斯原语表示为结构化3D令牌，并联合推理高斯特征和图像特征；(2) Streaming and Patchwise Geometry Encoder顺序处理原生分辨率视图，在变长视图集合中聚合信息；(3) Scale-Aligned Hybrid Depth Refiner使用PatchFusion式编解码器将RGB条件深度与高斯渲染的度量深度融合。深度图通过TSDF融合和Marching Cubes进行确定性网格提取。

Result: 实验表明AnyGS2Mesh在重建质量上达到最先进水平，同时相比基于优化的基线方法显著减少了推理时间，实现近实时的、高质量的网格重建。

Conclusion: 研究工作证明了将3D高斯表示与前馈Transformer架构相结合在可扩展的3D几何重建中的潜力，为未来高效网格重建提供了新方向。

Abstract: Existing 3D mesh reconstruction methods from Gaussian scene representations predominantly rely on iterative optimization, resulting in slow inference and limited scalability to high-resolution inputs. In this paper, we present AnyGS2Mesh, the first feed-forward framework for directly reconstructing 3D meshes from 3D Gaussian Splatting representations with support for arbitrary input image resolutions. Our approach incorporates a Gaussian-Guided Transformer architecture that exploits explicit 3D geometric priors for efficient mesh generation. We introduce three key components: (1) a Gaussian-Guided Spatial Reasoning Transformer represents Gaussian primitives as structured 3D tokens and jointly reasons over Gaussian and image features; (2) a Streaming and Patchwise Geometry Encoder processes native-resolution views sequentially and aggregates information across variable-length view sets; (3) a Scale-Aligned Hybrid Depth Refiner uses a PatchFusion-style encoder--decoder to fuse RGB-conditioned predicted depth with Gaussian-rendered metric depth, combining fine local structures with globally consistent metric scale. The refined depth maps are integrated through TSDF fusion, followed by Marching Cubes for deterministic mesh extraction. Extensive experiments show that AnyGS2Mesh achieves state-of-the-art reconstruction quality while significantly reducing inference time compared with optimization-based baselines, enabling near-real-time, high-quality mesh reconstruction. Our results demonstrate the potential of combining Gaussian representations and feed-forward Transformer architectures for scalable 3D geometry reconstruction. The code will be made publicly available upon acceptance.

</details>


<div id='math.AT'></div>

# math.AT [[Back]](#toc)

### [148] [Homology Nilpotency in Rational Homotopy Theory: Cell Attachments, Retractive Towers, Divergence, and Stabilization](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02980)
*Paul-Eugène Parent*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文研究单连通极小Sullivan代数的同调幂零性（homology nilpotency），建立了上下界判据（含有理胞腔附加方法），构造了满足 nil_h(M)=3 < Hnil(M)=4 的显式有限型极小Sullivan代数例子，并通过完全严格协调的收缩塔刻画了同伦幂长度与同调幂零性的差异，最后证明了在逐次有限楔和有理球面稳定化后，Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) 对所有单连通有限型有理空间成立。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 同调幂零性是与Lusternik-Schnirelmann范畴和同伦幂零性相关的重要代数拓扑不变量，但此前缺乏系统的上下界判据，且该概念与同伦幂零性之间的精确关系尚不清晰。作者旨在补齐这些工具，并澄清两者间的刚性差异。

Method: 作者为Sullivan代数的同调幂零性建立了上下界判据，引入了针对有理胞腔附加的方法；利用完全严格协调的收缩塔（completely strictly coordinated retractive towers）来区分同伦幂长度与同调幂零性；通过构造显式极小Sullivan代数展示两者的严格不等性；最后通过逐次有限楔和单连通有理球面的稳定化操作消除该刚性差异。

Result: 构造了满足 nil_h(M)=3<Hnil(M)=4 的显式有限型极小Sullivan代数；证明了在逐次有限楔和有理球面稳定化后，等式 Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) 对每个单连通有限型有理空间 X 成立，即同调幂零性在稳定化后与有理LS范畴重合。

Conclusion: 同调幂零性本质上是一种比同伦幂零性更精细的不变量，其额外差异源于商缺陷（quotient defect），但该缺陷在有理球面楔和的稳定化下消失，从而同调幂零性收敛到有理LS范畴。

Abstract: Homology nilpotency of a simply connected minimal Sullivan algebra $M$ is the least integer $n$ for which $(M^+)^{n+1}$ is contained in an acyclic differential ideal. We develop lower- and upper-bound criteria, including methods for rational cell attachments, and construct an explicit finite-type minimal Sullivan algebra $M$ satisfying $nil_h(M)=3<Hnil(M)=4$.
  Complete strictly coordinated retractive towers identify the obstruction: they characterize homotopical nil-length while exposing the additional quotient defect measured by homology nilpotency. We then show that this rigid defect disappears after stabilization by degreewise finite wedges of simply connected rational spheres: $$
  Hnil_s(X)=cat_o(X) $$ for every simply connected rational space $X$ of finite type.

</details>


### [149] [Generalized Telescope Conjecture](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03375)
*Jiacheng Liang*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文引入"原子粉碎框架"(atomic smashing frame)概念，将Balmer谱从张量三角几何推广到任意可表现对称幺半ω-范畴，建立了粉碎理想与局部刚性局部化之间的对应，并应用于色同伦理论、不稳态拓扑和Serre粉碎框架等广泛场景。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 经典Balmer谱理论局限于stable compactly-rigidly generated的张量三角范畴，作者希望将这一框架推广到更一般的可表现对称幺半ω-范畴，从而在更广泛的上下文中研究望远镜猜想(telescope conjecture)及相关结构。

Method: (1) 定义atomic smashing frame并证明在stable compactly-rigidly generated情形下可恢复经典Balmer谱；(2) 利用dualizable与rigid ω-范畴，建立smashing ideals与locally rigid localizations的一一对应，证明smashing frames的recollement定理；(3) 在非稳定情形，将∞-topoi的望远镜猜想刻画为smashing fields条件，对connective module categories用Pierce型条件刻画；(4) 引入Serre smashing frame作为介于atomic与usual smashing frames之间的中间结构。

Result: 主要应用成果包括：自然投影诱导Sp的smashing frame嵌入所有单色层Sp_{T(n)}的smashing frame之积（覆盖所有素数和高度），从而将Sp的spatiality完全归结为各Sp_{T(n)}的spatiality；在∞-topoi和非稳定模范畴（connective module categories及hypercomplete connective sheaves）中给出了望远镜猜想的等效刻画。

Conclusion: atomic smashing frame提供了一个统一的推广框架，将稳定与非稳定、代数与拓扑中的望远镜猜想问题整合到同一理论之下，并通过Serre smashing frame为connective R-module范畴上的望远镜猜想提供了中间结构层的研究工具。

Abstract: We introduce the atomic smashing frame, extending the Balmer spectrum from tensor-triangular geometry to an arbitrary presentably symmetric monoidal $\infty$-category $\mathcal{V}$. This yields a formulation of the telescope conjecture for $\mathcal{V}$ and recovers the classical Balmer spectrum in the stable compactly-rigidly generated case.
  Exploiting dualizable and rigid $\infty$-categories, we establish a correspondence between smashing ideals and locally rigid localizations. This leads to a recollement theorem for smashing frames in the (pre)stable setting, together with an atomic refinement in the stable compactly-rigidly generated case. As a major application to chromatic homotopy theory, we show that the natural projections induce an embedding of the smashing frame of $\mathrm{Sp}$ into the product of the smashing frames of the monochromatic layers $\mathrm{Sp}_{T(n)}$, over all primes and heights. In particular, the spatiality of the smashing frame of $\mathrm{Sp}$ reduces entirely to that of $\mathrm{Sp}_{T(n)}$.
  In the unstable setting, we characterize the telescope conjecture for $\infty$-topoi in terms of smashing fields, and for connective module categories and hypercomplete connective sheaves in terms of Pierce-type conditions. Finally, we introduce the Serre smashing frame. Over a connective $\mathbb{E}_\infty$-ring $R$, this frame sits between the atomic and usual smashing frames, providing an intermediate structural layer in the study of the telescope conjecture for the connective $R$-module category.

</details>


### [150] [From configuration spaces to graph complexes via FA-modules](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04033)
*Ayako Carter,Benjamin C. Ward*

Main category: math.AT

TL;DR: 本文识别了Gadish-Hainaut（源自Petersen的工作）所构造的多项式函子Φ[n,m]的系数，将其通过FA-模的cobar构造实现。该识别形式上蕴含这些系数出现在图同调的计算中，并由此给出若干图复形同调可嵌入楔形圆并的紧致支撑上同调的例子，包括genus 2的Payne-Willwacher标记图复形，从而得到模空间M_{2,n}的gr_{11}部分的显式树装饰复形分解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: Petersen以及Gadish和Hainaut的工作将楔形圆的紧致支撑上同调建模为多项式函子，但该函子的系数Φ[n,m]尚未被具体识别。识别这些系数对理解该类上同调与图同调之间的关系至关重要，并有助于研究模空间M_{2,n}的Hodge分次部分。

Method: 作者通过$\mathbf{FA}$-模的cobar构造来识别函子Φ[n,m]的系数。借助这一形式识别（formal implication），建立图同调与楔形圆紧致支撑上同调之间的联系，并针对genus 2情形，将Payne-Willwacher标记图复形分解为简单$\mathbf{FA}$-模的直和，进而构造装饰树复形来描述M_{2,n}的Hodge分次部分。

Result: 给出了多个图复形同调嵌入H_c^*(F(S^1∨S^1,n))的实例；对genus 2的Payne-Willwacher标记图复形给出了新的简单FA-模显式分解；将gr_{11}H_c^*(M_{2,n})描述为装饰树复形的上同调，并证明了gr_{11}H_c^{n+1}(M_{2,n})=0等结果。

Conclusion: 通过FA-模的cobar构造完成了Φ[n,m]系数的具体识别，建立了图同调与自由楔形圆构型空间上同调之间的形式联系，并为模空间M_{2,n}的Hodge分次上同调提供了新的计算框架和具体消没结果。

Abstract: Work of Gadish and Hainaut (after Petersen) models the compactly supported cohomology of a wedge of circles as a polynomial functor. We identify the coefficients of this functor, $Φ[n,m]$, via a cobar construction of $\mathbf{FA}$-modules. This identification formally implies that these coefficients will arise in computations of graph homology, and we use this result to give examples of graph complexes whose homology may be embedded in $H_c^\ast(F(S^1\vee S^1,n))$.
  This includes the Payne-Willwacher marked graph complex in genus 2, for which we give a new, explicit decomposition in terms of simple $\mathbf{FA}$-modules. This allows us to describe $\mathsf{gr}_{11}H_c^{\ast}(\mathcal{M}_{2,n})$ as the cohomology of a complex of decorated trees and to show, for example, $\mathsf{gr}_{11}H_c^{n+1}(\mathcal{M}_{2,n})=0$.

</details>


<div id='math.NA'></div>
