### [151] [Buckling Prediction for Nonlinear Elastic Beams with Soft Inclusions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.02956)
*Thien Tran-Duc,J. E. Bunder,A. J. Roberts*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 提出了一种高效的多尺度计算框架，用于预测含周期性软夹杂弹性梁的屈曲及后屈曲行为，通过尺度间补丁耦合算法仅在小补丁域内进行微观计算，兼顾精度与计算成本。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有线性弹性多尺度补丁计算均质化方法无法处理非线性屈曲问题，且全尺度数值模拟成本高昂，需要一种高效且精确的框架来预测含软夹杂梁的失稳及后屈曲行为，以服务工程应用。

Method: 在先前适用于线弹性的多尺度补丁计算均质化框架中引入非线性，使微观计算仅在稀疏的小补丁子域内进行，通过经过验证的补丁耦合算法恢复宏观行为；通过Jacobian矩阵特征值分析预测失稳起始点及临界应变/应力，生成分叉图并研究插值阶数的影响。

Result: 夹杂模量比在0.001~1范围内验证表明：夹杂刚度的降低减小了临界屈曲应变和临界屈曲应力，即更容易失稳，但产生较温和的后屈曲响应（更小的横向位移和应力降）。Jacobian特征值分析准确预测失稳；与全域模拟对比显示所提出框架准确预测屈曲阈值和后屈曲行为，同时大幅降低计算成本。

Conclusion: 该多尺度框架能够高效且准确地预测含周期性软夹杂梁的屈曲与后屈曲行为，采用分域补丁大幅节省计算成本，并可推广至非均质梁、板、壳及其他工程结构。

Abstract: We develop an efficient and accurate multiscale computational framework for predicting the buckling and post-buckling behaviour of elastic beams containing periodically distributed soft inclusions. The framework extends our previous multiscale, patch, computational homogenisation for linear elasticity by incorporating nonlinearity, and thus enables accurate prediction of both the buckling onset and the subsequent post-buckling response. Microscale computations are performed only within a sparse set of small subdomains (patches), while the macroscale behaviour is recovered through a proven patch-coupling algorithm. The scheme is assessed through quarter- and half-domain patch computations for beams with inclusion-to-matrix Young's modulus ratios ranging from 0.001 to 1. The results show that reducing the inclusion stiffness lowers both the critical buckling strain and the critical buckling stress, indicating an increased susceptibility to instability, while producing a milder post-buckling response with smaller transverse deflections and stress drops. Eigenvalue analysis of the Jacobian matrix accurately predicts the onset of instability and the corresponding critical strain and stress. Bifurcation diagrams of the nonlinear buckled configurations under compressive loading, and a quantitative analysis of the effect of the interpolation order on the predicted buckling and post-buckling responses, are also presented. Comparisons with full-domain simulations demonstrate that the proposed framework accurately predicts both the buckling threshold and the post-buckling behaviour while substantially reducing the computational cost. The methodology is readily extendable to heterogeneous beams, plates, shells, and other engineering structures.

</details>


### [152] [Dimensional hyperreduction of nonlinear finite element models via empirical cubature with manifold-adaptive weights](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03068)
*Joaquín A. Hernández,S. Ares de Parga,Riccardo Rossi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出Manifold-Adaptive-Weight Empirical Cubature Method (MAW-ECM)，允许超约简中的采样权重随潜在坐标连续非线性变化，通过贪心剪枝策略与凸二次权重再分配问题，在非线性流形约简模型的框架下大幅减少采样积分点数量，在两个非线性基准问题（超材料单胞均质化与历史依赖连续损伤）上验证了比固定权重的流形模型减少80%以上（均质化）和97%以上（损伤）剩余采样点，且精度基本保持。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 现有参数化有限元问题的非线性流形约简模型已能大幅压缩潜在坐标数和采样单元数（通过采样加权超约简），但现有采样加权方法的权重在整个解流形上保持固定。作者认为这种限制未能充分挖掘超约简潜力：若允许权重随潜在坐标连续非线性变化，可进一步减少采样空间实体的数量。

Method: 提出MAW-ECM方法：以固定权重ECM规则为可行起点，通过贪心剪枝策略移除采样实体，并利用强制执行局部条件与正性的凸二次权重再分配问题实现权重自适应调整。非线性流形通过初始线性压缩后，以输入信息驱动的潜在坐标识别方式（将潜在坐标表示为保留模态幅度的线性组合），结合相关时引入图信息以探寻解流形的内在维度。

Result: 在两个非线性基准（负增量刚度超材料单胞均质化、历史依赖连续损伤问题）上进行评估。非线性流形表示与MAW-ECM结合相比标准线性约简模型将采样积分点减少超过两个数量级；相对于仅固定权重的流形模型，自适应权重在均质化基准中消除了约80%的剩余点，在损伤基准中消除了超过97%的点，同时基本保持精度。

Conclusion: 允许权重随潜在坐标非线性变化能进一步释放超约简潜力，MAW-ECM结合非线性流形约简可在保持精度的前提下显著减少采样积分点数量，证明了自适应权重超约简方法的有效性与优越性。

Abstract: Nonlinear-manifold reduced-order models for parametrized finite element problems can achieve substantial compression both in the number of generalized (latent) coordinates and, through sampling-and-weighting hyperreduction, in the number of sampled elements/integration points. Yet current sampling-and-weighting approaches employ weights that remain fixed over the solution manifold. We contend that this restriction leaves hyperreduction potential untapped: allowing the weights to vary continuously and nonlinearly with the latent coordinates can further decrease the number of sampled spatial entities. To exploit this possibility, we propose the Manifold-Adaptive-Weight Empirical Cubature Method (MAW-ECM). Starting from a feasible fixed-weight ECM rule, a greedy pruning strategy removes sampled entities through convex quadratic weight-redistribution problems enforcing local conditions and positivity. The method is assessed on two nonlinear benchmarks: homogenization of a metamaterial unit cell exhibiting negative incremental stiffness, and a history-dependent continuum-damage problem. In both cases, the nonlinear manifold is constructed from an initial linear compression followed by an input-informed identification of the latent coordinates as general linear combinations of the retained modal amplitudes, incorporating graph information when relevant to seek the intrinsic dimensionality of the solution manifold. We show that combining the nonlinear-manifold representation with MAW-ECM reduces the number of sampled integration points by more than two orders of magnitude relative to the corresponding standard linear reduced model. Relative to the fixed-weight manifold models alone, the adaptive weights eliminate approximately 80% of the remaining points in the homogenization benchmark and more than 97% in the damage benchmark, while essentially preserving their accuracy.

</details>


### [153] [A discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin finite-element framework for the simulation of microwave-heated flows](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03155)
*Oreste Marquis,Matthias Maier,Bruno Blais*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种用于模拟微波加热流动的高阶多物理场求解器，该求解器将不连续Petrov-Galerkin（DPG）有限元法用于时谐Maxwell方程，与连续Galerkin有限元法求解热方程和不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程相耦合。通过多个基准问题验证后，展示了自适应网格细化能力，并利用低内存占用的并行设计实现了微波辅助化学过程的大规模模拟。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 微波加热流动涉及电磁场、热场和流场的强耦合，需要高精度的多物理场数值模拟工具来支撑微波辅助化学过程的仿真与优化。现有方法在精度、自适应能力和计算效率方面仍存在不足。

Method: 采用DPG有限元法求解时谐Maxwell方程，利用其内置误差估计器实现自适应网格细化；同时采用连续Galerkin有限元法分别求解热方程和不可压缩Navier-Stokes方程。电磁求解器基于共轭梯度（CG）迭代求解器，流体求解器采用无矩阵（matrix-free）实现，以降低内存占用并提高并行可扩展性。

Result: 在矩形波导中的波传播、具有奇异解的空腔问题以及微波加热障碍物问题等多个基准测试中验证了电磁求解器的有效性，结果与文献中的数值和实验数据一致。进一步通过含奇异几何特征的障碍物周围微波加热流动模拟展示了多物理场框架的能力，并确认了DPG方法内置误差估计器在自适应网格细化中的有效性。

Conclusion: 该高阶多物理场求解器在精度、自适应能力和可扩展性方面均得到验证，为微波辅助化学过程的仿真与优化提供了可靠且高效的数值工具，其低内存占用设计使其适用于大规模并行计算。

Abstract: We present a high-order multiphysics solver for the simulation of microwave-heated flows. The solver couples a discontinuous Petrov-Galerkin (DPG) finite element method for the time-harmonic Maxwell equations with continuous Galerkin finite element methods for the heat equation and the incompressible Navier-Stokes equations. We validate the electromagnetic solver against multiple benchmark problems: wave propagation in a rectangular waveguide, a cavity problem with a singular solution, and a microwave-heated obstacle problem, comparing our results against numerical and experimental data from the literature. The results confirm the validity of the implementation and demonstrate its ability to perform adaptive mesh refinement using the DPG method's built-in error estimator. The final part of the study showcases the capabilities of the multiphysics framework through simulations of microwave-heated flow around obstacles with singular geometric features. These results highlight the potential of the proposed framework for the simulation and optimization of microwave-assisted chemical processes. Finally, the developed high-order multiphysics solver has a low memory footprint, since the electromagnetic solver relies on a Conjugate Gradient (CG) iterative solver and the fluid solver is implemented in a matrix-free fashion, making the overall approach scalable and well-suited for large-scale parallel simulations.

</details>


### [154] [Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion Method with Applications in Drug Repurposing](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03190)
*Maryam Bagherian,Albert Hung,Ivo Dinov,Joshua Welch*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种新型的张量补齐框架——耦合张量-张量补齐（CTTC），该框架突破了传统方法仅能利用矩阵形式辅助信息的限制，首次支持以张量形式融入辅助信息（如基因-基因相似性等多模态数据），以提升张量补齐的性能，并在药物效果预测任务上优于HaLRTC、CTRC、Cell和NTDDR等现有最先进方法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 许多生物医学问题可建模为张量补齐问题（使用观测到的多维数组条目来填补缺失值）。在此类场景中，融入张量各模式（mode）的辅助信息（如基因间相似性）可以显著提升补齐性能。然而，现有的大多数张量补齐方法仅能融入矩阵形式的辅助信息，无法有效利用更高阶的张量辅助信息，这限制了其在复杂多模态生物医学场景中的应用。

Method: 本文提出了耦合张量-张量补齐（CTTC）框架，核心创新在于能够以张量而非仅仅是矩阵的形式融入辅助信息，利用多模态张量之间的隐藏连接来改进张量补齐效果。CTTC具有距离度量学习与群论层面的理论支撑，并推导了一种交替优化算法来求解CTTC的优化问题，同时证明了算法收敛到驻点。

Result: 在DTD和LINCS两个基准数据集上，CTTC与HaLRTC、CTRC、Cell及NTDDR等其他张量补齐方法相比，展现出更优的运行时效率和RSE张量补齐精度，在药物效应预测任务上表现优于现有最先进方法。

Conclusion: CTTC作为一种新型的张量补齐框架，成功将辅助信息从矩阵推广到张量形式，既具有实践效用又有理论基础（距离度量学习与群论），在药物效应预测等生物医学任务上表现出优越的性能和效率，为该领域提供了新的方法论突破。

Abstract: Many biomedical challenges can be posed as tensor completion problems where the observed entries of a multidimensional array (a tensor) are used to impute the missing values. In such settings, incorporating side information about the modes of the tensor, such as gene-gene similarity, can significantly enhance the solutions of the completion problem. Most existing tensor completion methods can only incorporate side information in the form of matrices. In this study, we introduce a novel framework to incorporate side information in the form of tensors. Our new approach, called Coupled Tensor-Tensor Completion (CTTC), leverages the hidden connections among multimodal tensors to improve tensor completion performance. In addition to practical utility, CTTC has theoretical foundations in distance metric learning and group theory. We derive an alternating algorithm to solve the CTTC optimization problem and establish its convergence to a stationary point. Finally, we show that CTTC outperforms state-of-the-art tensor completion methods at predicting drug effects.
  Results: Compared with other tensor completion methods, including HaLRTC, CTRC, Cell, and NTDDR, CTTC demonstrates superior run-time and RSE tensor completion accuracy on two benchmark datasets, DTD and LINCS.

</details>


### [155] [A novel parallel approach for solving some free boundary value problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03214)
*Peeyush Singh,Amboru Yalmanda*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一类新颖且高效的并行直接与间接迭代方法，用于求解一般障碍问题和自由边界值问题。在M-矩阵假设下建立了直接并行方法的唯一性；通过在每个线性近似迭代阶段最小化离散能量泛函，建立了间接方法的收敛性分析，涵盖Ferris和Mangasarian意义下凸集上的光滑与非光滑能量泛函。数值实现方面，一维障碍问题采用基于SPIKE算法的直接广义并行方法，并推广了快速递归版至非光滑情形；二维和三维问题采用方向分裂法，将能量函数最小化转化为抛物型时间相关问题并结合Armijo步长规则。该框架还扩展到约束二次规划优化求解器及图像去模糊问题。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 障碍问题和自由边界值问题在物理学、工程和图像处理等领域有广泛应用，但传统求解方法在高维情形下计算复杂度高且难以并行化。作者旨在设计一类高效的并行计算方法来加速求解过程，并建立严格的数学收敛性理论。

Method: 提出并行直接与间接两类迭代方法。(1) 直接法：基于SPIKE算法的广义并行方法，在M-矩阵条件下保证解的唯一性，并将快速递归版本推广至非光滑情形；(2) 间接法：在每个线性化阶段通过最小化Ferris-Mangasarian意义下凸集上的离散能量泛函来驱动收敛，对光滑与非光滑能量泛函均给出收敛性分析。高维问题采用方向分裂技术，将每个方向子问题化为一维障碍最小化问题；借助抛物型时间依赖形式及Armijo时间步进规则加速求解。

Result: 理论上分别确立了直接并行方法在M-矩阵假设下解的唯一性，以及间接方法在凸集上能量泛函最小化框架下对光滑与非光滑情形的收敛性。数值上：一维障碍问题通过SPIKE算法及其非光滑泛化的快速递归版本实现高效并行求解；二维和三维障碍问题通过方向分裂与Armijo步长规则成功求解。此外，该求解器被成功应用于约束二次规划优化和图像去模糊，能够恢复原始图像。

Conclusion: 该研究建立了一套在理论上具有严格收敛保证、在数值上高效可并行化的通用框架，适用于障碍问题、自由边界问题、约束二次规划及图像恢复等多种应用场景。数值实验验证了理论结果的正确性与算法的实用性。

Abstract: This paper introduces a novel, efficient class of parallel direct and indirect iterative schemes to solve general obstacle and free boundary value problems. The uniqueness of the solution for the direct parallel method is established under the assumption that the model problem yields an $M$-matrix. The convergence analysis of the indirect approach is predicated on minimizing the discrete energy functional at each iterative stage of the linear approximation process. Under the aforementioned setting, we establish theoretical convergence results for both smooth and nonsmooth energy functionals defined over a convex set, in the sense of Ferris and Mangasarian \cite{ferris1994parallel}. For the numerical computation of the one-dimensional obstacle problem, we adopt a direct generalized parallel approach based on the SPIKE algorithm \cite{polizzi2006parallel}. To accelerate convergence and reduce computational complexity, a fast recursive version of the scheme is generalized to the nonsmooth case. For two- and three-dimensional obstacle problems, we employ a directional splitting method that treats each directional subproblem as a one-dimensional obstacle minimization problem. By framing the energy functional minimization as a parabolic time-dependent problem and utilizing an Armijo time-stepping rule during the solution update process, we successfully obtain results for higher-dimensional obstacles. Additionally, this study explores the possibility of extending the algorithm into a constrained quadratic programming optimization solver. We also evaluate the framework on image deblurring phenomena under the aforementioned setting, successfully recovering the original images. Finally, numerical illustrations are provided to validate the theoretical results.

</details>


### [156] [Learning Informative Prior with Infinite-Dimensional Continuous Normalizing Flow for Bayesian Inverse Problem](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03343)
*Yang Zhao,Junxiong Jia,Tao Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种基于连续归一化流（CNF）的无限维贝叶斯先验模型，用于偏微分方程（PDE）反问题的无限维贝叶斯推断，通过无限维空间中的神经常微分方程将简单参考测度变换为编码先验信息的复杂测度，并建立了相应的理论框架、训练方法和采样算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 无限维贝叶斯推断中，传统的有限维参数化先验难以有效融入先验信息并保证体系良定性。PDE反问题的模型参数通常位于无限维Hilbert空间中，需要一个能够有效结合先验信息且理论上良定的无限维先验模型。

Method: 1) 在无限维Hilbert空间中引入定义良好的神经常微分方程（neural ODE），构建连续归一化流（CNF）模型；2) 通过该流将简单参考测度变换为编码先验信息的复杂测度；3) 建立理论框架确保无限维空间中贝叶斯先验的良定性（well-posedness）；4) 针对两种不同数据设置提供先验训练方法；5) 给出两种贝叶斯后验采样算法。

Result: 将该框架应用于三个代表性反问题（光滑反问题、逆散射问题、逆热传导问题）的数值实验，实验结果支持理论分析，并验证了所提算法的高效性。

Conclusion: 所提出的基于连续归一化流的无限维先验模型为PDE反问题的贝叶斯推断提供了有效的先验构建框架，兼具理论保证和计算效率，适用于多种具有代表性的逆问题场景。

Abstract: This paper addresses infinite-dimensional Bayesian inference for inverse problem of partial differential equations with model parameters in infinite-dimensional Hilbert space. To effectively incorporate prior information, we propose a novel continuous normalizing flows based infinite-dimensional model. Specifically, by introducing a well-defined neural ordinary differential equation in infinite-dimensional space, a simple reference measure can be transformed into a more complex measure which encodes the prior information. A corresponding theoretical framework is established to ensure the well-posedness of our proposed Bayesian prior in infinite-dimensional space. We also provide training methods of the prior for two distinct data settings, along with two sampling algorithms for the resulting Bayesian posterior. The proposed framework is applied to three representative inverse problems: the simple smooth inverse problem, inverse scattering problem, and the inverse heat conduction problem. Numerical experiments support the theoretical analysis and demonstrate the efficiency of the proposed algorithms.

</details>


### [157] [Spectral Convergence of Random Feature Method in Multiple Dimensions](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03401)
*Pingbing Ming,Hao Yu*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了随机特征方法（RFM）在多维目标函数逼近中的谱收敛性，覆盖Sobolev、Gevrey、超解析和带限函数类。通过核积分算子生成的插值尺度建立了高概率逼近估计，并同时分析了强形式与弱形式RFM离散化的误差估计。此外，证明了随机特征矩阵（RFMtxs）在Fourier特征下的超指数奇异值衰减和tanh特征下的指数衰减，揭示了高逼近精度与严重病态性共存的机制——同一谱逼近既带来高精度也导致病态条件数。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 随机特征方法作为一类重要的无网格/浅层神经网络类逼近工具，其在多维情形下的谱收敛理论尚不完善。作者旨在建立一套统一的数学框架，系统刻画RFM在不同正则性目标类上的逼近速率，并阐明其数值离散化误差及矩阵条件数行为背后的统一机制。

Method: （1）利用核积分算子生成的插值尺度，建立RFM在Sobolev、Gevrey、超解析及带限目标类上的高概率一般性逼近估计；在单一随机事件上同时对所有目标建立统一逼近性质。（2）针对规则性适配的频率分布和均匀频率窗口分布，分别推导从超指数到代数阶的收敛率。（3）建立强形式和弱形式RFM离散化的抽象误差估计，将其转化为多维二阶椭圆边值问题与特征值问题的收敛估计。（4）对RFMtxs证明Fourier特征下的超指数和tanh特征下的指数奇异值衰减，并给出相应的条件数下界。

Result: 在逼近方面，获得了随目标正则性变化的从超指数到代数阶的最优收敛率，且单随机空间可统一覆盖所有目标，单一系数向量同时达到所有可容许误差范数下的谱精度。在离散化误差方面，将逼近界成功转化为强/弱形式RFM求解二阶椭圆边值与特征值问题的收敛估计。在矩阵性质方面，证明了RFMtxs的奇异值衰减率及条件数下界。

Conclusion: 该研究揭示了随机特征方法的一个核心内在张力：决定其高逼近精度的谱逼近机制，同时也是导致其数值矩阵严重病态的原因。这一认识为理解和改善RFM在实际应用中的数值稳定性提供了理论依据，并为在多维PDE求解和特征值问题中选择合适的特征类型及频率分布提供了指导。

Abstract: We first prove spectral convergence of the random feature method (RFM) for multidimensional targets in Sobolev, Gevrey, ultra-analytic, and bandlimited classes. The analysis establishes general high-probability approximation estimates in the interpolation scale generated by a kernel integral operator. On a single event determined only by the sampled features, one random space approximates every target in a prescribed source ball; moreover, for each target, a single coefficient vector defines an approximant that attains spectral accuracy simultaneously in all admissible error norms. For both regularity-adapted frequency distributions and uniform distributions on growing frequency windows, the resulting rates range from super-exponential to algebraic, depending on the regularity of the target. Second, we establish abstract error estimates for strong- and weak-form RFM discretizations, thereby converting the preceding approximation bounds into convergence estimates for multidimensional second-order elliptic boundary value and eigenvalue problems. Finally, for random feature matrices (RFMtxs), we prove super-exponential singular-value decay with Fourier features and exponential decay with $\tanh$ features, together with corresponding condition-number lower bounds. The analysis identifies a common mechanism: the same spectral approximation that yields high accuracy also drives severe ill-conditioning.

</details>


### [158] [Semi-discrete quadratic Wasserstein energy and state-dependent Langevin exploration](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03405)
*Ran Gu,Gaoyue Guo,Kelvin Shuangjian Zhang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文研究了半离散二次Wasserstein能量的性质及其在Langevin温度控制中的应用。证明了能量的局部Lipschitz连续性、全局半凹性、强制性、耗散性、全局极小值点的无碰撞性及C^2正则性；建立了熵正则化的松弛Langevin温度控制框架，证明了受控动力学的强适定性、不发生爆炸和碰撞，值函数经典光滑，且高斯Euler链几何遍历、best-so-far能量几乎必然收敛到全局最小值。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 半离散二次Wasserstein能量在站点碰撞处是非光滑的，对其正则性、极小值点性质缺乏系统理解。同时，在高维（d≥2）情况下，如何对Langevin温度进行有效的熵正则化松弛控制以促进全局收敛是未解决的难题。

Method: 本文采用分析+概率控制结合的方法：首先通过Laguerre细胞的质心表达能量梯度、通过显式面公式给出Hessian，在碰撞自由配置空间上建立C^2正则性，并在每个序腔中显式求解一维问题。随后针对d≥2构造熵正则化的松弛Langevin温度控制，研究其探索性Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman方程的解的正则性，并分析高斯Euler链的几何遍历性与收敛性。

Result: （1）半离散Wasserstein能量具有局部Lipschitz连续性、全局半凹性、强制性和耗散性；（2）每个全局极小值点都是内部的且无碰撞；（3）碰撞自由配置空间上能量为C^2，Hessian满足全局单侧界；（4）熵正则化松弛Langevin控制具有强适定、无碰撞的受控动力学，值函数是HJB方程的经典内点解，其Laplacian局部C^1，温度反馈局部Lipschitz；（5）对任意远离零的Borel温度规则与小步长，高斯Euler链几何遍历且不变测度有全支撑；best-so-far能量几乎必然收敛到全局最小值。

Conclusion: 本文系统地刻画了半离散二次Wasserstein能量的分析与几何性质（包括1D的显式解和2D方形域上非极小化的Lloyd不动点反例），并在此基础上建立了熵正则化的Langevin温度松弛控制理论，在d≥2情形下保证了碰撞自由、几何遍历及全局几乎必然收敛等强性质。

Abstract: We study the semi-discrete quadratic Wasserstein energy. The energy is nonsmooth at collisions of sites. We prove local Lipschitz continuity on the full configuration space, together with global semiconcavity, coercivity, and dissipativity; show that every global minimizer is interior and collision free; and establish $C^2$ regularity on the collision-free configuration space. The gradient is expressed through the barycenters of the balanced Laguerre cells, while the Hessian is given by an explicit facet formula and satisfies a global one-sided bound. We also solve the one-dimensional problem explicitly in each ordering chamber and give a two-site example on the unit square with non-minimizing Lloyd fixed points.
  For $d\ge 2$, we then formulate an entropy-regularized relaxed control of the Langevin temperature. The controlled dynamics is strongly well posed, nonexplosive, and collision free. Its value function is a classical interior solution of the exploratory Hamilton-Jacobi-Bellman equation; the Laplacian of the value function is locally $C^1$, which yields a locally Lipschitz optimal temperature feedback. Independently of this optimal-control result, for every fixed Borel temperature rule bounded away from zero, and every sufficiently small step size, the associated Gaussian Euler chain is geometrically ergodic with a full-support invariant law. The raw iterates do not converge, whereas the best-so-far energy converges almost surely to the global minimum and the running record approaches the set of global minimizers.

</details>


### [159] [Adaptively-refinable polar-spline discrete differential forms: hierarchical construction, exactness, and applications](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03461)
*Diogo C. Cabanas,Deepesh Toshniwal,Rafael Vazquez*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文提出了一种自适应可细化极坐标样条（hierarchical polar splines）的构造方法，并将其推广到结构保持（structure-preserving）的框架中，构建了极坐标分层k-形式空间，证明了基函数线性无关性及其构成上同调正确的离散de Rham复形，为极坐标几何上的自适应结构保持模拟提供了数学基础。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 在等几何分析中，表示盘状和球状几何通常需要引入折叠边奇异性（collapsed-edge singularities），但此类奇异参数化上的张量积B样条空间缺乏所需的光滑性。已有的极坐标样条（polar splines）解决了这一问题并在de Rham复形的结构保持离散化中取得成功，但缺乏自适应细化能力，限制了其在混合问题（如电磁学和流体力学）中的应用。

Method: 本文的主要方法贡献包括两方面：(1) 将极坐标样条与分层样条（hierarchical splines）相结合，构造自适应可细化的极坐标样条；(2) 在结构保持方法的框架下，构造分层极坐标样条k-形式空间（hierarchical polar spline k-form spaces），证明其基函数线性无关，并证明它们构成上同调正确的离散de Rham复形。

Result: 通过数值实验验证了理论结果，包括：(a) 了一项虚假谐波（spurious harmonics）研究，揭示了极坐标与张量积环境下上同调破坏细化（cohomology-breaking refinements）的差异；(b) 在局部细化极坐标网格上对Maxwell特征值问题进行离散化；(c) 对Hodge-Laplace问题进行了自适应收敛研究。

Conclusion: 这些贡献为极坐标几何上的自适应结构保持模拟提供了数学上严格的基础，证明了分层极坐标样条空间在结构保持离散化中的可行性，并通过数值实验验证了其实际效果，特别是在需要保持结构性质的物理问题（如电磁学和流体动力学）中具有重要意义。

Abstract: A common approach to representing disk-like and sphere-like geometries in isogeometric analysis is to use collapsed-edge singularities in the underlying parameterization. The resulting tensor-product B-spline spaces on such singular parameterizations lack the required smoothness to be used in isogeometric analysis. Polar splines (Toshniwal et al., CMAME 2017) rectify this issue by extracting a smoother subspace, and have been successfully used to discretize high-order partial differential equations, as well as to perform structure-preserving discretizations of the de Rham complex.The latter is particularly relevant for mixed formulations that appear in applications such as electromagnetism and fluid dynamics.
  The main contributions of this paper are twofold. We combine the polar spline construction with hierarchical splines to obtain adaptively-refinable polar splines. Moreover, we do so in the context of structure-preserving methods. That is, we show how to construct hierarchical polar spline $k$-form spaces, prove that the corresponding basis functions are linearly independent, and prove that they form a cohomologically-correct discrete de Rham complex.
  These contributions provide a mathematically sound foundation for adaptive structure-preserving simulations on polar geometries. We also provide numerical experiments that validate the theory and illustrate the practical behavior of the construction with problems where preserving the structure is mandatory. These include a study of spurious harmonics that highlights how cohomology-breaking refinements differ between the polar and tensor-product settings, a discretization of a Maxwell eigenvalue problem on a locally-refined polar mesh, and an adaptive convergence study for a Hodge--Laplace problem.

</details>


### [160] [Residual neural networks overcome the curse of dimensionality for semilinear heat equations](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03626)
*Ilkhom Mukhammadiev,Diyora Salimova*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文证明了残差神经网络(ResNet)在数值逼近半线性热方程解时能够克服维度灾难，即在多项式和网络可逼近性假设下，存在参数数量至多为ηd^η ε^{-η}的ResNet，能以L²误差ε逼近任意维度d上的解。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 已有严格结果表明前馈神经网络在逼近高维PDE时可以克服维度灾难，但在非线性PDE场景下对ResNet的理论保证仍然有限。本文旨在填补这一空白，为ResNet在非线性PDE数值逼近中的理论能力提供严格的数学证明。

Method: 将多层皮卡德(Multilevel Picard)估计子的一个确定性实现表示为ResNet：捷径连接(shortcut connections)负责传递空间变量和标量累加器，而残差分支逐步累加估计子的各个求和项。对具有岭和(ridge-sum)初始条件、可容许sigmoidal激活函数以及非线性项的整体Lipschitz截断，推导出显式的参数数量上界。

Result: 证明存在η∈(0,∞)和ResNets Ψ_{d,ε}，其参数数量不超过ηd^η ε^{-η}，使得逼近误差（L²意义）不超过ε。在特定条件下（岭和初始条件、sigmoidal激活、Lipschitz截断非线性），对任意ξ>0获得显式上界C_ξ d^{4+ξ} ε^{-(3+ξ)}的参数数量。

Conclusion: ResNet在逼近半线性热方程（具全局Lipschitz连续、与梯度无关的非线性项）时能够克服维度灾难，从理论上证明了ResNet在高维非线性PDE数值逼近中的有效性。

Abstract: Rigorous results show that feedforward neural networks can overcome the curse of dimensionality in the numerical approximation of high-dimensional partial differential equations (PDEs), but comparatively little is known about residual neural networks (ResNets) in the nonlinear PDE setting. We prove that ResNets overcome the curse of dimensionality in the numerical approximation of solutions of semilinear heat equations with globally Lipschitz continuous, gradient-independent nonlinearities: under polynomial growth and network approximability hypotheses on the PDE data, there exist $η\in(0,\infty)$ and ResNets $Ψ_{d,\varepsilon}$, $d\in\mathbb{N}$, $\varepsilon\in(0,1]$, with at most $ηd^η\varepsilon^{-η}$ parameters whose realizations approximate the solution in dimension $d$ with an $L^2$-error of at most $\varepsilon$. The proof represents one deterministic realization of a multilevel Picard estimator by a ResNet whose shortcut connections transmit the spatial variable and a scalar accumulator, while the residual branches successively add the summands of the estimator. For ridge-sum initial conditions, admissible sigmoidal activations, and globally Lipschitz truncations of the nonlinearity, we obtain, for every $ξ>0$, the explicit bound $C_ξd^{4+ξ}\varepsilon^{-(3+ξ)}$ on the number of parameters.

</details>


### [161] [A fully globalized solver for discretized inverse elliptic coefficient problems with exact data](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03631)
*Bastian Harrach*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对椭圆型逆系数问题（如Calderón问题）的有限维非线性反演，提出了一种新的局部收敛算法，该算法具有显式残差准则以确保收敛到反问题解；同时提出一种全局化变体，保证在有限步全局搜索后自动切换到更快的局部收敛算法。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 椭圆型逆系数问题（如Calderón问题）以其强非线性和不适定性著称，数值求解高度依赖良好的初值。传统的局部收敛算法缺乏明确的收敛判据，且难以跳出局部极小；需要一种既有严格收敛保证又能自动加速全局搜索的算法。

Method: 提出一种具有显式残差判据的局部收敛算法，该判据可确保迭代收敛到反问题的真解；在此基础上构建全局化变体，保证在有限步全局搜索之后自动切换到上述更快的局部收敛算法。

Result: 新算法具备显式残差收敛准则以保证收敛性，全局化版本保证有限步全局搜索后自动切换至局部快速收敛阶段。

Conclusion: 该算法为有限维非线性椭圆逆系数问题提供了一种兼具快速收敛和全局收敛保证的新求解框架，克服了对初值的高度依赖问题。

Abstract: We consider finite-dimensional nonlinear inverse problems arising from finite element discretizations of elliptic inverse coefficient problems such as the Calderón problem with finitely many measurements and unknowns. Such inverse coefficient problems are notorious for their nonlinearity and ill-posedness, and numerical solvers tend to depend strongly on good initial values. In this work, we develop a new locally convergent algorithm with an explicit residual criterion that ensures convergence to the inverse problem solution, and a globalized variant that is guaranteed to automatically switch to the faster locally convergent algorithm after finitely many global search steps.

</details>


### [162] [Mini mixed finite element method for nearly incompressible linear elasticity problems](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03780)
*Zhijin Guan,Yue Feng,Hehu Xie,Chenguang Zhou*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对近不可压缩线性弹性边值问题及其特征值问题，提出基于Mini元的混合有限元方法以克服锁定现象，并设计了非嵌套增广子空间算法求解特征值问题，理论分析与数值实验均证明收敛率关于Lamé常数一致、与λ无关。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 标准低阶有限元在近不可压缩极限下会出现体积锁定现象，严重影响数值解的精度与可靠性；同时，相应的特征值问题在近不可压缩情形下也面临同样的数值困难，需要发展鲁棒且高效的求解方法。

Method: 采用Mini元（P1气泡速度+分片常数压力）构造混合变分格式以规避锁定；对边值问题证明混合变分形式适定并推导关于λ一致的先验误差估计；对特征值问题在混合有限元框架下设计非嵌套增广子空间迭代算法，并给出离散特征值逼近及迭代求解器的收敛性分析。

Result: 数值实验表明边值问题与特征值问题均达到最优收敛阶，且在λ趋于无穷时解具有鲁棒性；理论证明与数值结果相互印证，验证了Mini元及增广子空间算法在近不可压缩区域内的有效性和可靠性。

Conclusion: Mini元混合有限元格式和非嵌套增广子空间算法为近不可压缩线弹性问题提供了无锁定、高效且收敛率与λ无关的可靠数值计算方案。

Abstract: This paper addresses the numerical solution of nearly incompressible linear elasticity boundary value problems and their associated eigenvalue problems. A mixed finite element formulation based on Mini element is proposed to circumvent the locking phenomenon that plagues standard low-order elements in the nearly incompressible limit. For the boundary value problem, we establish the well-posedness of mixed variational formulation and derive a priori error estimates that are uniform with respect to the Lamé constant $\underlineλ$, thereby proving the method's locking-free property. For the eigenvalue problem, we develop an efficient non-nested augmented subspace algorithm designed within the mixed finite element framework. A comprehensive convergence analysis is provided for the discrete eigenvalue approximation and the proposed iterative solver, demonstrating that the convergence rates remain independent of $\underlineλ$. Numerical experiments on both problems confirm the theoretical conclusions, showing optimal convergence rates and robustness as $\underlineλ \to \infty$. The results validate the effectiveness of Mini element and proposed augmented subspace algorithm for reliable and efficient computation in the nearly incompressible regime.

</details>


### [163] [A stabilized scheme satisfying the discrete maximum principle for a time fractional convection-diffusion-reaction equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.03808)
*Christos Pervolianakis*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对二维有界区域上的时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程，构建了结合保形线性有限元法与代数通量修正法的稳定数值格式，证明了半离散格式的适定性和离散最大值原理，并用L1方法进行时间离散得到全离散格式，最后通过数值实验验证收敛阶并对含层解进行了测试。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程在物理和工程中广泛应用，但标准数值格式可能违反离散最大值原理，导致数值解出现非物理振荡，尤其当解含边界层或内部层时更为严重。因此需要构造既能保持离散最大值原理又具有良好收敛性的数值格式。

Method: 采用保形线性有限元方法进行空间离散，结合代数通量修正（AFCM）技术构造满足离散最大值原理的稳定数值格式；半离散格式为非线性，通过能量论证在非光滑初值假设下推导误差估计；时间方向采用L1方法进行离散，获得全离散格式并证明其适定性与离散最大值原理。

Result: 建立了半离散和全离散格式的适定性，证明了离散最大值原理成立；在非光滑初值条件下给出了半离散格式的误差估计；数值实验验证了理论收敛阶，并测试了所提格式对含有边界层或内部层解的鲁棒性。

Conclusion: 该工作为时间分数阶对流-扩散-反应方程提供了一套完整的、满足离散最大值原理的有限元数值框架，兼具理论保证与实际数值稳定性，对求解具有层状结构的分数阶问题具有重要价值。

Abstract: We study a time fractional convection-diffusion-reaction equation in a bounded domain $Ω\subset\mathbb{R}^2$. A stabilized numerical scheme satisfying a discrete maximum principle is constructed by combining the conforming linear finite element method with the algebraic flux correction method. The resulting semi-discrete scheme is nonlinear, and its well-posedness is established. Assuming nonsmooth initial data, we derive error estimates for the semi-discrete scheme using energy arguments. For the temporal discretization, we employ the L1 method, obtaining a fully discrete scheme for which we prove well-posedness and the discrete maximum principle. We also present numerical experiments that validate the order of convergence as well as we test our schemes to solutions that possess layers.

</details>


### [164] [Closure complexity of longest-edge bisection for triangular meshes](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04003)
*Yuwen Li,Zhiyuan Yang*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文针对三角网格上的最长边二分细分算法（配合最长边传播路径闭包），提出了一种基于终点优先规则来消解平局的方法，并证明了任意自适应网格序列满足累积闭包估计 #T_L - #T_0 ≲ Σ#M_ℓ。证明结合了有限相似类定理（保证直径以一致严格因子增长）与 Binev–Dahmen–DeVore 型计数论证。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 自适应网格细化（AMR）中，能否保证总网格增量与所有标记的累计数量线性相关（即最优计数值）是关键问题。经典的 Binev–Dahmen–DeVore 论证针对某些细分规则有效，但对最长边二分配合串行传播路径闭包的情形，需要新的几何与组合工具来建立这一最优性估计。

Method: (1) 提出终点优先规则解决最长边传播中的平局问题：当共享边是相邻三角形的最长边时，该对即被声明为终点并直接二分该边。 (2) 利用平面最长边二分的有限相似类定理，将可能出现的三角形直径转化为有限个 √2-几何格的并集，从而证明在每一步非终点步骤中直径都按一致严格因子增长，得到单次标记产生的所有三角形的"代际"与空间局部性。 (3) 将单标记的局部性结果通过 Binev–Dahmen–DeVore 计数论证转化为累积闭合估计。

Result: 对任意自适应网格序列 T_0,…,T_L 及标记子集序列 M_0,…,M_{L-1}，证明了 #T_L - #T_0 ≲ Σ_{\ell=0}^{L-1} #M_\ell，即累积闭合估计成立，表明该细分策略在累计网格复杂度上达到最优阶。

Conclusion: 该工作首次为带串行最长边传播路径闭包与终点优先平局消解规则的最长边二分法建立了最优累积复杂度估计。其结果在方法论上结合了几何相似类分类与组合计数论证，为三角网格自适应算法的最优性理论提供了新的证明框架。

Abstract: On triangular meshes, we analyze local mesh refinement based on longest-edge bisection equipped with the serial longest-edge propagation-path closure. Ties are resolved by terminal priority: if the incoming shared edge is a longest edge of the neighboring triangle, the pair is declared terminal and that edge is bisected. For every adaptive mesh sequence $\mathcal{T}_0, \mathcal{T}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{T}_L$ with marked subset sequence $\mathcal{M}_0, \mathcal{M}_1, \ldots, \mathcal{M}_{L-1}$, we prove the cumulative closure estimate $\#\mathcal{T}_L - \#\mathcal{T}_0 \lesssim \sum_{\ell=0}^{L-1}\#\mathcal{M}_\ell$. The proof has two ingredients. First, the finite-similarity-class theorem for planar longest-edge bisection turns the set of possible diameters into a finite union of $\sqrt{2}$-geometric lattices. Hence diameters grow by a uniform strict factor at every nonterminal step. This yields generation and spatial locality for all triangles created by a single mark. Second, a Binev--Dahmen--DeVore type charging argument converts this single-mark locality into the cumulative estimate.

</details>


### [165] [Conditioning of solutions to the Sylvester equation](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04050)
*Massimiliano Fasi,Behnam Hashemi*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文部分回答了Nick Higham提出的关于Sylvester和Lyapunov方程解的条件数（conditioning）的开放问题。作者首先构造反例表明，即使系数矩阵A、B、C以及Kronecker和均完美良态，Sylvester方程的解仍然可以任意病态；随后推导了条件数的一般先验上界，针对A和B可对角化的情形给出特定界，并提供了涉及矩阵指数和Zolotarev数的下界。对于Lyapunov方程，作者在两种设定下获得上界：(i) A对称正定且C对称负定；(ii) A严格耗散且C对称正定。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 该问题源于Sylvester和Lyapunov方程数值算法的向后稳定性分析。Nick Higham提出的开放问题涉及这些方程解的良态性判断——理解解的条件数对于评估数值算法的向后稳定性至关重要。此前尚不明确在什么条件下方程解是良态的，以及良态的系数矩阵是否能保证良态的解。回答这一问题有助于数值线性代数中相关算法的误差分析。

Method: 本文采用理论分析的方法。首先构造具体的反例（counterexample）证明Sylvester方程解的条件数可以在所有系数均良态的情况下任意大。然后利用Kronecker和谱理论、矩阵范数分析等方法推导解的条件数的通用先验上界；针对A和B可对角化的特殊情形推导更精细的界。借用矩阵指数和Zolotarev数构造条件数的下界。对Lyapunov方程，分别在其系数满足特定正定性/耗散性假设的两种设定下推导上界。

Result: (1) 构造了反例，证明确实存在A、B、C及Kronecker和均完美良态但Sylvester方程解任意病态的情形，部分回答了Higham的开放问题。(2) 给出了Sylvester方程解条件数的一般先验上界和针对可对角化情形的改进上界，以及基于矩阵指数和Zolotarev数的下界。(3) 对Lyapunov方程，在A对称正定且C对称负定、以及A严格耗散且C对称正定这两种设定下，分别导出了条件数的上界。

Conclusion: 研究揭示了Sylvester和Lyapunov方程解的良态性远比系数矩阵的良态性微妙：即使所有输入数据完美良态，解仍可能严重病态，这暗示了相关数值算法向后稳定性分析中的内在困难。同时，文中导出的先验上下界为实际应用中估计解的精度损失提供了定量工具，为该开放问题提供了部分但重要的解答。

Abstract: We partially answer an open problem, posed by Nick Higham, concerning the conditioning of solutions to Sylvester and Lyapunov equations. The question arises in the backward stability analysis of numerical algorithms for these equations. We first show that the solution to the Sylvester equation $AX-XB = C$ can be arbitrarily ill-conditioned even if $A, B, C$ and the Kronecker sum $I \otimes A - B^T \otimes I$ are all perfectly conditioned. We then derive general a priori bounds on the condition number of the solution, as well as bounds for the Sylvester equation when $A$ and $B$ are diagonalizable. We also provide lower bounds involving matrix exponentials and Zolotarev numbers. For the Lyapunov equation $AX+XA^T = -C$, we obtain upper bounds in two settings: (i) when $A$ is symmetric positive definite while $C$ is symmetric negative definite, and (ii) when $A$ is strictly dissipative and $C$ is symmetric positive definite.

</details>


### [166] [A Multigrid Method for CutFEM and its Convergence](https://arxiv.org/abs/2609.04067)
*Michal Wichrowski*

Main category: math.NA

TL;DR: 本文建立了针对切割有限元离散泊松问题的、采用顶点补丁光滑子的几何多重网格收敛理论，在离散延拓性质下证明了两层收敛界对网格尺寸和切割几何均匀成立，并给出了W循环界。


<details>
  <summary>Details</summary>
Motivation: 切割有限元方法在处理复杂几何（如界面问题和嵌入式域）时具有优势，但截止域与物理域的不匹配以及非继承的层形式给多重网格预条件子带来了理论分析的困难。已有的工作缺乏对这些场景下多重网格收敛性的严格理论保证，特别是顶点补丁光滑子在切割网格上的应用缺乏系统的收敛性分析。

Method: 论文发展了针对切割有限元离散化的几何多重网格收敛理论框架。具体包括：1) 处理非继承的level forms（粗网格形式不能简单继承自细网格）；2) 处理物理域与活跃域（active domain）之间的不匹配问题；3) 利用离散延拓性质（discrete extension property）证明两层收敛界；4) 在两层收敛率的小性假设下建立W循环界。数值实验中则测试了V循环（尽管对此没有收敛界声明）。

Result: 两层的收敛界对网格尺寸和切割几何均匀成立，W循环界在额外的两层收敛率小性假设下成立。数值结果表明：收敛常数随多项式次数p增大而退化；降低ghost penalty可改善迭代次数；对齐双单元模型中存在p^{-2}阶的半正定性阈值，而p次切割模式的可见尺度呈指数衰减。在模型阈值处进行的实验减少了迭代次数，但未能建立组装算子层面的阈值。

Conclusion: 论文为切割有限元背景下的几何多重网格（顶点补丁光滑子）提供了首个系统的收敛理论，但理论保证仅覆盖两层和W循环，V循环缺乏收敛证明。研究发现收敛常数随p退化，且半正定性阈值的模型分析与实际组装算子行为之间存在差距，说明实际离散系统的谱特性比简化模型更为复杂。

Abstract: We develop a convergence theory for geometric multigrid with vertex-patch smoothers applied to cut finite element discretizations of the Poisson problem. The framework addresses non-inherited level forms and the mismatch between the physical and active domains. Using the discrete extension property, we prove two-level convergence bounds uniform in the mesh size and the cut geometry, and W-cycle bounds under an additional smallness assumption on the two-level rate. The numerical experiments intentionally use the stronger V-cycle, for which no convergence bound is claimed here.
  The convergence constants degrade with the degree $p$. Lowering the ghost penalty improves iteration counts. An aligned two-cell model exhibits a semidefiniteness threshold of order $p^{-2}$, whereas the visibility scale of a degree-$p$ cut mode decreases exponentially. Experiments at the model threshold reduce the iteration counts, but do not establish an assembled-operator threshold.

</details>
